Again that sixth sense of impending disaster, which is perhaps bred into some men, alerted Ross. Why he turned suddenly and backed against a bushy willow, he could not have explained. However, because he did so the loop of hide rope meant for his throat hit his shoulder harmlessly. It fell to the ground, and he stamped one boot down on it. Then it was the work of seconds to grasp it and give it a quick jerk. The surprised man who held the other end was brought sprawling into the open.

Ross had seen that round face before. "Lal of the town of Nodren." He found words to greet the ropeman even as his knee came up against the fellow's jaw, jarring Lal so that he dropped a flint knife. Ross kicked it into the willows. "What do you hunt here, Lal?"

"Traders!" The voice was weak, but it held heat.

The tribesman did not try to struggle against Ross's hold, and Ross, gripping him by the nape of the neck, moved through a screen of brush to a hollow. Luckily there was no water cupped there, for McNeil lay in the bottom of that dip, his arms tied tightly behind him and his ankles lashed together with no thought for the pain of his burned leg.

===Chinese===
又是那种预感灾祸的第六感——也许是某些人骨子里天生就有的——警醒了罗斯。他为什么突然转身退靠在一棵枝叶茂密的柳树上，他自己也说不清。然而，正因如此，那条原本要套住他脖子的皮绳套无害地打在了他的肩膀上。绳圈落在地上，他一脚踩住，然后只用了几秒钟就抓住绳子猛力一拽。拽着另一端的那个人猝不及防，被拉得扑倒在空地上。

罗斯以前见过那张圆脸。"诺德伦镇的拉尔（Lal）。"他找到话来问候那个拿绳子的人，同时膝盖已经顶上了对方的下巴，震得拉尔松手掉了一把燧石刀。罗斯一脚把它踢进了柳树丛。"你在这里猎什么，拉尔？"

"商人！"声音虽然微弱，但透着一股火气。

部落人没有试图挣脱罗斯的控制。罗斯抓着他的后颈，穿过一道灌木屏风来到一处凹地。幸好那里没有积水，因为麦克尼尔就躺在那个洼地的底部，双臂被紧紧反绑在身后，脚踝也绑在了一起，丝毫不顾他那条烧伤的腿的疼痛。

## VII

===Original===
Ross whirled the rope which had been meant to bring him down around Lal. He lashed the tribesman's arms tight to his body before he knelt to cut loose his fellow time traveler. Lal now huddled against the far wall of the cup, fear in every line of his small body. So apparent was this fear that Ross felt no satisfaction at turning the tables on him. Instead he felt increasingly uneasy.

===Chinese===
罗斯把那根本是用来对付他的绳子甩向拉尔，将部落人的双臂紧紧绑在身前，然后才蹲下身去给自己的同伴松绑。拉尔此刻蜷缩在杯形谷地远处的墙角，矮小的身体每一寸都在发抖。这恐惧如此明显，罗斯丝毫没有扳回一局的快感，反而越来越不安。

===Original===
"What is this all about?" he asked McNeil as he stripped off his bonds and helped him up.
McNeil massaged his wrists, took a step or two, and grimaced with pain. "Our friend seeks to be an obedient servant of Lurgha."

===Chinese===
"这到底是怎么回事？"他一边给麦克尼尔（McNeil）解绑扶他起身，一边问道。
麦克尼尔揉着手腕，试着走了两步，疼得直皱眉头。"咱们这位朋友想给卢尔加（Lurgha）当一条忠犬。"

===Original===
Ross picked up his bow. "The tribe is out to hunt us?"
"Lurgha has ordered—out of thin air again—that any traders who escaped are to be brought in and introduced to him personally at the sacrifice for the enrichment of the fields!"

===Chinese===
罗斯拾起弓。"全族人都在追捕我们？"
"卢尔加又从凭空发号施令了——凡是逃掉的商人，都得抓回来，在献祭仪式上亲自呈给他，好让田地丰饶！"

===Original===
The old, old gift of blood and life at the spring sowing. Ross recalled grisly details from his cram lessons. Any wandering stranger or enemy tribesman taken in a raid before that day would meet such a fate. On unlucky years when people were not available a deer or wolf might serve. But the best sacrifice of all was a man. So Lurgha had decreed—from the air—that traders were his meat? What of Ashe? Let any hunter from the village track him down.

===Chinese===
春播时节，献上鲜血与生命的古老仪式。罗斯从突击课程中回忆起那些血腥的细节：在那日之前捕获的任何流浪异乡人或敌对部落成员，都会落得这样的下场。不走运的年份若凑不齐活人，一头鹿或一匹狼也能凑数。但最好的祭品终究是人。所以卢尔加从空中降下旨意，说商人是他的肉？阿什怎么办？让村里的猎手去找他吧。

===Original===
"We have to move fast," Ross told McNeil as he took up the rope which made a leading cord for Lal. Ashe would want to question the tribesman about this second order from Lurgha.
Impatient as Ross was, he had to mend his pace to accommodate McNeil. The man from the hill post was close to the end of his strength. He had started off bravely enough, but now he wavered. Ross sent Lal ahead with a sharp push, ordering him to stay there, while he went to McNeil's aid. It was well into the afternoon before they came up the stream and saw the fire before the cave.

===Chinese===
"我们得快点走，"罗斯对麦克尼尔说，一边拿起绳子当作牵拉尔的缰绳。阿什会想审问这个部落人，搞清楚卢尔加第二道命令的来龙去脉。
罗斯再心急，也不得不放慢脚步迁就麦克尼尔。这个从山上哨所来的人已经快要撑不住了。他起初走得很硬气，现在却开始晃悠。罗斯猛推了拉尔一把，命他到前面待着，然后折回去搀扶麦克尼尔。直到午后很晚，他们才沿溪流走回山洞，看见洞口前生着火。

===Original===
"Macna!" Ashe hailed Ross's companion with the native version of his name. "And Lal. But what do you here, Lal of Nodren's town?"
"Mischief." Ross helped McNeil within the cave and to the pile of brush which was his own bed. "He was hunting traders as a present for Lurgha."

===Chinese===
"麦克纳！"阿什（Ashe）用本地名字招呼罗斯的同伴。"还有拉尔（Lal）。可你在这儿做什么，诺德伦（Nodren）镇上的拉尔？"
"惹了祸。"罗斯扶麦克尼尔走进山洞，把他送到那堆枯枝旁——那是他自己的铺位。"他在猎捕商人，想献给卢尔加当礼物。"

===Original===
"So—" Ashe turned upon the tribesman—"and by whose word did you go hunting my kinsman, Lal? Was it Nodren's? Has he forgotten the blood bond between us? For it was in the name of Lurgha himself that that bond was made—"

===Chinese===
"那么——"阿什转向部落人——"是谁派你来猎杀我的亲族的，拉尔？诺德伦吗？他忘了我们之间的血誓吗？那血誓正是以卢尔加本人的名义立下的——"

===Original===
"Aaaah—" The tribesman squatted down against the wall where Ross had shoved him. Unable to hide his head in his arms, he brought his face down upon his knees so that only his shaggy topknot of hair was exposed. Ross realized, with stupefaction, that the little man was crying like a child, his hunched shoulders rising and falling with the force of his sobs. "Aaaah—" he wailed.

===Chinese===
"啊——"部落人蹲在罗斯推他去的墙根下，双臂遮不住头，就把脸埋在膝盖里，只露出一蓬乱糟糟的发髻。罗斯惊愕地发现，这个小个子男人竟像个孩子一样哭了起来，佝偻的肩膀随着抽泣剧烈起伏。"啊——"他哀号道。

===Original===
Ashe allowed him a moment or two of noisy grief and then limped over to grasp his topknot and pull up his head. Lal's eyes were screwed tightly shut, but there were tears on his cheeks, and his mouth twisted in another wail.

===Chinese===
阿什由着他哭了一阵，然后一瘸一拐地走过去，抓住他的发髻把头拽起来。拉尔的眼睛紧紧闭着，泪水挂满面颊，嘴巴扭曲着又要发出哀号。

===Original===
"Be quiet!" Ashe shook him, but not too harshly. "Have you yet felt the bite of my sharp knife? Has an arrow holed your skin? You are alive, and you could be dead. Show that you are glad you live and continue to breathe by telling us what you know, Lal."

===Chinese===
"安静！"阿什摇晃他，但没有太用力。"你尝过我利刃的滋味吗？箭头射穿过你的皮肉吗？你还活着，本来可以死的。告诉我你知道的一切，拉尔，以此证明你庆幸自己还活着，还能呼吸。"

===Original===
The woman Cassca had displayed a measure of intelligence and ease at their meeting upon the road. But it was very plain that Lal was of different stuff, a simple man in whose head few ideas could find house room at one time. And to him the present was all black. Little by little they dragged the story out of him.

===Chinese===
那个女人卡斯卡（Cassca）在路上初遇时表现出相当的智识与从容。但拉尔显然截然不同——他是个头脑简单的人，脑子里同一时间装不下几个念头。而此刻在他看来，一切都是漆黑一片。他们一点一点地从他嘴里掏出了事情的经过。

===Original===
Lal was poor, so poor that he had never dared dream of owning for himself some of the precious things the hill traders displayed to the wealthy of Nodren's town. But he was also a follower of the Great Mother's, rather than one who made sacrifices to Lurgha. Lurgha was the god for warriors and great men; he was too high to concern himself with such as Lal.

===Chinese===
拉尔很穷，穷到从不敢奢望拥有山上商人展示给诺德伦镇富人们的那些珍宝。但他又是大地之母（the Great Mother）的信徒，而非向卢尔加献祭的人。卢尔加是战士和大人物的神，高高在上，不会关心拉尔这号人。

===Original===
So when Nodren reported the end of the hill post under the storm fist of Lurgha, Lal had been impressed only to a point. He was still convinced it was none of his concern, and instead he began thinking of the treasures which might lie hidden in the destroyed buildings. It occurred to him that Lurgha's Wrath had been laid upon the men who had owned them, but perhaps it would not stretch to the fine things themselves. So he had gone secretly to the hill to explore.

===Chinese===
所以当诺德伦报告山上哨所被卢尔加的风暴之拳摧毁时，拉尔只被触动了一点点。他仍然确信这与自己无关，反而开始盘算那些毁坏建筑里可能埋藏的财宝。他想到卢尔加的怒火（Lurgha's Wrath）是降在拥有者身上的，也许不会殃及那些精美物件本身。于是他偷偷上了山去搜寻。

===Original===
What he had seen there had utterly converted him to a belief in the fury of Lurgha and he had been frightened out of his simple wits, fleeing without making the search he had intended. But Lurgha had seen him there, had read his impious thoughts……

===Chinese===
可他在那里看到的景象彻底征服了他，让他对卢尔加的震怒深信不疑，吓得魂飞魄散，连原本计划的搜寻都没做就逃走了。然而卢尔加已经在那里看见了他，看穿了他那亵渎的心思……

===Original===
At that point Ashe interrupted the stream of Lal's story. How had Lurgha seen Lal?
Because—Lal shuddered, began to cry again, and spoke the next few sentences haltingly—that very morning when he had gone out to hunt wild fowl in the marshes Lurgha had spoken to him, to Lal, who was less than a flea creeping upon a worn-out fur rug.

===Chinese===
这时阿什打断了拉尔源源不断的故事。卢尔加怎么会看见拉尔的？
因为——拉尔浑身一哆嗦，又开始哭泣，接下来的几句话说得断断续续——就在当天早上他去沼泽猎野鸟的时候，卢尔加对他开了口，对拉尔开了口，而拉尔不过是趴在破旧毛毯上的一只跳蚤。

===Original===
And how had Lurgha spoken? Ashe's voice was softer, gentle.
Out of the air, even as he had spoken to Nodren, who was a chief. He said that he had seen Lal in the hill post, and so Lal was his meat. But not yet would he eat him, not if Lal served him in other ways. And he, Lal, had lain flat on the ground before the bodiless voice of Lurgha and had sworn that he would serve Lurgha to the end of his life.

===Chinese===
卢尔加是怎么开口的？阿什的声音更轻柔了。
从空中，就像他对诺德伦——那个首领——说话一样。卢尔加说他在山上哨所看见了拉尔，所以拉尔是他的肉。但他暂时还不吃他，前提是拉尔能以其他方式效力。于是拉尔匍匐在卢尔加那无形的声音面前，发誓终生效忠于他。

===Original===
Then Lurgha had told him to hunt down one of the evil traders who was hiding in the marshes, and bind him with ropes. Then he was to call the men of the village and together they would carry the prisoner to the hill where Lurgha had loosed his wrath, and there they would leave him. Later they might return and take what they found there and use it to bless the fields at sowing time, and all would be well with Nodren's village. And Lal had sworn that he would do as Lurgha bade, but now he could not. So Lurgha would eat him up—he was a man without hope.

===Chinese===
然后卢尔加命令他去追捕躲在沼泽里的一个邪恶商人，用绳子捆起来。之后他要召集村里的男人们，一起把囚犯抬到卢尔加释放怒火的山上，留在那里。过后他们可以回去取走发现的东西，用来在播种时节祝福田地，诺德伦的村庄就会万事大吉。拉尔发誓一定照卢尔加的吩咐去做，可现在他做不到了。卢尔加会把他吃掉——他已经是个没有希望的人了。

===Original===
"Yet," Ashe said even more gently, "have you not served the Great Mother all these years, giving to her a portion of the first fruits even when the yield of your one field was small?"

===Chinese===
"可是，"阿什说得更加轻柔，"你这些年来难道不是一直侍奉大地之母吗？即便你那一小块田收成寥寥，你也总把第一茬果实的一部分献给她。"

===Original===
Lal stared at him, his woebegone face still smeared with tears. It took a second or two for the question to penetrate his fear-clouded mind. Then he nodded timidly.

===Chinese===
拉尔瞪着他，那张愁苦的脸还挂着泪痕。这问题花了一两秒才穿透他被恐惧蒙蔽的脑子。然后他怯怯地点了点头。

===Original===
"Has she not dealt with you well in return, Lal? You are a poor man, that is true. But you are not gaunt of belly, even though this is the thin season when men fast before the coming of the new harvest. The Great Mother watches over her own. And it is she who has brought you to us now. For this I say to you, Lal, and I, Assha of the traders, speak with a straight tongue. The Lurgha who struck our post, who spoke to you from the air, means you no good—"

===Chinese===
"她待你不好吗，拉尔？你是个穷人，这不假。可你并不骨瘦如柴，即便眼下正是青黄不接、人们挨饿等待新收成的苦日子。大地之母照看着自己的子民。正是她把你带到了我们面前。所以我要对你说，拉尔，我——商人阿莎（Assha）——说的是真心话。那个摧毁我们哨所、从空中对你说话的卢尔加，对你没安好心——"

===Original===
"Aaaah!" wailed Lal. "So do I know, Assha. He is of the blackness and the wandering spirits of the dark!"

===Chinese===
"啊——！"拉尔哀号道。"这我知道，阿莎。他属于黑暗，属于黑暗中游荡的幽灵！"

===Original===
"Just so. Thus he is no kin to the mother, for she is of the light and of good things, of the new grain, and the newborn lambs for your flocks, of the maids who wed with men and bring forth sons to lift their fathers' spears, daughters to spin by the hearth and sow the yellow grain in the furrows. Lurgha's quarrel lies with us, Lal, not with Nodren nor with you. And we take upon us that quarrel." He limped into the outer air where the shadows of evening were beginning to creep across the ground.

===Chinese===
"正是如此。所以他不是地母的亲属，因为地母属于光明和美好之物——新粮、羊群里新生的羔羊、嫁给男人并生下儿子举起父辈长矛的少女，还有在炉旁纺线、在沟垄里播撒金谷的女儿们。卢尔加的争端针对的是我们，拉尔，不是诺德伦，也不是你。我们把这份争端揽在自己身上。"他一瘸一拐地走到外面，暮色正开始在地面蔓延。

===Original===
"Hear me, Lurgha," he called into the coming night, "I am Assha of the traders, and upon myself I take your hate. Not upon Lal, nor upon Nodren, nor upon the people who live in Nodren's town, shall your wrath lie. Thus do I say it!"

===Chinese===
"听我说，卢尔加，"他朝着降临的夜色喊道，"我是商人阿莎，我把你的仇恨揽到自己身上。拉尔、诺德伦、诺德伦镇上的居民——你的怒火不许落在他们头上。我就是这样说的！"

===Original===
Ross, noticing that Ashe concealed from Lal a wave of his hand, was prepared for some display meant to impress the tribesman. It came in a spectacular burst of green fire beyond the stream. Lal wailed again, but when that fire was followed by no other manifestation he ventured to raise his head once more.

===Chinese===
罗斯注意到阿什背着拉尔做了个手势，猜到他要搞一番旨在震慑部落人的把戏。果然，溪流对面爆发出一团耀眼的绿色火焰。拉尔又哀号起来，但那火焰之后再无其他异象，他才壮着胆子再次抬起头。

===Original===
"You have seen how Lurgha answered me, Lal. Toward me only will his wrath be turned. Now—" Ashe limped back and dragged out the white wolf skin, dropping it before Lal—"this you will give to Cassca that she may make a curtain for the Mother's home. See, it is white and so rare that the Mother will be pleased with such a fine gift. And you will tell her all that has chanced and how you believe in her powers over the powers of Lurgha, and the Mother will be well pleased with you. But you shall say nothing to the men of the village, for this quarrel is between Lurgha and Assha now and not for the meddling of others."

===Chinese===
"你已经看到卢尔加怎么回应我了，拉尔。他的怒火只会转向我。现在——"阿什一瘸一拐地走回来，拽出那张白狼皮，丢在拉尔面前——"你把它交给卡斯卡，让她给地母的居所做一道帘子。看，它是白色的，这样稀罕，地母会喜欢这件好礼物的。你把发生的一切告诉她，告诉她你相信地母的力量胜过卢尔加的力量，地母会对你非常满意。但你不要对村里的人说半个字，因为这场争端现在只关乎卢尔加和阿莎，旁人不得插手。"

===Original===
He unfastened the rope which bound Lal's arms. Lal reached out a hand to the wolf skin, his eyes filled with wonderment. "This is a fine thing you give me, Assha, and the Mother will be pleased, for in many years she has not had such a curtain for her secret place. Also, I am but a little man; the quarrels of great ones are not for me. Since Lurgha has accepted your words this is none of my affair. Yet I will not go back to the village for a while—with your permission, Assha. For I am a man of loose and wagging tongue and oftentimes I speak what I do not really wish to say. So if I am asked questions, I answer. If I am not there to be asked such questions, I cannot answer."

===Chinese===
他解开了绑住拉尔手臂的绳子。拉尔伸手摸向狼皮，眼中满是惊奇。"你给了我一件好东西，阿莎，地母会高兴的，因为她已经好多年没有这样一道帘子来装点她的秘所了。而且我只是个小人物，大人物的争端轮不到我掺和。既然卢尔加接受了你的话，这事就与我无关了。不过我想暂时不回村子——请你允许，阿莎。因为我这个人管不住舌头，常常说些自己并不真想说的话。人家问我，我就答；我不在那里被问，就没法答。"

===Original===
McNeil laughed, and Ashe smiled. "Well enough, Lal. Perhaps you are a wiser man than you think. But also I do not believe you should stay here."

===Chinese===
麦克尼尔笑了，阿什也笑了。"很好，拉尔。也许你比自己想的更聪明。不过我也不认为你应该留在这里。"

===Original===
The tribesman was already nodding. "That do I say, too, Assha. You are now facing the Wrath of Lurgha, and with that I wish no part. Thus I shall go into the marsh for a while. There are birds and hares to hunt, and I shall work upon this fine skin so that when I take it to the Mother it shall indeed be a gift worth her smiles. Now, Assha, I would go before the night comes if it pleases you."

===Chinese===
部落人已经在点头了。"这也是我的想法，阿莎。你现在要面对卢尔加的怒火，我不想沾边。所以我去沼泽里躲一阵子。那里有鸟和野兔可猎，我还要好好处理这张好皮子，等把它献给地母时，它得是一件值得她展颜的好礼物。现在，阿莎，如果你不介意，我想在天黑之前就走。"

===Original===
"Go with good fortune, Lal." Ashe stood apart while the tribesman ducked his head in a shy, awkward farewell to the others, pattering out into the valley.

===Chinese===
"一路平安，拉尔。"阿什站在一旁，部落人羞涩而笨拙地朝其他人低了低头，啪嗒啪嗒地跑进了山谷。

===Original===
"What if they pick him up?" McNeil asked wearily.
"I don't think they can," Ashe returned. "And what would you do—keep him here? If we tried that, he'd scheme to escape and try to turn the tables on us. Now he'll keep away from Nodren's village and out of sight for the time being. Lal's not too bright in some ways, but he's a good hunter. If he has reason for hiding out, it'll take a better hunter to track him. At least we know now that the Reds are afraid they did not make a clean sweep here. What happened, McNeil?"

===Chinese===
"要是他们抓到他怎么办？"麦克尼尔疲惫地问。
"我觉得不会，"阿什答道。"而且你打算怎么办——留他在这儿？硬留的话他会想办法逃跑，还会反咬我们一口。眼下他会远离诺德伦的村子，暂时不露面。拉尔有些方面是不太灵光，但他是个好猎手。他既然有理由躲藏，就得有个更厉害的猎手才追得到他。至少我们现在知道赤色分子（the Reds）担心他们没有把这里清理干净。麦克尼尔，到底发生了什么？"

===Original===
While he was telling his story in more detail both Ashe and Ross worked on his burns, making him comfortable. Then Ashe sat back as Ross prepared food.
"How did they spot the post?" Ashe rubbed his chin and frowned at the fire.
"Only way I can guess is that they picked up our post signal and pinpointed the source. That means they must have been hunting us for some time."
"No strangers about lately?"

===Chinese===
麦克尼尔讲述更详细的经过时，阿什和罗斯一边处理他的烧伤，帮他舒服些。然后阿什靠回去，罗斯去准备食物。
"他们是怎么发现哨所的？"阿什搓着下巴，对着火堆皱眉。
"我能想到的唯一可能是他们截获了我们的哨所信号，定位了来源。这意味着他们追踪我们已经有一阵子了。"
"最近有陌生人来过吗？"

===Original===
McNeil shook his head. "Our cover wasn't broken that way. Sanford was a wonder. If I hadn't known better, I would have sworn he was born one of the Beaker folk. He had a network of informants running all the way from here into Brittany. Amazing how he was able to work without arousing any suspicions. I suppose his being a member of the smiths' guild was a big help. He could pick up a lot of news from any village where there was one at work. And I tell you," McNeil propped himself up on his elbow to exclaim more vehemently—"there wasn't a whisper of trouble from here clear across the channel and pretty far to the north. We were already sure the south was clean before we ever took cover as Beakers, especially since their clans are thick in Spain."

===Chinese===
麦克尼尔摇摇头。"我们不是那样暴露的。桑福德（Sanford）简直是个奇才。要不是我早就了解情况，我真会发誓他生来就是比克尔人（Beaker folk）。他的线人网络从这里一直延伸到布列塔尼（Brittany）。他竟然能行事这么久而不引起丝毫怀疑，实在令人惊叹。我想他身为铁匠行会的成员帮了大忙。任何一个有铁匠铺的村庄，他都能打听到大量消息。我跟你们说，"麦克尼尔用胳膊肘撑起身来，说得更加激动——"从这里一直到海峡对岸，甚至北边很远的地方，一点麻烦的迹象都没有。我们装扮成比克尔人之前就已经确定南方很安全了，何况他们的氏族在西班牙（Spain）遍地都是。"

===Original===
Ashe chewed a broiled wing reflectively. "Their permanent base with the transport has to be somewhere within the bounds of the territory they hold in our own time."

===Chinese===
阿什若有所思地嚼着一只烤翅膀。"他们的永久基地和运输工具，必定设在他们自己时代所控制的领土范围之内。"

===Original===
"They could plant it in Siberia and laugh at us," McNeil exploded. "No hope of our getting in there—"

===Chinese===
"他们大可以把基地设在西伯利亚（Siberia），然后嘲笑我们根本进不去——"麦克尼尔愤愤地说。

===Original===
"No." Ashe threw the stripped bone into the fire and licked grease from his fingers. "Then they would be faced with the old problem of distance. If what they are exploiting lay within their modern boundaries, we would never have tumbled to the thing in the first place. What the Reds want must lie outside their twentieth century holdings, a slender point in our favor. Therefore they will plant their shift point as close to it as they can. Our transportation problem is more difficult than theirs will ever be.

===Chinese===
"不会。"阿什把啃干净的骨头扔进火里，舔掉手指上的油脂。"那样他们就逃不掉距离的老问题。如果他们利用的东西在他们现代疆域之内，我们一开始就不会发现它。赤色分子想要的东西一定在他们二十世纪的领土之外，这是对我们有利的一点。所以他们会把转移点设得尽可能靠近目标。我们的运输难题比他们的要棘手得多。

===Original===
"You know why we chose the arctic for our base; it lies in a section of the world never populated by other than roving hunters. But I'll wager anything you want to name that their point is somewhere in Europe where they have people to contend with. If they are using a plane, they can't risk its being seen—"

===Chinese===
"你知道我们为什么选北极做基地；那里除了游猎民族，从未有人定居过。但我敢打赌，他们的转移点在欧洲某处，那里有当地人可以差遣。如果他们用的是飞机，他们绝不敢冒险让人看见——"

===Original===
"I don't see why not," Ross broke in. "These people couldn't possibly know what it was—Lurgha's bird—magic—"

===Chinese===
"为什么不敢？"罗斯插嘴道。"这些原始人根本不可能知道那是什么——卢尔加的神鸟——魔法——"

===Original===
Ashe shook his head. "They must have the interference-with-history worry as much as we have. Anything of our own time has to be hidden or disguised in such a way that the native who may stumble upon it will never know it is man-made. Our sub is a whale to all appearances. Possibly their plane is a bird, but neither can bear too close an examination. We don't know what could result from a leak of real knowledge in this or any primitive time……how it might change history……"

===Chinese===
阿什摇摇头。"他们一定跟我们一样担心干扰历史。我们时代的一切都必须隐藏或伪装，让偶然发现的当地人永远看不出是人造的。我们的潜艇（sub）外表像鲸鱼。他们的飞机大概伪装成鸟，但两者都经不起仔细检查。我们不知道在任何原始时代泄露真实知识会导致什么后果……历史可能会被怎样改写……"

===Original===
"But," Ross advanced what he believed to be the best argument against that reasoning, "suppose I handed Lal a gun and taught him to use it. He couldn't duplicate the weapon—the technology required lies so far beyond this age. These people couldn't reproduce such a thing."

===Chinese===
"但是，"罗斯提出了他认为最有力的反驳，"假如我给拉尔一把枪，教他怎么用。他不可能复制这种武器——制造它需要的技术远远超前于这个时代。这些原始人造不出这样的东西。"

===Original===
"True enough. On the other hand, don't belittle the ingenuity of the smiths or the native intelligence of men in any era. These tribesmen might not be able to reproduce your gun, but it would set them thinking along new lines. We might find that they would think our time right out of being. No, we dare not play tricks with the past. This is the same situation we faced immediately after the discovery of the atom bomb. Everybody raced to produce that new weapon and then sat around and shivered for fear we'd be crazy enough to use it on each other.

===Chinese===
"确实如此。但另一方面，别小看了铁匠的巧思，也别小看任何时代人类与生俱来的智慧。这些部落人也许造不出你的枪，但它会让他们沿着全新的思路去想。我们可能会发现，他们一想就把我们这个时代想没了。不行，我们不敢在历史上动手脚。这跟原子弹刚发明后我们面临的情况一模一样。各国争先恐后地造出那种新武器，然后坐下来发抖，生怕有人疯到往彼此头上扔。

===Original===
"The Reds have made new discoveries which we have to match, or we will go under. But back in time we have to be careful, both of us, or perhaps destroy the world we do live in."

===Chinese===
"赤色分子有了新的发现，我们必须跟上，否则就会被淘汰。可回到过去，我们双方都必须小心，否则说不定会毁掉我们自己生活的世界。"

===Original===
"What do we do now?" McNeil wanted to know.
"Murdock and I came here only for a trial run. It's his test. The sub is to call for us about nine days from now."
"So if we sit tight—if we can sit tight—" McNeil lay down again—"they will take us out. Meanwhile we have nine days."

===Chinese===
"我们现在怎么办？"麦克尼尔问。
"默多克（Murdock）和我来这里只是试运行，是他的测试。潜艇大约九天后来接我们。"
"所以我们只要按兵不动——如果我们能按兵不动的话——"麦克尼尔又躺了下去——"他们就会把我们接走。同时我们有九天时间。"

===Original===
They spent three more days in the cave. McNeil was on his feet and impatient to leave before Ashe was able to hobble well enough to travel. Though Ross and McNeil took turns at hunting and guard duty, they saw no signs that the tribesmen were tracking them. Apparently Lal had done as he promised, withdrawing to the marsh and hiding there apart from his people.

===Chinese===
他们在山洞里又待了三天。麦克尼尔已经能站起来，急着要走，而阿什还没能跛着行走足够远的距离上路。罗斯和麦克尼尔轮流狩猎和站岗，但没发现部落人追踪的迹象。显然拉尔履行了承诺，退到沼泽里，远离族人藏了起来。

===Original===
In the gray of pre-dawn on the fourth day Ashe wakened Ross. Their fire had been buried with earth, and already the cave seemed bleak. They ate venison roasted the night before and went out into the chill of a fog. A little way down the valley McNeil joined them out of the mist from his guard post. Keeping their pace to one which favored Ashe's healing wound, they made their way inland in the direction of the track linking the villages.

===Chinese===
第四天天刚蒙蒙亮，阿什就唤醒了罗斯。火堆已经用土掩埋，山洞显得格外凄冷。他们吃了前一晚烤好的鹿肉，走入冰冷的雾气中。沿山谷往下走了一小段，麦克尼尔从雾中他的岗哨位置赶上来会合。他们放慢脚步，照顾阿什正在愈合的伤口，沿着连接各村落的小路向内陆走去。

===Original===
Crossing that road they continued northward, the land beginning to rise under them. Far away they heard the blatting of sheep, the bark of a dog. In the fog, Ross stumbled in a shallow ditch beyond which lay a stubbled field. Ashe paused to look about him, his nostrils expanding as if he were a hound smelling out their trail.

===Chinese===
穿过那条路后继续向北，脚下的地势开始升高。远处传来羊的叫声、狗的吠声。雾中罗斯在一条浅沟里绊了一下，沟外是一片茬地。阿什停下脚步环顾四周，鼻孔翕动，像猎犬在嗅他们的踪迹。

===Original===
The three went on, crossing a whole series of small, irregular fields. Ross was sure that the yield from any of these cleared strips must be scanty. The fog was thickening. Ashe pressed the pace, using his handmade crutch carefully. He gave an audible sigh of relief when they were faced at last by two stone monoliths rising like pillars. A third stone lay across them, forming a rude arch through which they saw a narrow valley running back into the hills.

===Chinese===
三人继续前行，穿过一整片大小不一的不规则田地。罗斯确信这些开垦地的收成一定很微薄。雾越来越浓了。阿什加快步伐，小心地拄着自制的拐杖。当他们最终面对两块像柱子般耸立的巨石时，他明显松了口气。第三块石头横搁在两柱之上，形成一道粗陋的拱门，透过它可以看到一条狭窄的山谷向山里延伸。

===Original===
Through the fog Ross could sense the eerie strangeness of the valley beyond the massive gate. He would have said that he was not superstitious, that he had merely studied these tribal beliefs as lessons; he had not accepted them. Yet now, if he had been alone, he would have avoided that place and turned aside from the valley, for that which waited within was not for him. To his secret relief Ashe paused by the arch to wait.

===Chinese===
透过雾气，罗斯能感觉到巨石门后山谷的诡异与陌生。他本会说自己并不迷信，只是把这些部落信仰当作功课来研读，并未真正接受。可此刻若他独自一人，他会避开那个地方，绕过山谷，因为那里面等待的东西不属于他。令他暗自庆幸的是，阿什在拱门旁停下来等候。

===Original===
The older man gestured the other two into cover. Ross obeyed willingly, though the dank drops of condensing fog dripped on his cloak and wet his face as he brushed against prickly-leafed shrubs. Here were walls of evergreen plants and dwarfed pines almost as if this tunnel of year-round greenery had been planted with some purpose in mind. Once his companions had concealed themselves, Ashe called, shrill but sweetly, with a bird's rising notes. Three times he made that sound before a figure moved in the fog, the rough gray-white of its long cloak melting in the wisps of mist.

===Chinese===
年长的手势示意另外两人躲藏。罗斯顺从地照做了，尽管凝结的雾水滴落在斗篷上，带刺的灌木刮过他的脸庞。这里有常绿灌木和矮松形成的屏障，仿佛这条四季常青的通道是出于某种目的而栽种的。同伴们藏好之后，阿什发出一声尖锐而悦耳的鸟鸣般的呼唤，音调上扬。他连唤三次，雾中才出现一个身影，长长的斗篷灰白粗粝，融入丝丝雾气之中。

===Original===
Down that green tunnel, out of the heart of the valley, the other came, a loop of cloak concealing the entire figure. It halted right in back of the arch and Ashe, making a gesture to the others to stay where they were, faced the muffled stranger.

===Chinese===
那人沿着绿色通道从山谷深处走来，斗篷裹得严严实实，遮住了整个身形。它停在拱门正后方，阿什做了个手势让其他人留在原地，然后面对这个裹得严实的陌生人。

===Original===
"Hands and feet of the Mother, she who sows what may be reaped—"
"Outland stranger who is under the Wrath of Lurgha," the other mocked him in the voice of Cassca. "What do you want, outlander, that you dare to come here where no man may enter?"

===Chinese===
"地母的手与脚，播种可收获之物的人——"
"蒙卢尔加怒火的异乡人，"对方用卡斯卡的声调嘲讽道。"你想要什么，异乡人，竟敢闯入此等无人可进之地？"

===Original===
"That which you know. For on the night when Lurgha came you also saw—"
Ross heard the hiss of a sharply drawn breath. "How knew you that, outlander?"
"Because you serve the Mother and you are jealous for her and her service. If Lurgha is a mighty god, you wanted to see his acts with your own eyes."

===Chinese===
"你知道的那件事。因为卢尔加降临的那夜，你也看见了——"
罗斯听到一声急促的倒吸气。"你怎么知道的，异乡人？"
"因为你侍奉地母，你为她和她的福祉而忧心。如果卢尔加是强大的神，你会想亲眼见证他的作为。"

===Original===
When she finally answered, there was anger as well as frustration in her voice. "And you know of my shame then, Assha. For Lurgha came—on a bird he came, and he did even as he said he would. So now the village will make offerings to Lurgha and beg his favor, and the Mother will no more have those to harken to her words and offer her the first fruits—"

===Chinese===
当她终于回答时，声音里既有愤怒也有挫败。"那你知道我的耻辱了，阿莎。卢尔加来了——他乘着一只鸟来的，而且确实如他所说做了。如今村里人要向卢尔加献祭、乞求他的恩惠，地母将不再有人聆听她的话语、向她献上第一茬果实——"

===Original===
"But from whence came this bird which was Lurgha, can you tell me that, she who waits upon the Mother?"
"What difference does it make from what direction Lurgha came? That does not add nor take from his power." Cassca moved beneath the arch. "Or does it in some strange way, Assha?"
"Perhaps it does. Only tell me."

===Chinese===
"可那只化身为卢尔加的鸟从哪个方向来，你能告诉我吗，侍奉地母的人？"
"卢尔加从哪个方向来有什么区别？这不会增减他的力量。"卡斯卡移步到拱门下。"或者这在某种奇怪的意义上会有影响，阿莎？"
"也许会。只管告诉我。"

===Original===
She turned slowly and pointed over her right shoulder. "From that way he came, Assha. Well did I watch, knowing that I was the Mother's and that even Lurgha's thunderbolts could not eat me up. Does knowing that make Lurgha smaller in your eyes, Assha? When he has eaten up all that is yours and your kin with it?"

===Chinese===
她慢慢转过身，右肩一扬指了个方向。"他从那个方向来，阿莎。我看得清清楚楚，因为我知道自己是地母的人，连卢尔加的雷电也吃不掉我。知道这个会让卢尔加在你眼里变小吗，阿莎？即便他已经吞掉了你的一切，连你的亲人也一并吞掉？"

===Original===
"Perhaps," Assha repeated. "I do not think Lurgha will come so again."
She shrugged, and the heavy cloak flapped. "That shall be as it shall be, Assha. Now go, for it is not good that any man come hither."
Cassca paced back into the heart of the green tunnel, and Ross and McNeil came out of concealment. McNeil faced in the direction she had pointed. "Northeast—" he commented thoughtfully, "the Baltic lies in that quarter."

===Chinese===
"也许吧，"阿莎重复道。"我不认为卢尔加会再这样来了。"
她耸了耸肩，厚重的斗篷随之翻动。"该怎样便怎样，阿莎。现在走吧，任何人来此都不好。"
卡斯卡走回绿色隧道的深处，罗斯和麦克尼尔从隐蔽处走出来。麦克尼尔面向她所指的方向。"东北方——"他若有所思地说，"波罗的海（Baltic）就在那个方向。"

## VIII

===Original===
"… and that is about all." Ten days later Ashe, a dressing on his leg and a few of the pain lines smoothed from his face, sat on a bunk in the arctic time post nursing a mug of coffee in his hands and smiling, a little crookedly, at Nelson Millaird.

===Chinese===
"……大概就是这些了。"十天后，阿什（Gordon Ashe）——腿上缠着绷带，脸上的痛苦纹路消退了几分——坐在北极时间哨站的铺位上，双手捧着一杯咖啡，朝纳尔逊·米莱尔德（Nelson Millaird）微微歪嘴笑了笑。

===Original===
Millaird, Kelgarries, Dr. Webb, all the top brass of the project had not only come through the transfer point to meet the three from Britain but were now crammed into the room, nearly pushing Ross and McNeil through the wall. Because this was it! What they had hunted for months—years—now lay almost within their grasp.

===Chinese===
米莱尔德（Nelson Millaird）、凯尔加里斯少校（Major Kelgarries）、韦布医生（Dr. Webb）——项目的所有高层人物不仅穿过传送点来迎接从不列颠归来的三人，此刻更是挤在这间屋子里，几乎要把罗斯（Ross Murdock）和麦克尼尔（McNeil）挤到墙上去。因为关键时刻到了！他们追寻数月乃至数年的东西，此刻几乎触手可及。

===Original===
Only Millaird, the director, did not seem so confident. A big man with a bushy thatch of coarse graying hair and a heavy, fleshy face, he did not look like a brain. Yet Ross had been on the roster long enough to know that it was Millaird's thick and hairy hands that gathered together all the loose threads of Operation Retrograde and deftly wove them into a workable pattern. Now the director leaned back in a chair which was too small for his bulk, chewing thoughtfully on a toothpick.

===Chinese===
只有主管米莱尔德看起来并不那么自信。他身材魁梧，一头粗硬花白的浓发蓬乱如草，脸盘宽厚肉感，怎么看都不像个智囊。然而罗斯在项目名册上待得够久了，深知正是米莱尔德那双粗毛大手，将逆行行动（Operation Retrograde）的千头万绪拢在一起，灵巧地编织成一个可行的方案。此刻，这位主管靠在一把对他来说太小的椅子上，若有所思地叼着一根牙签。

===Original===
"So we have the first whiff of a trail," he commented without elation.

===Chinese===
"这么说，我们嗅到了第一条线索，"他评论道，语气里并无多少振奋。

===Original===
"A pretty strong lead!" Kelgarries broke in. Too excited to sit still, the major stood with his back against the door, as alert as if he were about to turn and face the enemy. "The Reds wouldn't have moved against Gog if they did not consider it a menace to them. Their big base must be in this time sector!"

===Chinese===
"一条相当强的线索！"凯尔加里斯少校（Major Kelgarries）插话道。他兴奋得坐不住，背靠门板站着，整个人警觉得像是随时要转身面对敌人。"赤色分子（the Reds）要是不觉得哥格哨站（Outpost Gog）对他们构成威胁，就不会对其动手。他们的大本营一定就在这个时间层！"

===Original===
"A big base," Millaird corrected. "The one we are after, no. And right now they may be switching times. Do you think they will sit here and wait for us to show up in force?" But Millaird's tone, intended to deflate, had no effect on the major.

===Chinese===
"一个大本营，"米莱尔德纠正道。"但我们追查的那个——不是。此刻他们说不定正在转换时间层。你们以为他们会坐在这里等着我们大军压境？"但米莱尔德这番泄气的话对少校毫无作用。

===Original===
"And just how long would it take them to dismantle a big base?" that officer countered. "At least a month. If we shoot a team in there in a hurry—"

===Chinese===
"拆除一个大本营需要多久？"那位军官反问道。"至少一个月。如果我们赶快派一支队伍进去——"

===Original===
Millaird folded his huge hands over his barrel-shaped body and laughed, without a trace of humor. "Just where do we send that team, Kelgarries? Northeast of a coastal point in Britain is a rather vague direction, to say the least. Not," he spoke to Ashe now, "that you didn't do all you could, Ashe. And you, McNeil, nothing to add?"

===Chinese===
米莱尔德将那双大手交叉放在圆桶般的身躯上，干笑了两声，毫无幽默之意。"我们把队伍派往哪里，凯尔加里斯？不列颠某个海岸点的东北方——这方向未免太含糊了吧。我不是说，"他转向阿什说道，"你没尽力，阿什。麦克尼尔，你有什么要补充的吗？"

===Original===
"No, sir. They jumped us out of the blue when Sandy thought he had every possible line tapped, every safeguard working. I don't know how they caught on to us, unless they located our beam to this post. If so, they must have been deliberately hunting us for some time, because we only used the beam as scheduled—"

===Chinese===
"没有，长官。桑迪（Sandy）以为所有线路都已监控、所有安全措施都在运转，他们却突然发动袭击。我不知道他们是怎么发现我们的，除非他们截获了我们发往本站的波束信号。如果真是这样，他们肯定蓄意追踪我们有一阵子了，因为我们只按规定时间使用波束——"

===Original===
"The Reds have patience and brains and probably some more of their surprise gadgets to help them. We have the patience and the brains, but not the gadgets. And time is against us. Get anything out of this, Webb?" Millaird asked the hitherto silent third member of his ruling committee.

===Chinese===
"赤色分子有耐心、有头脑，可能还有更多他们那些出其不意的装置相助。我们有耐心，有头脑，但没有那些装置。而且时间对我们不利。韦布，你从中看出什么没有？"米莱尔德转向他领导委员会中一直沉默的第三位成员。

===Original===
The quiet man adjusted his glasses on the bridge of his nose, a flattish nose which did not support them very well. "Just another point to add to our surmises. I would say that they are located somewhere near the Baltic Sea. There are old trade routes there, and in our own time it is a territory closed to us. We never did know too much about that section of Europe. Their installation may be close to the Finnish border. They could disguise their modern station under half a dozen covers; that is strange country."

===Chinese===
这位安静的人推了推鼻梁上的眼镜——他鼻梁扁平，眼镜架不太稳。"只能说再给我们的推测添一个佐证。我认为他们的位置在波罗的海（Baltic）附近某处。那里有古老的贸易路线，在我们自己的时代属于禁区。我们对欧洲那一片从来知之甚少。他们的设施可能靠近芬兰边境。他们可以用好几种伪装来掩饰现代哨站——那是一片陌生的地带。"

===Original===
Millaird's hands unfolded and he produced a notebook and pen from a shirt pocket. "Won't hurt to stir up some of the present-day agents of the M.I. and the rest. They might just come up with a useful hint. So you'd say the Baltic. But that is a big slice of country."

===Chinese===
米莱尔德展开双手，从衬衫口袋里掏出笔记本和笔。"不妨动用一些军情部门和别处的当代特工。他们说不定能提供有用的线索。那么你认为是波罗的海。但那片地域可不小。"

===Original===
Webb nodded. "We have one advantage—the old trade routes. In the Beaker period they are pretty well marked. The major one into that section was established for the amber trade. The country is forested, but not so heavily as it was in an earlier period. The native tribes are mostly roving hunters, and fishermen along the coast. But they have had contact with traders." He shoved his glasses back into place with a nervous gesture. "The Reds may run into trouble themselves there at this time—"

===Chinese===
韦布（Webb）点了点头。"我们有一个优势——古老的贸易路线。在比克尔时期（Beaker period），这些路线标识相当清晰。通往那一带的主路线是为琥珀贸易（amber trade）而开辟的。那地方有森林覆盖，但不像更早时期那么密。当地部落大多是流浪的猎人，海岸边还有渔民。不过他们跟商人有过接触。"他紧张地把眼镜推回原位。"赤色分子在那个时代那里可能也会遇上麻烦——"

===Original===
"How?" Kelgarries demanded.

===Chinese===
"什么麻烦？"凯尔加里斯追问。

===Original===
"Invasion of the ax people. If they have not yet arrived, they are due very soon. They formed one of the big waves of migratory people, who flooded the country, settled there. Eventually they became the Norse or Celtic stock. We don't know whether they stamped out the native tribes they found there or assimilated them."

===Chinese===
"战斧人（ax people）的入侵。如果他们还没到，也快了。他们是迁徙大潮中的一支，涌入境内并定居下来。后来他们演化成了挪威人（Norse）或凯尔特人（Celtic）。我们不知道他们是消灭了当地原住民还是将其同化。"

===Original===
"That might be a nice point to have settled more definitely," McNeil commented. "It could mean the difference between getting your skull split and continuing to breathe."

===Chinese===
"这个问题要是能弄清楚就好了，"麦克尼尔评论道。"搞不好就是脑袋开花和继续喘气的区别。"

===Original===
"I don't think they would tangle with the traders. Evidence found today suggests that the Beaker folk simply went on about their business in spite of a change in customers," Webb returned.

===Chinese===
"我认为他们不会跟商人起冲突。今天的证据表明，比克尔人（Beaker folk）只是照常做他们的生意，顾客换了也不在意，"韦布答道。

===Original===
"Unless they were pushed into violence." Ashe handed his empty mug to Ross. "Don't forget Lurgha's Wrath. From now on our enemies might take a very dim view of any Beaker trade posts near their property."

===Chinese===
"除非他们被逼到动武。"阿什把空杯子递给罗斯。"别忘了卢尔加之怒（Lurgha's Wrath）。从现在起，我们的敌人可能会对任何靠近他们地盘的比克尔贸易据点极为不满。"

===Original===
Webb shook his head slowly. "A wholesale attack on Beaker establishments would constitute a shift in history. The Reds won't dare that, not just on general suspicion. Remember, they are not any more eager to tinker with history than we are. No, they will watch for us. We will have to stop communication by radio—"

===Chinese===
韦布缓缓摇头。"对比克尔人据点发动全面攻击会改变历史进程。赤色分子不敢那样做，不会仅凭一般的怀疑就动手。记住，他们跟我们一样不愿篡改历史。不，他们会监视我们。我们必须停止无线电通讯——"

===Original===
"We can't!" snapped Millaird vehemently. "We can cut it down, but I won't send the boys out without some means of quick communication. You lab boys put your brains to work and see what you can turn out in the way of talk boxes that they can't snoop. Time!" He drummed on his knee with his thick fingers. "It all comes back to a question of time."

===Chinese===
"做不到！"米莱尔德激烈地反驳。"可以减少通讯量，但我不能让小伙子们出去后没有任何快速联络手段。你们实验室的人动动脑筋，看看能不能造出他们窃听不了的通话器。时间！"他用粗厚的手指敲着膝盖。"一切又回到了时间问题上。"

===Original===
"Which we do not have," Ashe observed in his usual quiet voice. "If the Reds are afraid they have been spotted, they must be dismantling their post right now, working around the clock. We'll never again have such a good chance to nail them. We must move now."

===Chinese===
"而我们恰恰没有时间，"阿什用他一贯平静的语气说道。"如果赤色分子担心暴露了，他们现在一定在没日没夜地拆卸据点。我们再也不会有这么好的机会抓住他们了。必须立刻行动。"

===Original===
Millaird's lids drooped almost shut; he might have been napping. Kelgarries stirred restlessly by the door, and Webb's round face had settled into what looked like permanent lines of disapproval.

===Chinese===
米莱尔德的眼皮几乎合上了；他看上去像是在打盹。凯尔加里斯在门边焦躁地动来动去，韦布的圆脸上已经定格出一副看来永远不赞成的神情。

===Original===
"Doc," Millaird spoke over his shoulder to the fourth man of his following, "what is your report?"

===Chinese===
"医生，"米莱尔德扭头对随行的第四个人说，"你的报告呢？"

===Original===
"Ashe must be under treatment for at least five days. McNeil's burns aren't too bad, and Murdock's slash is almost healed."

===Chinese===
"阿什至少还要治疗五天。麦克尼尔的烧伤不太严重，默多克（Murdock）的刀伤也快愈合了。"

===Original===
"Five days—" Millaird droned, and then flashed a glance at the major. "Personnel. We're tied down without any useful personnel. Who in processing could be switched without tangling them up entirely?"

===Chinese===
"五天——"米莱尔德拉长了声调，随即瞥了少校一眼。"人手。没有可用的人手，我们被困住了。培训里有哪些人可以抽调，不会搞得一团糟？"

===Original===
"No one. I can recall Jansen and Van Wyke. These ax people might be a good cover for them." The momentary light in Kelgarries' eyes faded. "No, we have no proper briefing and can't get it until the tribe does appear on the map. I won't send any men in cold. Their blunders would not only endanger them but might menace the whole project."

===Chinese===
"没有。我可以召回詹森（Jansen）和范怀克（Van Wyke）。战斧人对他们来说或许是个好伪装。"凯尔加里斯眼中短暂闪过的光芒熄灭了。"不行，我们没有充分的情报简报，而且在那个部落真正出现在地图上之前也无法获取。我不会毫无准备地派人过去。他们的失误不仅会危及自身，还可能威胁整个项目。"

===Original===
"So that leaves us with you three," Millaird said. "We'll recall what men we can and brief them again as fast as possible. But you know how long that will take. In the meantime—"

===Chinese===
"那就只剩你们三个了，"米莱尔德说。"我们会尽可能召回能用的人，尽快给他们重新做简报。但你们知道那要花多长时间。在此期间——"

===Original===
Ashe spoke directly to Webb. "You can't pinpoint the region closer than just the Baltic?"

===Chinese===
阿什直接对韦布说："你就不能把区域锁定得更精确些，而不只是波罗的海？"

===Original===
"We can do this much," the other answered him slowly, and with obvious reluctance. "We can send the sub cruising offshore there for the next five days. If there is any radio activity—any communication—we should be able to trace the beams. It all depends upon whether the Reds have any parties operating from their post. Flimsy—"

===Chinese===
"我们能做到这一点，"对方缓缓答道，明显不情愿。"我们可以派潜艇（the sub）在那片海域巡航五天。如果有任何无线电活动——任何通讯——我们应该能追踪到波束。就看赤色分子有没有队伍从他们的据点出发行动了。线索虽然脆弱——"

===Original===
"But something!" Kelgarries seized upon it with the relief of one who needed action.

===Chinese===
"但总比什么都没有强！"凯尔加里斯抓住了这点，就像一个急需行动的人终于找到了出口。

===Original===
"And they will be waiting for just such a move on our part," Webb continued deliberately.

===Chinese===
"而他们正等着我们做出这种举动，"韦布慢条斯理地继续说。

===Original===
"All right, so they'll be watching!" the major said, about to lose his temper, "but it is about the only move we can make to back up the boys when they do go in."

===Chinese===
"好吧，那他们就在监视！"少校快要发火了，"但这差不多是我们唯一能做的、好在小伙子们进去后接应他们的举动了。"

===Original===
He whipped around the door and was gone. Webb got up slowly. "I will work over the maps again," he told Ashe. "We haven't scouted that area, and we don't dare send a photo-plane over it now. Any trip in will be a stab in the dark."

===Chinese===
他猛地转身出了门。韦布慢慢站起来。"我再研究一下地图，"他告诉阿什。"我们从未侦察过那一带，现在也不敢派侦察机过去。任何行动都是在黑暗中摸索。"

===Original===
"When you have only one road, you take it," Ashe replied. "I'll be glad to see anything you can show me, Miles."

===Chinese===
"只有一条路的时候，就走这条路，"阿什回答。"迈尔斯（Miles），你能给我看的任何东西我都很想看。"

===Original===
If Ross had believed that his pretrial-run cramming had been a rigorous business, he was soon to laugh at that estimation. Since the burden of the next jump would rest on only three of them—Ashe, McNeil, and himself—they were plunged into a whirlwind of instruction, until Ross, dazed and too tired to sleep on the third night, believed that he was more completely bewildered than indoctrinated. He said as much sourly to McNeil.

===Chinese===
如果说罗斯曾以为任务前的突击训练已经够严苛了，那他很快就会嘲笑自己这种看法了。因为下一次穿越的重担只落在他们三个人身上——阿什、麦克尼尔和他自己——于是他们被卷入一场教学旋风，直到第三天晚上罗斯晕头转向、累得睡不着，觉得自己与其说是接受了灌输，不如说是彻底糊涂了。他酸溜溜地跟麦克尼尔说了这番话。

===Original===
"Base has pulled back three other teams," McNeil replied. "But the men have to go to school again, and they won't be ready to come on for maybe three, four weeks. To change runs means unlearning stuff as well as learning it—"

===Chinese===
"基地撤回了另外三组人，"麦克尼尔答道。"但那些人还得重新上课，大概三四个星期后才能准备好。转换任务意味着既要忘掉旧东西，又要学新东西——"

===Original===
"What about new men?"

===Chinese===
"新人呢？"

===Original===
"Don't think Kelgarries isn't out now beating the bushes for some! Only, we have to be fitted to the physical type we are supposed to represent. For instance, set a small, dark-headed pugnose among your Norse sea rovers, and he's going to be noticed—maybe remembered too well. We can't afford to take that chance. So Kelgarries had to discover men who not only look the part but are also temperamentally fitted for this job. You can't plant a fellow who thinks as a seaman—not a seaman, you understand, but one whose mind works in that pattern—among a wandering tribe of cattle herders. The protection for the man and the project lies in his being fitted into the right spot at the right time."

===Chinese===
"你以为凯尔加里斯现在没在到处搜罗人才吗！只不过，我们必须符合我们要扮演的体貌特征。比方说，把一个小个子、黑头发、塌鼻子的人塞进你的挪威海盗队里，他立刻就会被注意到——也许还会被牢牢记住。我们冒不起这个险。所以凯尔加里斯不仅要找到长得像的人，还得找到性格气质也适合这份工作的人。你不能把一个满脑子水手思维的人——不是说他真的是水手，而是说他的思维方式是那一类——安插到一个游牧的牧牛部落里。保护个人和项目的关键，就是让他在正确的时间出现在正确的位置。"

===Original===
Ross had never really thought of that point before. Now he realized that he and Ashe and McNeil were of a common mold. All about the same height, they shared brown hair and light eyes—Ashe's blue, his own gray, and McNeil's hazel—and they were of similar build, small-boned, lean, and quick-moving. He had not seen any of the true Beakermen except on the films. But now, recalling those, he could see that the three time traders were of the same general physical type as the far-roving people they used as a cover.

===Chinese===
罗斯以前从未真正想过这一点。现在他意识到自己、阿什和麦克尼尔是同一模子刻出来的。三人身高相仿，都是棕色头发、浅色眼睛——阿什是蓝色，他自己是灰色，麦克尼尔是淡褐色——体型也相似，骨架小，精瘦，动作敏捷。除了影片之外，他还没见过真正的比克尔人。但现在回忆起来，他能看出这三个时间商人（time traders）的体貌特征和他们所假扮的那些远行商人大致属于同一类型。

===Original===
It was on the morning of the fifth day while the three were studying a map Webb had produced that Kelgarries, followed at his own weighty pace by Millaird, burst in upon them.

===Chinese===
第五天早上，三人正在研究韦布提供的一张地图时，凯尔加里斯冲了进来，身后跟着步履沉重的米莱尔德。

===Original===
"We have it! This time we have the luck! The Reds slipped. Oh, how they slipped!"

===Chinese===
"我们拿到了！这次运气在我们这边！赤色分子露了马脚。哦，他们露了多大的马脚啊！"

===Original===
Webb watched the major, a thin little smile pulling at his pursed mouth. "Miracles sometimes do happen," he remarked. "I suppose the sub has a fix for us."

===Chinese===
韦布看着少校，薄薄的嘴唇微微上扬。"奇迹有时候确实会发生，"他说。"想必潜艇给我们定位了。"

===Original===
Kelgarries passed over the flimsy strip of paper he had been waving as a banner of triumph. Webb read the notation on it and bent over the map, making a mark with one of those needle-sharp pencils which seemed to grow in his breast pocket, ready for use. Then he made a second mark.

===Chinese===
凯尔加里斯递过来他一直在当作胜利旗帜挥舞的那张薄纸条。韦布读了上面的记录，俯身到地图前，用一支从胸袋里像长出来似的随时待用的削得极尖的铅笔做了个标记。接着又做了第二个标记。

===Original===
"Well, it narrows it a bit," he conceded. Ashe looked in turn and laughed.

===Chinese===
"嗯，范围缩小了一些，"他承认。阿什依次看了看，笑了。

===Original===
"I would like to hear your definition of 'narrow' sometime, Miles. Remember we have to cover this on foot, and a difference of twenty miles can mean a lot."

===Chinese===
"迈尔斯，我倒想听听你对'缩小'的定义。别忘了我们得靠双脚走完这段路，差二十英里可不是小事。"

===Original===
"That mark is quite a bit in from the sea." McNeil offered his own protest when he saw the marking. "We don't know that country—"

===Chinese===
"那个标记离海岸可有不少距离。"麦克尼尔看到标记也提出了异议。"我们对那地方一无所知——"

===Original===
Webb shoved his glasses back for the hundredth time that morning. "I suppose we could consider this critical, condition red," he said in such a dubious tone that he might have been begging someone to protest his statement. But no one did. Millaird was busy with the map.

===Chinese===
韦布把眼镜推回原位——这天早上他已经做了上百次这个动作了。"我想我们可以把这视为危急情况，红色警报，"他用那种犹疑不决的语气说道，简直像是在求人反驳他的判断。但没人反驳。米莱尔德正忙着研究地图。

===Original===
"I think we do, Miles!" He looked to Ashe. "You'll parachute in. The packs with which you will be equipped are special stuff. Once you have them off sprinkle them with a powder Miles will provide and in ten minutes there won't be enough of them left for anyone to identify. We haven't but a dozen of these, and we can't throw them away except in a crisis. Find the base and rig up the detector. Your fix in this time will be easy—but it is the other end of the line we must have. Until you locate that, stick to the job. Don't communicate with us until you have it!"

===Chinese===
"我认为我们确实到了这一步，迈尔斯！"他转向阿什。"你们要空降。你们装备的背包是特制材料。取下来后撒上迈尔斯提供的粉末，十分钟之内就会毁得面目全非。这种背包我们只有十二个，除非紧急关头不能浪费。找到据点，架设探测器。你们在这个时间层的定位不难——但我们必须拿到另一端的坐标。在找到之前，专心执行任务。找到之前不要联系我们！"

===Original===
"There is the possibility," Ashe pointed out, "the Reds may have more than one intermediate post. They probably have played it smart and set up a series of them to spoil a direct trace, as each would lead only to another farther back in time—"

===Chinese===
"有这种可能，"阿什指出，"赤色分子可能不只一个中转站。他们大概耍了聪明，设了一系列中转站来阻断直接追踪，因为每一站只会引向更早时间层的另一站——"

===Original===
"All right. If that proves true, just get us the next one back," Millaird returned. "From that we can trace them along if we must send in some of the boys wearing dinosaur skins later. We have to find their primary base, and if that hunt goes the hard way, well, we do it the hard way."

===Chinese===
"好吧。如果确实如此，就给我们找到下一个中转站，"米莱尔德回答。"从那里我们就能继续追踪，哪怕以后得派穿着恐龙皮的小伙子们进去。我们一定要找到他们的主基地，如果那趟搜寻走的是硬路——那就硬上。"

===Original===
"How did you get the fix?" McNeil asked.

===Chinese===
"你们怎么定位的？"麦克尼尔问。

===Original===
"One of their field parties ran into trouble and yelled for help."

===Chinese===
"他们的一支野外分队遇上了麻烦，发出了求救信号。"

===Original===
"Did they get it?"

===Chinese===
"他们得救了吗？"

===Original===
The major grinned. "What do you think? You know the rules—and the ones the Reds play by are twice as tough on their own men."

===Chinese===
少校咧嘴一笑。"你猜呢？你知道规矩——赤色分子对自己人执行的规矩比这严酷两倍。"

===Original===
"What kind of trouble?" Ashe wanted to know.

===Chinese===
"什么性质的麻烦？"阿什想知道。

===Original===
"Some kind of a local religious dispute. We do our best with their code, but we're not a hundred percent perfect in reading it. I gather they were playing with a local god and got their fingers burned."

===Chinese===
"某种当地宗教纠纷。我们尽力破译他们的密码，但解读能力还达不到百分之百准确。我推测他们跟某个当地神祇搅在一起，结果引火烧身。"

===Original===
"Lurgha again, eh?" Ashe smiled.

===Chinese===
"又是卢尔加（Lurgha），是吧？"阿什笑了。

===Original===
"Foolish," Webb said impatiently. "That is a silly thing to do. You were almost over the edge of prudence yourself, Gordon, with that Lurgha business. To use the Great Mother was a ticklish thing to try, and you were lucky to get out of it so easily."

===Chinese===
"愚蠢，"韦布不耐烦地说。"这种事干得实在不明智。戈登，你自己在卢尔加那件事上也差点过了谨慎的底线。用地母（the Great Mother）是很冒险的尝试，你能那么轻易脱身纯属走运。"

===Original===
"Once was enough," Ashe agreed. "Though using it may have saved our lives. But I assure you I am not starting a holy war or setting up as a prophet."

===Chinese===
"一次就够了，"阿什同意道。"虽说那次用它也许救了我们的命。但我保证我不会发动圣战，也不会自立为先知。"

===Original===
Ross had been taught something of map reading, but mentally he could not make what he saw on paper resemble the countryside. A few landmarks, if there were any outstanding ones, were all he could hope to impress upon his memory until he was actually on the ground.

===Chinese===
罗斯受过一些地图识读训练，但在脑子里他没法让纸上的图形与实际地形对应起来。如果有什么醒目的地标，那也是他到实地之前能指望记住的全部了。

===Original===
Landing there according to Millaird's instruction was another experience he would not have chosen of his own accord. To jump was a matter of timing, and in the dark with a measure of rain thrown in, the action was anything but pleasant. Leaving the plane in a blind, follow-the-leader fashion, Ross found the descent into darkness one of the worst trials he had yet faced. But he did not make too bad a landing in the small parklike expanse they had chosen for their target.

===Chinese===
按照米莱尔德的指示在那里着陆是他不会主动选择的又一种体验。跳伞讲究时机，何况还是在黑暗中，还夹着雨，这滋味一点都不好受。盲跳跟队下机的过程中，罗斯发现坠入黑暗是他迄今为止经历过的最难受的考验之一。但在他们选定的小片公园般的开阔地里，他的着陆还算没出大问题。

===Original===
Ross pulled loose his harness and chute, dragging them to what he judged to be the center of the clearing. Hearing a plaintive bray from the air, he dodged as one of the two burden asses sent to join them landed and began to kick at its trappings. The animals they had chosen were the most docile available and they had been given sedation before the jump so that now, feeling Ross's hands, the donkey stood quietly while Ross stripped it of its hanging straps.

===Chinese===
罗斯解开安全带和降落伞，把它们拖到他判断是林间空地中央的位置。空中传来一声哀怨的驴叫，他躲闪了一下——派来协助的两头驮驴中的一头着了陆，开始踢腾身上的挽具。他们选的是最温顺的驴子，跳伞前还注射了镇静剂，所以现在感觉到罗斯的手，驴子安安静静地站着，任他解下身上悬垂的皮带。

===Original===
"Rossa—" The sound of his Beaker name called through the dark brought Ross facing in the other direction.

===Chinese===
"罗萨（Rossa）——"黑暗中传来他比克尔化名的呼唤声，罗斯转身面向另一个方向。

===Original===
"Here, and I have one of the donkeys."

===Chinese===
"在，我牵着一头驴。"

===Original===
"And I the other!" That was McNeil.

===Chinese===
"另一头在我这儿！"是麦克尼尔。

===Original===
Their eyes adjusted to a gloom which was not as thick as it would be in the forest and they worked fast. Then they dragged the parachutes together in a pile. The rain would, Webb had assured them, add to the rapid destruction wrought by the chemical he had provided. Ashe shook it over the pile, and there was a faint greenish glow. Then they moved away to the woodland and made camp for the balance of the night.

===Chinese===
三人的眼睛适应了黑暗——这里不像林深处那么黑——然后迅速行动起来。他们把降落伞拖到一起堆成一堆。韦布向他们保证，雨水会加速化学药剂的销毁效果。阿什把粉末撒到堆上，泛起淡淡的绿色荧光。随后他们退入林间，在那里扎营度过余下的夜晚。

===Original===
So much of their whole exploit depended upon luck, and this small part had been successful. Unless some agent had been stationed to watch for their arrival Ross believed they could not be spotted.

===Chinese===
他们整个行动有太多环节依赖运气，而这小小的一环算是成功了。除非有特工专门守在附近监视他们的到来，否则罗斯相信他们不可能被发现。

===Original===
The rest of their plan was elastic. Posing as traders who had come to open a new station, they were to stay near a river which drained a lake and then angled southward to the distant sea. They knew this section was only sparsely settled by small tribes, hardly larger than family clans. These people were generations behind the civilized level of the villagers of Britain—roving hunters who followed the sweep of game north or south with the seasons.

===Chinese===
他们计划的其余部分是弹性的。他们假扮前来开设新站点的商人，驻扎在一条河流附近——这条河从一个湖泊流出，然后折向南边，通向遥远的大海。他们知道这一带只有小部落零星居住，规模几乎不超过家族宗族。这些人比不列颠的村民落后了好几代——是随着季节追逐猎物南北迁徙的流浪猎人。

===Original===
Along the seashore the fishermen had established more permanent holdings which were slowly becoming towns. There were perhaps a few hardy pioneer farmers on the southern fringes of the district, but the principal reason traders came to this region was to get amber and furs. The Beaker people dealt in both.

===Chinese===
沿海岸线上，渔民们建立了更稳定的居住点，正在慢慢演变成城镇。这片区域的南缘可能有几个胆大的拓荒农民，但商人来此的主要目的是收购琥珀和毛皮。比克尔人两样都做。

===Original===
Now as the three sheltered under the wide branches of a towering pine Ashe fumbled with a pack and brought out the "beaker" which was the identifying mark of his adopted people. He measured into it a portion of the sour, stimulating drink which the traders introduced wherever they went. The cup passed from hand to hand, its taste unpleasant on the tongue, but comfortingly warm to one's middle.

===Chinese===
此刻三人躲在一棵高耸松树的宽大树枝下，阿什从背包里翻出那只"钟形杯（beaker）"——他所冒充的那个民族的身份标志。他量了一份酸涩而提神的饮料倒进去，这种饮料是商人每到一处都大力推广的。杯子在手中传递，味道在舌头上并不好受，但入腹之后令人舒坦地温暖起来。

===Original===
They took turns keeping the watch until the gray of false dawn became the clearer light of morning. After breakfasting on flat cakes of meal, they packed the donkeys, using the same knots and cross lashing which were the mark of real Beaker traders. Their bows protected from dampness under their cloaks, they set out to find the river and their path southward.

===Chinese===
三人轮值守夜，直到假曙光的灰白渐渐变成清晨的光亮。吃过面饼当早餐后，他们用比克尔商人特有的绳结和交叉捆扎法收拾好驮驴。弓箭裹在斗篷里防潮，然后他们出发去寻找那条河，踏上南行之路。

===Original===
Ashe led, Ross towed the donkeys, and McNeil brought up the rear. In the absence of a path they had to set a ragged course, keeping to the edge of the clearing until they saw the end of the lake.

===Chinese===
阿什在前面领路，罗斯牵着驴子，麦克尼尔殿后。没有现成的路，他们不得不沿着不规则的路线前行，沿着林间空地的边缘走，直到看见湖的尽头。

===Original===
"Woodsmoke," Ashe commented when they had completed two thirds of their journey. Ross sniffed and was able to smell it too. Nodding to Ashe, McNeil oozed into nothingness between the trees with an ease Murdock envied. As they waited for him to return, Ross became conscious of another life about them, one busy with its own concerns, which were in no way those of human beings, except that food and perhaps shelter were to be reckoned among them.

===Chinese===
"柴烟，"行程走了大约三分之二时阿什说道。罗斯嗅了嗅，也闻到了。麦克尼尔朝阿什点了点头，像烟一样无声无息地融进了树林间，那身手令默多克暗自羡慕。等他回来的时候，罗斯开始觉察到周围还有其他生命在活动，忙碌于与人类毫不相干的营生——若说有什么共通之处，大概只有食物和栖身之所这两样。

===Original===
In Britain, Ross had known there were others of his kind about, but this was different. Here, he could have believed it if he had been told he was the first man to walk this way.

===Chinese===
在不列颠，罗斯知道周围还有同族的人生活着，但这里不同。在这里，如果有人告诉他他是踏上此地的第一个人，他也会信以为真。

===Original===
A squirrel ran out on a tree limb and surveyed the two men with curious beady eyes, then clung head down on the tree trunk to see them better. One of the donkeys tossed its head, and the squirrel was gone with a flirt of its tail. Although it was quiet, there was a hum underneath the surface which Ross tried to analyze, to identify the many small sounds which went into its making.

===Chinese===
一只松鼠跑到树枝上，用圆溜溜的黑眼睛好奇地打量着两个男人，然后倒挂在树干上看得更清楚些。一头驴甩了甩头，松鼠尾巴一甩便不见了。四周虽然安静，但表面之下有一种嗡嗡的低鸣，罗斯试图分析，想辨认出构成这低鸣的种种细微声响。

===Original===
Perhaps because he was trying so hard, he noted the faint noise. His hand touched Ashe's arm and a slight movement of his head indicated the direction of the sound. Then, as fluidly as he had melted into the woods, McNeil returned. "Company," he said in a soft voice.

===Chinese===
也许正因为他过于专注，他察觉到了那微弱的声响。他的手碰了碰阿什的胳膊，微微偏头示意声音的方向。接着，麦克尼尔像融进树林时一样悄无声息地回来了。"有人，"他压低声音说。

===Original===
"What kind?"

===Chinese===
"什么人？"

===Original===
"Tribesmen, but wilder than any I've seen, even on the tapes. We are certainly out on the fringes now. These people look about cave level. I don't think they've ever heard of traders."

===Chinese===
"部落民，但比我见过的任何人都更原始，连记忆磁带（tapes）里的都没这么野。我们确实到了边缘地带。这些人看上去还在穴居文明的水平。我觉得他们从没听说过商人。"

===Original===
"How many?"

===Chinese===
"多少人？"

===Original===
"Three, maybe four families. Most of the males must be out hunting, but there're about ten children and six or seven women. I don't think they've had good luck lately by the look of them."

===Chinese===
"三四个家庭的样子。大部分成年男性应该都出去打猎了，但有大约十个孩子和六七个妇女。看他们的样子，我猜最近运气不太好。"

===Original===
"Maybe their luck and ours are going to turn together," Ashe said, motioning Ross forward with the donkeys. "We will circle about them to the river and then try bartering later. But I do want to establish contact."

===Chinese===
"也许他们的运气和我们的要一起转了，"阿什说，示意罗斯牵驴上前。"我们绕过他们到河边去，以后再设法交易。但我确实想跟他们建立接触。"

## IX

===Original===
"Not to be too hopeful—" McNeil rubbed his arm across his hot face—"so far, so good." After kicking from his path some of the branches Ross had lopped from the trees they had been felling, he went to help his companion roll another small log up to a shelter which was no longer temporary. If there had been any eyes other than the woodland hunters' to spy upon them, they would have seen only the usual procedure of the Beaker traders, busily constructing one of their posts.

===Chinese===
"别高兴得太早——"麦克尼尔用胳膊抹了一把汗津津的脸——"眼下还算顺利。"他把罗斯从伐倒的树上砍下来的枝条踢到一边，走过去帮同伴把另一根小圆木滚到那个已不再是临时窝棚的住所前。如果除了林中猎手之外还有别的眼睛在窥探他们，看到的只会是比克尔商人（Beaker traders）的寻常营生——忙着搭建一处贸易哨站。

===Original===
That they were being watched by the hunters, all three were certain. That there might be other spies in the forest, they had to assume for their own safety. They might prowl at night, but in the daytime all of the time agents kept within the bounds of the roles they were acting.

===Chinese===
三人都确信猎手们在监视他们。出于安全考虑，他们不得不假设林中可能还有其他间谍。他们可以在夜间活动，但白天所有时间特工都必须恪守扮演的角色。

===Original===
Barter with the head men of the hunting clan had brought those shy people into the camp of the strangers who had such wonders to exchange for tanned deer hides and better furs. The news of the traders' arrival spread quickly during the short time they had been here, so that two other clans had sent men to watch the proceedings.

===Chinese===
与狩猎氏族首领们的以物易物，把这些羞怯的猎人吸引到了外来者的营地——外来者拥有如此神奇的物件，愿与鞣制鹿皮和上等毛皮交换。商人们到来的消息在这短短时日里迅速传开，另外两个氏族也派了人来旁观交易。

===Original===
With the trade came news which the agents sifted and studied. Each of them had a list of questions to insert into their conversations with the tribesmen if and when that was possible. Although they did not share a common speech with the forest men, signs were informative and certain nouns could be quickly learned. In the meantime Ashe became friendly with the nearest and first of the clan groups they discovered, going hunting with the men as an excuse to penetrate the unknown section they must quarter in their search for the Red base.

===Chinese===
伴随贸易而来的是情报，特工们加以筛选和研读。每人都有一份问题清单，伺机在与部落人的交谈中穿插进去。虽然他们与森林居民没有共同语言，但手势传递了不少信息，某些名词也能迅速学会。与此同时，阿什与他们发现的第一个近邻氏族交好，跟随猎人们外出狩猎，以此为掩护深入那片未知区域——他们必须在那里搜寻赤色分子（the Reds）的基地。

===Original===
Ross drank river water and mopped his own hot face. "If the Reds aren't traders," he mused aloud, "what is their cover?"

===Chinese===
罗斯喝着河水，抹了一把汗湿的脸。"如果赤色分子不是商人，"他自言自语地琢磨，"那他们的伪装是什么？"

===Original===
McNeil shrugged. "A hunting tribe—fishermen—"

===Chinese===
麦克尼尔耸耸肩。"狩猎部落——渔夫——"

===Original===
"Where would they get the women and children?"

===Chinese===
"女人和孩子从哪儿来？"

===Original===
"The same way they get their men—recruit them in our own time. Or in the way lots of tribes grew during periods of stress."

===Chinese===
"跟招募男人一样——在我们自己的时代招募。或者像许多部落在动荡时期那样壮大。"

===Original===
Ross set down the water jug. "You mean, kill off the men, take over their families?" This was a cold-bloodedness he found sickening. Although he had always prided himself on his toughness, several times during his training at the project he had been confronted by things which shook his belief in his own strong stomach and nerve.

===Chinese===
罗斯放下水罐。"你是说，杀光男人，接管他们的家庭？"这种冷血令他作呕。尽管他一直以自己的强硬自夸，但在项目训练期间好几次遇到的事情动摇了他对自己铁胆雄心的信心。

===Original===
"It has been done," McNeil remarked bleakly, "hundreds of times by invaders. In this setup—small family clans, widely scattered—that move would be very easy."

===Chinese===
"这种事发生过，"麦克尼尔冷冷地说，"入侵者干过成百上千次。在这种环境下——小型家族氏族，分散居住——那招太容易得手了。"

===Original===
"They would have to pose as farmers, not hunters," Ross pointed out. "They couldn't move a base around with them."

===Chinese===
"他们得扮成农夫，而非猎人，"罗斯指出。"他们不能拖着一个基地四处走。"

===Original===
"All right, so they set up a farming village. Oh, I see what you mean—there isn't any village around here. Yet they are here, maybe underground."

===Chinese===
"好吧，那他们建一个农耕村落。哦，我明白你的意思了——这附近根本没有村落。可他们就在这里，也许在地下。"

===Original===
How right their guesses were they learned that night when Ashe returned, a deer's haunch on his shoulder. Ross knew him well enough by now to sense his preoccupation. "You found something?"

===Chinese===
他们的猜测有多准，当晚阿什扛着一条鹿腿回来时便见分晓。罗斯已经很了解阿什，立刻感觉到了他的心事重重。"发现了什么？"

===Original===
"A new set of ghosts," Ashe replied with a strange little smile.

===Chinese===
"又一群鬼，"阿什带着一丝奇怪的微笑答道。

===Original===
"Ghosts!" McNeil pounced upon that. "The Reds like to play the supernatural angle, don't they? First the voice of Lurgha and now ghosts. What do these ghosts do?"

===Chinese===
"鬼！"麦克尼尔立刻抓住了这个字眼。"赤色分子喜欢玩弄超自然把戏，是不是？先是卢尔加的声音，现在又是鬼。这些鬼是干什么的？"

===Original===
"They inhabit a bit of mountainous territory southeast of here, a stretch strictly taboo for all hunters. We were following a bison track until the beast headed for the ghost country. Then Ulffa called us off in a hurry. It seems that the hunter who goes in there after his quarry never reappears, or if he does, it's in a damaged condition, blown upon by ghosts and burned to death! That's one point."

===Chinese===
"他们占据了这里东南方的一片山地，所有猎人一律禁入。我们原本在追踪一头野牛的足迹，直到那头牛朝鬼域跑去。乌尔法（Ulffa）急忙叫我们撤退。据说进去追猎的猎人再也没有出来过，即使出来，也是残破不堪——被鬼吹过，活活烧死！这是一点。"

===Original===
He sat down by the fire and stretched his arms wearily. "The second is a little more disturbing for us. A Beaker camp about twenty miles south of here, as far as I can judge, was exterminated just a week ago. The message was passed to me because I was thought to be a kinsman of the slain—"

===Chinese===
他在火堆旁坐下，疲惫地伸了个懒腰。"第二点更令人不安。据我判断，大约二十英里南边的一处比克尔营地，在一周前被灭了。消息传到我这里，是因为他们以为我是死者的亲族——"

===Original===
McNeil sat up. "Done because they were hunting us?"

===Chinese===
麦克尼尔猛地坐起来。"是冲着追捕我们来的？"

===Original===
"Might well be. On the other hand, the affair may have been just one of general precaution."

===Chinese===
"很可能。但换个角度看，也许只是一次预防性清洗。"

===Original===
"The ghosts did it?" Ross wanted to know.

===Chinese===
"那些鬼干的？"罗斯问。

===Original===
"I asked that. No, it seems that strange tribesmen overran it at night."

===Chinese===
"我问过了。不是，似乎是一群陌生的部落人在夜里袭击了那里。"

===Original===
"At night?" McNeil whistled.

===Chinese===
"夜里？"麦克尼尔吹了声口哨。

===Original===
"Just so." Ashe's tone was dry. "The tribes do not fight that way. Either someone slipped up in his briefing, or the Reds are overconfident and don't care about the rules. But it was the work of tribesmen, or their counterfeits. There is also a nasty rumor speeding about that the ghosts do not relish traders and that they might protest intrusions of such with penalties all around—"

===Chinese===
"正是如此。"阿什语气干涩。"部落人不那样打仗。要么是谁的简报出了纰漏，要么是赤色分子过于自信，不在乎规矩。但干这事的是部落人，或者他们的冒牌货。还有一条恶毒的谣言在流传，说那些鬼不待见商人，可能会对入侵者施以惩罚，一个不留——"

===Original===
"Like the Wrath of Lurgha," supplied Ross.

===Chinese===
"就像卢尔加的怒火（Lurgha's Wrath），"罗斯补充道。

===Original===
"There is a certain repetition in this which suggests a lot to the suspicious mind," Ashe agreed.

===Chinese===
"这里面有种重复的模式，"阿什赞同道，"多疑的人会觉得意味深长。"

===Original===
"I'd say no more hunting expeditions for the present," McNeil said. "It is too easy to mistake a friend for a deer and weep over his grave afterward."

===Chinese===
"我说眼下别再出去打猎了，"麦克尼尔说。"太容易把朋友错当成鹿，事后对着他的坟哭。"

===Original===
"That is a thought which entered my mind several times this afternoon," Ashe agreed. "These people are deceptively simple on the surface, but their minds do not work along the same patterns as ours. We try to outwit them, but it takes only one slip to make it fatal. In the meantime, I think we'd better make this place a little more snug, and it might be well to post sentries as unobtrusively as possible."

===Chinese===
"这个想法今天下午在我脑子里转了好几回，"阿什同意道。"这些人表面上看似简单，但思维方式和我们不同。我们试图智胜他们，可只要一个闪失就是致命的。眼下，我觉得我们最好把这个地方弄舒服些，再尽可能不显眼地布置哨兵。"

===Original===
"How about faking some signs of a ruined camp and heading into the blue ourselves?" McNeil asked. "We could strike for the ghost mountains, traveling by night, and Ulffa's crowd would think we were finished off."

===Chinese===
"伪造一些营地被毁的迹象，我们自己远走高飞，怎么样？"麦克尼尔提议。"我们可以趁夜色直奔鬼山，乌尔法那帮人会以为我们完蛋了。"

===Original===
"An idea to keep in mind. The point against it would be the missing bodies. It seems that the tribesmen who raided the Beaker camp left some very distasteful evidence of what happened to the camp's personnel. And those we can't produce to cover our trail."

===Chinese===
"值得记住的想法。但反对的理由是缺少尸体。袭击比克尔营地的部落人似乎留下了一些令人不快的证据，说明营地人员的下场。而那些东西我们可拿不出来掩护行踪。"

===Original===
McNeil was not yet convinced. "We might be able to fake something along that line too—"

===Chinese===
麦克尼尔仍不服气。"我们也许也能伪造一些类似的——"

===Original===
"We may have to fake nothing," Ross cut in softly. He was standing close to the edge of the clearing where they were building their hut, his hand on one of the saplings in the palisade they had set up so laboriously that day. Ashe was beside him in an instant.

===Chinese===
"也许什么也不用伪造，"罗斯轻声打断道。他站在他们搭建小屋的林中空地边缘，手扶着那天费了好大劲竖起的栅栏中的一根小树。阿什瞬间就到了他身旁。

===Original===
"What is it?"

===Chinese===
"怎么了？"

===Original===
Ross's hours of listening to the sounds of the wilderness were his measuring gauge now. "That bird has never called from inland before. It is the blue one we've seen fishing for frogs along the river."

===Chinese===
罗斯长时间聆听荒野声响的经验此刻成了他的标尺。"那种鸟从没在内陆叫过。是我们见过在河边捉青蛙的那种蓝色鸟。"

===Original===
Ashe, not even glancing at the forest, went for the water jug. "Get your trail supplies," he ordered.

===Chinese===
阿什甚至没朝森林瞥一眼，径直走向水罐。"带上行装，"他命令道。

===Original===
Their leather pouches which held enough iron rations to keep them going were always at hand. McNeil gathered them from behind the fur curtain fronting their half-finished cabin. Again the bird called, its cry piercing and covering a long distance. Ross could understand why a careless man would select it for the signal. He crossed the clearing to the donkeys' shelter, slashing through their nose halters. Probably the patient little beasts would swiftly fall victims to some forest prowlers, but at least they would have their chance to escape.

===Chinese===
装着足够口粮的皮囊随时放在手边。麦克尼尔从半完工小屋前的毛皮帘子后面把它们取来。那只鸟又叫了一声，声音尖利，传得很远。罗斯明白粗心的人为什么会选它当信号。他穿过空地走向驴子的棚子，割断了鼻绳。这些温顺的小畜生大概很快就会沦为林中掠食者的猎物，但至少它们有了逃生的机会。

===Original===
McNeil, his cloak slung about him to conceal the ration bags, picked up the leather bucket as if he were merely going down to the river for water, and came to join Ross. They believed that they were carrying it off well, that the camp must appear normal to any lurkers in the woods. But either they had made some slip or the enemy was impatient. An arrow sped out of the night to flash across the fire, and Ashe escaped death only because he had leaned forward to feed the flames. His arm swung out and sent the water in the jar hissing onto the blaze as he himself rolled in the other direction.

===Chinese===
麦克尼尔把斗篷裹在身上遮住口粮袋，提起皮水桶，装作不过是去河边打水的样子，走过来和罗斯会合。他们以为掩饰得很好，营地在林中暗哨看来一定一切如常。可要么是他们露了马脚，要么是敌人等不及了。一支箭从夜色中射出，擦过火堆飞过，阿什之所以躲过一死，只因他恰好俯身添柴。他手臂一挥，把水罐里的水浇向火焰，自己则滚向另一侧。

===Original===
Ross plunged for the brush with McNeil. Lying flat on the half-frozen ground, they started to work their way to the river bank where the open area would make surprise less possible.

===Chinese===
罗斯和麦克尼尔一起扑进灌木丛。他们趴在半冻的土地上，开始匍匐向河岸移动，那里开阔的地带不容易遭到偷袭。

===Original===
"Ashe?" he whispered and felt McNeil's warm breath on his cheek as he replied:

===Chinese===
"阿什？"他低声问，感到麦克尼尔温热的气息喷在脸颊上，回答道：

===Original===
"He'll make it the other way! He's the best we have for this sort of job."

===Chinese===
"他会从另一条路走！他是我们当中最擅长这种事的。"

===Original===
They made a worm's progress, twice lying, with dagger in hand, while they listened to a faint rustle which betrayed the passing of one of the attackers. Both times Ross was tempted to rise and try to cut off the stranger, but he fought down the impulse. He had learned a control of himself that would have been impossible for him a few months earlier.

===Chinese===
他们像虫子一样蠕动前进，两次趴下不动，匕首在手，听着轻微的沙沙声——那是某个袭击者走过暴露的行迹。两次罗斯都想跃起截击那个陌生人，但都压住了冲动。他学会了克制自己，这在几个月前是不可能的。

===Original===
The glimmer of the river was pale through the clumps of bushes which sometimes grew into the flood. In this country winter still clung tenaciously in shadowy places with cups of leftover snow, and there was a bite in the wind and water. Ross rose to his knees with an involuntary gasp as a scream cut through the night. He wrenched around toward the camp, only to feel McNeil's hand clamp on his forearm.

===Chinese===
河水的微光透过有时长到洪水里的灌木丛隐约可见。在这片土地上，冬天仍在阴暗处顽固地盘踞着，残雪成堆，风和水都刺骨寒冷。一声尖叫划破夜空，罗斯不由自主地倒吸一口气，跪了起来。他扭身朝营地望去，却被麦克尼尔一把攥住了前臂。

===Original===
"That was a donkey," whispered McNeil urgently. "Come on, let's go down to that ford we discovered!"

===Chinese===
"那是驴子，"麦克尼尔急切地低语。"快走，去我们发现的那个浅滩！"

===Original===
They turned south, daring now to trot, half bent to the ground. The river was swollen with spring floods which were only now beginning to subside, but two days earlier they had noticed a sandbar at one spot. By crossing that shelf across the bed, they might hope to put water between them and the unknown enemy tonight. It would give them a breathing space, even though Ross privately shrank from the thought of plowing into the stream. He had seen good-sized trees swirling along in the current only yesterday. And to make such a dash in the dark……

===Chinese===
他们转向南方，现在敢半弯着腰小跑了。河水因春汛而暴涨，此刻才刚开始退去，但两天前他们注意到某处有一道沙洲。如果能涉过那道横在河床上的沙梁，今晚就有望在自己和不明敌人之间隔出一条水来。这能争取到喘息之机，尽管罗斯私下里一想到要趟进那条河就发怵。昨天他还亲眼看见不小的大树在激流中翻滚。在黑暗中做这样的冲刺……

===Original===
From McNeil's throat burst a startling sound which Ross had last heard in Britain—the questing howl of a hunting wolf. The cry was answered seconds later from downstream.

===Chinese===
麦克尼尔喉间迸出一声令人心惊的嘶吼——那是罗斯在不列颠（Britain）最后听到的声音——猎狼探寻猎物的嚎叫。几秒钟后，下游传来了回应。

===Original===
"Ashe!"

===Chinese===
"阿什！"

===Original===
They worked their way along the edge of the water with continued care, until they came upon Ashe at last, so much a part of his background that Ross started when the lump he had taken for a bush hunched forward to join them. Together they made the river crossing and turned south again to head for the mountains. It was then that disaster struck.

===Chinese===
他们小心翼翼地沿着水边前行，终于找到了阿什——他与周遭融为一体，以至于罗斯猛然一惊：那个他以为是灌木丛的东西竟弓起身朝他们走来。三人一起渡过河流，再次转向南方朝山地进发。就在这时，灾祸降临了。

===Original===
Ross heard no birdcall warning this time. Though he was on guard, he never sensed the approach of the man who struck him down from behind. One moment he had been trailing McNeil and Ashe; the next moment was black nothingness.

===Chinese===
这一次罗斯没有听到鸟鸣预警。尽管他保持着警觉，却丝毫没有察觉身后那个人的逼近。一秒钟前他还在跟随麦克尼尔和阿什；下一秒便是漆黑一片。

===Original===
He was aware of a throb of pain which carried throughout his body and then localized in his head. Forcing open his eyes, the dazzle of light was like a spear point striking directly into his head, intensifying his pain to agony. He brought his hand up to his face and felt stickiness there.

===Chinese===
他感到一阵剧痛贯穿全身，随后集中在头部。他勉强睁开眼，强光的刺目犹如矛尖直刺脑门，把疼痛加剧到难以忍受的地步。他抬手摸了摸脸，摸到了黏腻的东西。

===Original===
"Assha—" He believed he called that aloud, but he did not even hear his own voice. They were in a valley; a wolf had attacked him out of the bushes. Wolf? No, the wolf was dead, but then it came alive again to howl on a river bank.

===Chinese===
"阿莎——"他觉得喊出了声，却连自己的声音都没听见。他们在山谷里；一头狼从灌木丛中扑向了他。狼？不，狼已经死了，但它又活了过来，在河岸上嚎叫。

===Original===
Ross forced his eyes open once more, enduring the pain of beams he recognized as sunshine. He turned his head to avoid the glare. It was hard to focus, but he fought to steady himself. There was some reason why it was necessary to move, to get away. But away from what and where? When Ross tried to think he could only see muddled pictures which had no connection.

===Chinese===
罗斯再次强迫自己睁开眼，忍受着那被他认出是阳光的光束带来的刺痛。他扭过头避开强光。视线难以聚焦，但他努力让自己稳住。有什么理由必须移动，必须逃离。但逃离什么？逃往何处？罗斯试图思考，却只看到杂乱无章、彼此毫无关联的画面。

===Original===
Then a moving object crossed his very narrow field of vision, passing between him and a thing he knew was a tree trunk. A four-footed creature with a red tongue hanging from its jaws. It came toward him stiff-legged, growling low in its throat, and sniffed at his body before barking in short excited bursts of sound.

===Chinese===
接着一个移动的物体掠过他极其狭窄的视野，从他和一根他知道是树干的东西之间穿过。一头四足兽，血红的舌头垂在嘴外。它僵直着腿朝他走来，喉咙里发出低沉的咆哮，嗅了嗅他的身体，然后兴奋地短促吠叫了几声。

===Original===
The noise hurt his head so much that Ross closed his eyes. Then a shock of icy liquid thrown into his face aroused him to make a feeble protest and he saw, hanging over him in a strange upside-down way, a bearded face which he knew from the past.

===Chinese===
那声音疼得他闭上了眼。接着一盆冰凉的液体泼在他脸上，他被激醒，虚弱地抗议了一声，然后看到一张倒悬的、胡须浓密的面孔俯视着他——一张他从过去认识的面孔。

===Original===
Hands were laid on him and the roughness with which he was moved sent Ross spiraling back into the dark once again. When he aroused for the second time it was night and the pain in his head was dulled. He put out his hands and discovered that he lay on a pile of fur robes, and was covered by one.

===Chinese===
有人搬动了他，粗鲁的力道让罗斯再次坠入黑暗。第二次醒来时已是夜晚，头疼缓和了许多。他伸出手，发现自己躺在一堆毛皮袍子上，身上盖着一件。

===Original===
"Assha—" Again he tried that name. But it was not Assha who came in answer to his feeble call. The woman who knelt beside him with a horn cup in her hand had neatly braided hair in which gray strands showed silver by firelight. Ross knew he had seen her before, but again where and when eluded him. She slipped a sturdy arm under his head and raised him while the world whirled about. The edge of the horn cup was pressed to his lips, and he drank bitter stuff which burned in his throat and lit a fire in his insides. Then he was left to himself once again and in spite of his pain and bewilderment he slept.

===Chinese===
"阿莎——"他又试了一次这个名字。但应声而来的不是阿莎。跪在他身旁、手里拿着一只角杯的女人，头发编得整整齐齐，灰白的发丝在火光中泛着银光。罗斯知道自己见过她，可再一次想不起何时何地。她将一只粗壮的胳膊伸到他头下扶他坐起，整个世界天旋地转。角杯的边缘抵在他唇上，他喝下了苦涩的液体，那东西灼烧着他的喉咙，在体内点燃了一把火。然后他又被留给了自己，尽管疼痛和迷惘，还是睡着了。

===Original===
How many days he lay in the camp of Ulffa, tended by the chief's head wife, Ross found it hard to reckon. It was Frigga who had argued the tribe into caring for a man they believed almost dead when they found him, and who nursed Ross back to life with knowledge acquired through half a hundred exchanges between those wise women who were the doctors and priestesses of these roaming peoples.

===Chinese===
他在乌尔法的营地里躺了多少天，由首领的正妻照料，罗斯很难算清。是弗丽嘉（Frigga）说服部落收留了一个他们发现时以为已经奄奄一息的男人，用从那些游牧民族的智者——既是医者也是女祭司——之间上百次交流中积累的知识，把罗斯从死亡线上拉了回来。

===Original===
Why Frigga had bothered with the injured stranger at all Ross learned when he was able to sit up and marshal his bewildered thoughts into some sort of order. The matriarch of the tribe thirsted for knowledge. That same urge which had led her to certain experiments with herbs, had made her consider Ross a challenge to her healing skill. When she knew that he would live she determined to learn from him all he had to give.

===Chinese===
弗丽嘉为何要为一个受伤的陌生人费心，罗斯在能够坐起来、把混乱的思绪理出些许头绪之后才明白。这位部落的女族长渴望知识。正是同一种冲动驱使她做了各种草药实验，也让她把罗斯视为对自己医术的挑战。当她知道他能活下来时，便下定决心要从他那里学到他能给出的一切。

===Original===
Ulffa and the men of the tribe might have eyed the metal weapons of the traders with awe and avid desire, but Frigga wanted more than trade goods. She wanted the secret of the making of such cloth as the strangers wore, everything she could learn of their lives and the lands through which they had come. She plied Ross with endless questions which he answered as best he could, for he lay in an odd dreamy state where only the present had any reality. The past was dim and far away, and while he was now and then dimly aware that he had something to do, he forgot it easily.

===Chinese===
乌尔法和部落的男人们也许对商人的金属武器怀着敬畏和热切的渴望，但弗丽嘉想要的不止是贸易品。她想知道陌生人穿的那种布料的制作秘密，想了解他们的一切生活和他们来时经过的那些土地。她不停地向罗斯抛出问题，他尽其所能地回答，因为他躺在一种奇异的恍惚状态中，唯有当下才是真实的。过去模糊而遥远，虽然他偶尔隐约意识到自己有什么事要做，却很快就会忘记。

===Original===
The chief and his men prowled the half-built station after the attackers had withdrawn, bringing back with them a handful of loot—a bronze razor, two skinning knives, some fishhooks, a length of cloth which Frigga appropriated. Ross eyed this spoil indifferently, making no claim upon it. His interest in everything about him was often blanked out by headaches which kept him limp on his bed, uncaring and stupid for hours or even full days.

===Chinese===
袭击者撤退后，首领和他的男人们在那座半完工的哨站里搜寻，带回一把战利品——一只青铜剃刀、两把剥皮刀、几只鱼钩、一段布料，被弗丽嘉据为己有。罗斯漠然地看着这些缴获物，没有索取任何东西。他对自己周围一切的兴趣常常被头痛淹没，那头痛让他瘫在床上，对外界不闻不问、木然呆滞，一持续就是好几个小时甚至整天。

===Original===
He gathered that the tribe had been living in fear of an attack from the same raiders who had wiped out the trading post. But at last their scouts returned with the information that the enemy had gone south.

===Chinese===
他得知部落一直生活在恐惧之中，担心那支摧毁了贸易哨站的同一伙袭击者再次来袭。但最终他们的斥候带回消息：敌人已经南撤了。

===Original===
There was one change of which Ross was not aware but which might have startled both Ashe and McNeil. Ross Murdock had indeed died under that blow which had left him unconscious beside the river. The young man whom Frigga had drawn back to sense and a slow recovery was Rossa of the Beaker people. This same Rossa nursed a hot desire for vengeance against those who had struck him down and captured his kinsmen, a feeling which the family tribe who had rescued him could well understand.

===Chinese===
有一个变化罗斯自己并未察觉，但足以令阿什和麦克尼尔大吃一惊。罗斯·默多克（Ross Murdock）确实在河边被击倒昏迷的那一击中"死去"了。被弗丽嘉从昏迷中救回并缓慢恢复知觉的年轻人，是比克尔人罗萨（Rossa of the Beaker people）。正是这个罗萨心中燃烧着复仇的渴望——向那些击倒他、掳走他亲族的人复仇——而救了他的家族部落对此深有共鸣。

===Original===
There was the same old urgency pushing him to try his strength now, to keep to his feet even when they were unsteady. His bow was gone, but Ross spent hours fashioning another, and he traded his copper bracelet for the best dozen arrows in Ulffa's camp. The jet pin from his cloak he presented to Frigga with all his gratitude.

===Chinese===
那股古老的紧迫感驱使他现在就试探自己的力气，即使双腿发软也要站稳。他的弓丢了，但罗斯花了好几天工夫自制了一把新的，又用铜手镯换来了乌尔法营地里最好的十二支箭。斗篷上的黑曜石胸针他送给了弗丽嘉，连同全部的感激。

===Original===
Now that his strength was coming back he could not rest easy in the camp. He was ready to leave, even though the gashes on his head were still tender to the touch. Ulffa indulgently planned a hunt southward, and Rossa took the trail with the tribesmen.

===Chinese===
体力既已恢复，他在营地里再也待不住了。他准备动身，尽管头上的伤口触之仍痛。乌尔法纵容地安排了一场南行的狩猎，罗萨随部落猎人们踏上了征途。

===Original===
He broke with the clan hunters when they turned aside at the beginning of the taboo land. Ross, his own mind submerged and taken over by his Beaker cover, hesitated too. Yet he could not give up, and the others left him there, his eyes on the forbidden heights, unhappy and tormented by more than the headaches which still came and went with painful regularity. In the mountains lay what he sought—a hidden something within his brain told him that over and over—but the mountains were taboo, and he should not venture into them.

===Chinese===
当氏族猎人们在禁地边缘折返时，他与他们分道扬镳了。罗斯——他的自我意识已被比克尔人的伪装身份淹没和接管——也犹豫了。然而他无法放弃，其他人便将他留在那里。他望着那片被禁的山峦，不仅被仍以疼痛的规律反复发作的头痛折磨，更被内心深处的渴望煎熬。山中藏着他寻找的东西——脑中一个隐秘的声音一遍又一遍地告诉他——但那山是禁地，他不该贸然闯入。

===Original===
How long he might have hesitated there if he had not come upon the trail, Ross did not know. But on the day after the hunters of Ulffa's clan left, a glint of sunlight striking between two trees pointed out a woodsman's blaze on a third tree trunk. The two halves of Ross's memory clicked together for an instant as he examined that cut. He knew that it marked a trace and he pushed on, hunting a second cut and then a third. Convinced that these would lead him into the unknown territory, Ross's desire to explore overcame the grafted superstitions of his briefing.

===Chinese===
