若非发现那条小径，罗斯不知自己还会在那里犹豫多久。乌尔法氏族的猎人们离开后的第二天，阳光从两棵树之间射入，照亮了第三棵树干上的一个伐木人标记。罗斯端详那道切口时，记忆中的两个部分瞬间咬合在了一起。他知道那标记了一条痕迹，便继续前行，寻找第二个、第三个标记。确信这些痕迹会把他带入未知领地后，罗斯探索的欲望压倒了移植进他脑中的那些迷信。

===Original===
There were other signs that this was an often-traveled route: a spring cleared of leaves and walled with stone, a couple of steps cut in the turf on a steep slope. Ross moved warily, alert to any sound. He might not be an expert woodsman, but he was learning fast, perhaps the faster because his false memories now supplanted the real ones.

===Chinese===
还有其他迹象表明这是一条常有人走的路：一处被清理过落叶、四周砌了石墙的泉眼，在陡坡的草皮上凿出的几级台阶。罗斯谨慎前行，对任何声响都保持警觉。他也许不是老练的林中行者，但他学得很快——或许正因为虚假的记忆已经取代了真实的。

===Original===
That night he built no fire, crawling instead into the heart of a rotted log to sleep, awakening once to the call of a wolf and another time at the distant crash of a dead tree yielding to wind.

===Chinese===
那天晚上他没有生火，而是爬进一根朽木的空心里睡觉。一次被狼嚎惊醒，另一次则因远处一棵枯树被风折断的轰响而醒来。

===Original===
In the morning he was about to climb back to the trail he had prudently left the night before when he saw five bearded, fur-clad men looking much the same as Ulffa's people. Ross hugged the earth and watched them pass out of sight before he followed.

===Chinese===
早晨他正要爬回昨夜谨慎离开的那条小径，忽然看见五个胡须浓密、身裹毛皮的男人，看上去和乌尔法的族人差不多。罗斯伏在地上，等他们走出视线后才跟了上去。

===Original===
All that day he wove an up-and-down trail behind the small band, sometimes catching sight of them as they topped a rise well ahead or stopped to eat. It was late afternoon when he crept cautiously to the top of a ridge and gazed down into a valley.

===Chinese===
整整一天他都在那支小队伍后面迂回跟踪，时而看见他们翻上远处的高坡，时而看见他们停下来进食。直到午后很晚，他才小心翼翼地爬到一道山脊顶上，俯瞰下方的山谷。

===Original===
There was a town in that valley, sturdy houses of logs behind a stockade. He had seen towns vaguely like it before, yet it had a dreamlike quality as if it were not as real as it appeared.

===Chinese===
山谷里有一座城镇，原木建造的坚固房屋环绕在栅栏之后。他以前隐约见过类似的城镇，但眼前这座有一种梦幻般的质感，仿佛并不如看上去那样真实。

===Original===
Ross rested his chin on his arms and watched that town and the people moving in it. Some were fur-clad hunters, but others dressed quite differently. He started up with a little cry at the sight of one of the men who had walked so swiftly from one house to the next; surely he was a Beaker trader!

===Chinese===
罗斯把下巴搁在手臂上，望着那座城镇和在其中走动的人。有些是穿毛皮的猎人，但另一些人的穿着截然不同。当他看见其中一个从一栋房子快步走向另一栋房子的人时，不禁轻呼一声坐了起来；那分明是一个比克尔商人！

===Original===
His unease grew stronger with every moment he watched, but it was the oddness he sensed in that town which bothered him and not any warning that he, himself, was in danger. He had gotten to his knees to see better when out of nowhere a rope sang through the air, settling about his chest with a vicious jerk which not only drove the air from his lungs but pinioned his arms tight to his body.

===Chinese===
他越看越不安，但困扰他的是那座城镇散发出的怪异气息，而非自己身处危险的任何预警。他正跪起身想要看得更清楚，一条绳索毫无征兆地破空飞来，狠狠一收便勒住了他的胸口，不仅挤出了他肺里的空气，还将他的双臂紧紧箍在了身侧。

## X

===Original===
Having been cuffed and battered into submission more quickly than would have been possible three weeks earlier, Murdock now stood sullenly surveying the man who, though he dressed like a Beaker trader, persisted in using a language Ross did not know.

===Chinese===
默多克（Murdock）挨了一顿耳光和拳脚，比三周前更容易地就被打服了，此刻正阴沉沉地打量着眼前这个人——虽然穿着比克尔商人（Beaker trader）的衣服，嘴里说的却是罗斯（Ross）听不懂的语言。

===Original===
"We do not play as children here." At last the man spoke words Ross could understand. "You will answer me or else others shall ask the questions, and less gently. I say to you now—who are you and from where do you come?"

===Chinese===
"我们这里不跟小孩子玩游戏。"那人终于说出了罗斯能听懂的话。"你要么回答我，要么换别人来问，那可就不客气了。我现在问你——你是什么人，从哪里来？"

===Original===
For a moment Ross glowered across the table at him, his inbred antagonism to authority aroused by that contemptuous demand, but then common sense cautioned. His initial introduction to this village had left him bruised and with one of his headaches. There was no reason to let them beat him until he was in no shape to make a break for freedom when and if there was an opportunity.

===Chinese===
罗斯隔着桌子怒视了对方片刻，骨子里对权威的抵触被那番轻蔑的逼问激发出来。但理智随即发出了警告。他初到这个村子时挨了打，脑袋也犯了病。没有理由让他们一直打下去，打到他连趁机逃跑的力气都没有。

===Original===
"I am Rossa of the traders," he returned, eying the man with a carefully measured stare. "I came into this land in search of my kinsmen who were taken by raiders in the night."

===Chinese===
"我是商人罗萨（Rossa），"他回敬道，用一种精心拿捏的目光盯着对方。"我来到这片土地，是为了寻找夜里被劫匪抓走的亲人。"

===Original===
The man, who sat on a stool by the table, smiled slowly. Again he spoke in the strange tongue, and Ross merely stared stolidly back. His words were short and explosive sounding, and the man's smile faded; his annoyance grew as he continued to speak.

===Chinese===
那人坐在桌旁的凳子上，缓缓露出微笑。他又用那种陌生的语言说了起来，罗斯只是面无表情地回望着他。那人的话语短促而猛烈，微笑渐渐消退；随着他继续说下去，恼怒越来越明显。

===Original===
One of Ross's two guards ventured to interrupt, using the Beaker language. "From where did you come?" He was a quiet-faced, slender man, not like his companion, who had roped Murdock from behind and was of the bully breed, able to subdue Ross's wildcat resistance in a very short struggle.

===Chinese===
罗斯的两个看守中有一个壮着胆子用比克尔语插了话。"你从哪里来？"他面容沉静，身材清瘦，不像他的同伴——那个从背后用绳索套住默多克的家伙，天生一副恶霸体格，在很短的搏斗中就把罗斯野猫般的反抗压制住了。

===Original===
"I came to this land from the south," Ross answered, "after the manner of my people. This is a new land with furs and the golden tears of the sun to be gathered and bartered. The traders move in peace, and their hands are raised against no man. Yet in the darkness there came those who would slay without profit, for what reason I have no knowing."

===Chinese===
"我从南方来到这片土地，"罗斯答道，"遵循我们民族的方式。这里有毛皮和太阳的金色泪珠——琥珀——可以采集和交易。商人们以和平方式行商，不对任何人举起武器。然而黑暗中来了那些无端杀戮之人，我不知他们意欲何为。"

===Original===
The quiet man continued the questioning and Ross answered fully with details of the past of one Rossa, a Beaker merchant. Yes, he was from the south. His father was Gurdi, who had a trading post in the warm lands along the big river. This was Rossa's first trip to open new territory. He had come with his father's blood brother, Assha, who was a noted far voyager, and it was an honor to be chosen as donkey-leader for such a one as Assha. With Assha had been Macna, one who was also a far trader, though not as noted as Assha.

===Chinese===
沉静的男人继续盘问，罗斯事无巨细地讲述了罗萨——一个比克尔商人——的身世。是的，他来自南方。他的父亲叫古尔迪（Gurdi），在大河沿岸的温暖地带有一个贸易站。这是罗萨第一次出来开辟新领地。他是跟父亲的结拜兄弟阿莎（Assha）一起来的，阿莎是一位著名的远行航海家，能被选作阿莎的驴队领队是一种荣幸。和阿莎同行的还有麦克纳（Macna），也是一位远行商人，虽然名气不如阿莎。

===Original===
Of a certainty, Assha was of his own race! Ross blinked at that question. One need only to look upon him to know that he was of trader blood and no uncivilized woodsrunner. How long had he known Assha? Ross shrugged. Assha had come to his father's post the winter before and had stayed with them through the cold season. Gurdi and Assha had mingled blood after he pulled Gurdi free from the river in flood. Assha had lost his boat and trade goods in that rescue, so Gurdi had made good his loss this year. Detail by detail he gave the story. In spite of the fact that he provided these details glibly, sure that they were true, Ross continued to be haunted by an odd feeling that he was indeed reciting a tale of adventure which had happened long ago and to someone else. Perhaps that pain in his head made him think of these events as very colorless and far away.

===Chinese===
阿莎当然是自己人！罗斯听到这个问题眨了眨眼。只要看他一眼就知道他是商人血统，不是什么未开化的林中蛮人。他认识阿莎多久了？罗斯耸耸肩。阿莎去年冬天来到他父亲的贸易站，在那里度过了整个寒冬。古尔迪和阿莎在洪水暴发时结下了生死之交——阿莎把古尔迪从暴涨的河水中拉了出来，自己的船和货物都在那次救援中损失了，所以古尔迪今年赔偿了他的损失。他一五一十地讲述着。尽管这些细节他说得滔滔不绝，深信都是真的，罗斯却始终被一种奇怪的感觉缠绕着——他确实是在复述一段很久以前发生在别人身上的冒险故事。也许是头上的疼痛让他觉得这些事件显得苍白而遥远。

===Original===
"It would seem"—the quiet man turned to the one behind the table—"that this is indeed one Rossa, a Beaker trader."

===Chinese===
"看来，"沉静的男人转向桌子后面的那人说，"此人确实是罗萨，一个比克尔商人。"

===Original===
But the man looked impatient, angry. He made a sign to the other guard, who turned Ross around roughly and sent him toward the door with a push. Once again the leader gave an order in his own language, adding a few words more with a stinging snap that might have been a threat or a warning.

===Chinese===
但那人一脸不耐烦，满面怒容。他向另一个看守打了个手势，那人粗暴地把罗斯转了个身，一把推向门口。头领又用自己的语言下了道命令，末尾加了几个字，带着刺耳的锐利语气，像是威胁或警告。

===Original===
Ross was thrust into a small room with a hard floor and not even a skin rug to serve as a bed. Since the quiet man had ordered the removal of the ropes from Ross's arms, he leaned against the wall, rubbing the pain of returning circulation away from his wrists and trying to understand what had happened to him and where he was. Having spied upon it from the heights, he knew it wasn't an ordinary trading station, and he wanted to know what they did here. Also, somewhere in this village he hoped to find Assha and Macna.

===Chinese===
罗斯被推进一间小屋，地面硬邦邦的，连一张兽皮毯子都没有当床用。沉静的男人下令解掉了他胳膊上的绳索，他靠在墙上，揉着麻木的手腕以缓解血液回流的疼痛，同时试图弄清楚自己遭遇了什么、身在何处。他从高处侦察过这里，知道这不是一个普通的贸易站，他想知道他们在这里做什么。而且，他希望在这座村庄的某个角落能找到阿莎和麦克纳。

===Original===
At the end of the day his captors opened the door only long enough to push inside a bowl and a small jug. He felt for those in the dusk, dipping his fingers into a lukewarm mush of meal and drinking the water from the jug avidly. His headache dulled, and from experience Ross knew that this bout was almost over. If he slept, he would waken with a clearer mind and no pain. Knowing he was very tired, he took the precaution of curling up directly in front of the door so that no one could enter without arousing him.

===Chinese===
这一天结束时，看守只开了一下门，塞进一只碗和一个小水壶。他在暮色中摸索到那些东西，把手指伸进温热的面糊里，贪婪地喝着壶里的水。头痛缓和了些，凭经验罗斯知道这一轮快过去了。睡一觉的话，醒来时头脑会更清醒，疼痛也会消失。知道自已非常疲惫，他特意蜷缩在门前——这样谁要进来都得先惊动他。

===Original===
It was still dark when he awoke with a curious urgency remaining from a dream he could not remember. Ross sat up, flexing his arms and shoulders to combat the stiffness which had come with his cramped sleep. He could not rid himself of a feeling that there was something to be done and that time was his enemy.

===Chinese===
他醒来时天还黑着，一场记不清的梦留下了一股莫名的紧迫感。罗斯坐起来，活动手臂和肩膀以对抗蜷缩入睡带来的僵硬感。他怎么也摆脱不了一种感觉——有些事必须去做，而时间是他最大的敌人。

===Original===
Assha! Gratefully he seized on that. He must find Assha and Macna, for the three of them could surely discover a way to get out of this village. That was what was so important!

===Chinese===
阿莎！他感激地抓住了这个念头。他必须找到阿莎和麦克纳，三个人一定能想出办法逃出这个村子。这才是最重要的事！

===Original===
He had been handled none too gently, and they were holding him a prisoner. But Ross believed that this was not the worst which could happen to him here, and he must be free before the worst did come. The question was, How could he escape? His bow and dagger were gone, and he did not even have his long cloak pin for a weapon, since he had given that to Frigga.

===Chinese===
他被对待得一点也不客气，他们把他关了起来。但罗斯相信这还不是最坏的情况，在最坏的事发生之前他必须获得自由。问题是——他怎么逃跑？他的弓和匕首都不在了，连那根长斗篷别针也没有当武器，因为他把它送给了弗里嘉（Frigga）。

===Original===
Running his hands over his body, Ross inventoried what remained of his clothing and possessions. He unfastened the bronze chain-belt still buckled in his kilt tunic, swinging the length speculatively in one hand. A masterpiece of craftsmanship, it consisted of patterned plates linked together with a series of five finely wrought chains and a front buckle in the form of a lion's head, its protruding tongue serving as a hook to support a dagger sheath. Its weight promised a weapon of sorts, which when added to the element of surprise might free him.

===Chinese===
罗斯用手在身上摸索，清点还剩什么衣物和随身物品。他解开了仍扣在短裙束腰上的青铜链腰带，若有所思地拎在手里掂了掂分量。这是一件工艺杰作——由雕花的铜板通过五条精工细作的链子串联而成，前方的扣头是一个狮头造型，突出的舌头充当挂匕首鞘的钩子。它的分量足以当一种武器，再加上出其不意的因素，或许能帮他脱身。

===Original===
By rights they would be expecting him to produce some opposition, however. It was well known that only the best fighters, the shrewdest minds, followed the traders' roads. It was a proud thing to be a trader in the wilderness, a thought that warmed Ross now as he waited in the dark for what luck and Ba-Bal of the Bright Horns would send. Were he ever to return to Gurdi's post, Ba-Bal, whose boat rode across the sky from dawn to dusk, would have a fine ox, jars of the first brewing, and sweet-smelling amber laid upon his altar.

===Chinese===
不过按常理，他们应该料到他会反抗。众所周知，只有最出色的战士和最精明的头脑才会走商人的路。在荒野中做一名商人是值得骄傲的事，这个念头在此刻暗中等待运气和光角巴巴尔（Ba-Bal of the Bright Horns）降下什么安排时，温暖着罗斯的心。如果他能回到古尔迪的贸易站，巴巴尔——他那从黎明到黄昏横渡天空的神舟的主人——将得到一头上好的公牛、几罐新酿的好酒和芳香的琥珀作为供品。

===Original===
Ross had patience which he had learned from the mixed heritage of his two pasts, the real and the false graft. He could wait as he had waited many times before—quiet, and with outward ease—for the right moment to come. It came now with footsteps ringing sharply, halting before his cell door.

===Chinese===
罗斯有耐心，这耐心来自他双重过去的混合遗产——真实的与嫁接的。他能像以前多次做过的那样等待——安静地、外表从容地——等待正确的时机到来。此刻时机来了，伴随着清脆的脚步声，在牢房门前停住。

===Original===
With the noiseless speed of a hunting cat, Ross flung himself from behind the door to a wall, where he would be hidden from the newcomer for that necessary instant or two. If his attack was to be successful, it must occur inside the room. He heard the sound of a bar being slid out of its brackets, and he poised himself, the belt rippling from his right hand.

===Chinese===
罗斯像猎猫一样无声而敏捷地从门后闪到墙边，在新来者进门后那一两秒必要的瞬间里藏好身形。如果突袭要成功，就必须在房间里完成。他听到门闩被抽出卡槽的声音，全身蓄势待发，右手握着的腰带已经甩动起来。

===Original===
The door was opening inward, and a man stood silhouetted against the outer light. He muttered, looking toward the corner where Ross had thrown his single garment in a roll which might just resemble, for the needed second or two, a man curled in slumber. The man in the doorway took the bait, coming forward far enough for Ross to send the door slamming shut as he himself sprang with the belt aimed for the other's head.

===Chinese===
门朝里打开了，一个男人的身形映在门外的光线中。他嘟囔着，朝角落看去——罗斯把唯一的衣物卷成一团扔在那里，在需要的那一两秒钟里看起来就像一个蜷缩沉睡的人。门口那人上了当，向前走了足够远，罗斯猛地把门甩上，同时挥起腰带朝对方的脑袋砸去。

===Original===
There was a startled cry, cut off in the middle as the belt plates met flesh and bone in a crushing force. Luck was with him! Ross caught up his kilt and belted it around him after he had made a hurried examination of the body now lying at his feet. He was not sure that the man was dead, but at any rate he was completely unconscious. Ross stripped off the man's cloak, located his dagger, freed it from the belt hook, and snapped it on his own.

===Chinese===
一声惊叫响起，中途戛然而止——腰带上的铜片以碾压般的力量砸中了皮肉和骨头。运气站在我这边！罗斯匆匆检查了一下倒在地上的身体，不确定那人是否已死，但至少完全失去了知觉。他捡起短裙系好，剥下那人的斗篷，找到他的匕首，从腰带钩上取下来，别到了自己身上。

===Original===
Then inch by inch Ross edged open the door, peering through the crack. As far as he could see, the hall was empty, so he jerked the portal open, and dagger in hand, sprang out, ready for attack. He closed the door, slipping the bar back into its brackets. If the man inside revived and pounded for attention, his own friends might think it was Ross and delay investigating.

===Chinese===
然后罗斯一寸一寸地推开房门，从门缝向外窥探。目力所及，走廊空无一人，他猛地拉开门，手持匕首跳了出来，准备迎战。他关上门，把门闩插回卡槽。如果里面那人醒过来敲门求救，他的同伴可能以为是罗斯在闹，从而拖延来查看的时间。

===Original===
But the escape from the cell was the easiest part of what he planned to do, as Ross well knew. To find Assha and Macna in this maze of rooms occupied by the enemy was far more difficult. Although he had no idea in which of the village buildings they might be confined, this one was the largest and seemed to be the headquarters of the chief men, which meant it could also serve as their prison.

===Chinese===
但逃出牢房不过是他计划中最容易的一步，罗斯心里清楚。在这座敌占区房间迷宫里找到阿莎和麦克纳要困难得多。他虽然不知道他们被关在村中哪栋建筑里，但这栋是最大的，看起来像是头领们的总部，也就意味着它同样可以充当监狱。

===Original===
Light came from a torch in a bracket halfway down the hall. The wood burned smokily, giving off a resinous odor, and to Ross the glow was sufficient illumination. He slipped along as close to the wall as he could, ready to freeze at the slightest sound. But this portion of the building might well have been deserted, for he saw or heard no one. He tried the only two doors opening out of the hall, but they were secured on the other side. Then he came to a bend in the corridor, and stopped short, hearing a murmur of low voices.

===Chinese===
走廊中段的一只支架上插着一支火把，提供着光源。木头燃烧时烟雾缭绕，散发出松脂的气味，但对罗斯来说这光亮已足够照明。他紧贴墙壁潜行，一有响动就准备立刻冻住不动。但这部分建筑似乎已经被弃用了，他没有看到也没有听到任何人。他试了试走廊上仅有的两扇门，但都从里面锁死了。接着他来到走廊的一个拐角，猛地停住了脚步——他听到了压低的说话声。

===Original===
If he had used a hunter's tricks of silent tread and vigilant wariness before, Ross was doubly on guard now as he wriggled to a point from which he could see beyond that turn. Mere luck prevented him from giving himself away a moment later.

===Chinese===
如果说之前他用过猎人的无声脚步和警觉的谨慎，那么现在罗斯加倍警戒，匍匐到能看到拐弯处另一侧的位置。纯粹是运气才让他没有在片刻之后暴露自己。

===Original===
Assha! Assha, alive, well, apparently under no restraint, was just turning away from the same quiet man who had had a part in Ross's interrogation. That was surely Assha's brown hair, his slender wiry body draped with a Beaker's kilt. A familiar tilt of the head convinced Ross, though he could not see the man's face. The quiet man went down the hall, leaving Assha before a door. As he passed through it Ross sped forward and followed him inside.

===Chinese===
阿莎！阿莎，活着，安好，显然没有被捆绑，正从那个参与过罗斯审讯的沉静男人身边转身离开。那棕色的头发，那精瘦有力的身躯裹着比克尔人的短裙——毫无疑问就是阿莎。一个熟悉的偏头角度让罗斯确信了，尽管他看不到那人的脸。沉静的男人沿着走廊走远了，把阿莎留在一扇门前。阿莎推门进去的瞬间，罗斯飞速冲上前跟了进去。

===Original===
Assha had crossed the bare room and was standing on a glowing plate in the floor. Ross, aroused to desperate action by some fear he did not understand, leaped after him. His left hand fell upon Assha's shoulder, turning the man half around as Ross, too, stepped upon the patch of luminescence.

===Chinese===
阿莎穿过空荡荡的房间，站在了地板上一块发光的板面上。罗斯被某种自己也说不清的恐惧驱使着，拼死扑了过去。他的左手抓住阿莎的肩膀，将人扳转了半圈，同时自己也踏上了那片发出荧光的地方。

===Original===
Murdock had only an instant to realize that he was staring into the face of an astonished stranger. His hand flashed up in an edgewise blow which caught the other on the side of the throat, and then the world came apart about them. There was a churning, whirling sickness which griped and bent Ross almost double across the crumpled body of his victim. He held his head lest it be torn from his shoulders by the spinning thing which seemed based behind his eyes.

===Chinese===
默多克只来得及一瞬意识到自己正瞪着一张全然陌生的、同样惊骇的面孔。他的手闪电般挥出一记横劈，击中对方的脖颈侧面，接着整个世界在他们周围碎裂了。一阵翻搅旋转的眩晕袭来，恶心感揪住了罗斯，他弯着腰几乎折成两段，扑倒在那人瘫软的身体上。他抱住脑袋，生怕被那个似乎扎根在他眼窝后面的旋转物从肩膀上扯下来。

===Original===
The sickness endured only for a moment, and some buried part of Ross's mind accepted it as a phenomenon he had experienced before. He came out of it gasping, to focus his attention once more on the man at his feet.

===Chinese===
眩晕只持续了一瞬，罗斯意识深处某个被掩埋的部分将此判定为他此前经历过的现象。他大口喘着气回过神来，注意力重新聚焦到脚下那个人身上。

===Original===
The stranger was still breathing. Ross stooped to drag him from the plate and began binding and gagging him with lengths torn from his kilt. Only when his captive was secure did he begin looking around him curiously.

===Chinese===
那个陌生人还在呼吸。罗斯弯腰把他从板面上拖下来，从自己的短裙上撕下布条，将他捆绑堵嘴。直到俘虏被牢牢制服，他才开始好奇地打量四周。

===Original===
The room was bare of any furnishings and now, as he glanced at the floor, Ross saw that the plate had lost its glow. The Beaker trader Rossa rubbed sweating palms on his kilt and thought fleetingly of forest ghosts and other mysteries. Not that the traders bowed to those ghosts which were the plague of lesser men and tribes, but anything which suddenly appeared and then disappeared without any logical explanation, needed thinking on. Murdock pulled the prisoner, who was now reviving, to the far end of the room and then went back to the plate with the persistence of a man who refused to treat with ghosts and wanted something concrete to explain the unexplainable. Though he rubbed his hands across the smooth surface of the plate, it did not light up again.

===Chinese===
房间里没有任何家具，此刻罗斯低头看向地面，发现那块板面已经失去了荧光。比克尔商人罗萨在短裙上擦了擦汗湿的手掌，脑中一闪而过关于森林幽灵和其他神秘事物的念头。倒不是说商人们会向那些骚扰凡人和部落的幽灵低头，但任何突然出现又凭空消失、找不到合理解释的东西，都值得好好琢磨。默多克把那个正在苏醒的俘虏拖到房间另一头，然后回到板面前——他固执得像一个不肯跟鬼打交道的人，非要找到具体的东西来解释这无法解释的现象。尽管他用手掌在板面上反复摩擦，它再也没有亮起来。

===Original===
His captive having writhed himself half out of the corner of the room, Ross debated the wisdom of another silencing—say a tap on the skull with the heavy hilt of his dagger. Deciding against it because he might need a guide, he freed the victim's ankle bonds and pulled him to his feet, holding the dagger ready where the man could see it. Were there any more surprises to be encountered in this place, Assha's double would test them first.

===Chinese===
俘虏已经在角落里扭动着半个身子挣出来了，罗斯考虑着是否应该再让他安静一下——比如用匕首沉重的柄朝他脑袋上来一下。最后他否决了这个想法，因为可能需要一个向导。他解开那人脚踝上的绳索，把他拉起来，匕首举在对方看得见的地方。如果这地方还有别的陷阱，阿莎的替身先去趟。

===Original===
The door did not lead to the same corridor, or even the same kind of corridor Ross had passed through moments earlier. Instead they entered a short passage with walls of some smooth stuff which had almost the sheen of polished metal and were sleek and cold to the touch. In fact, the whole place was chill, chill as river water in the spring.

===Chinese===
这扇门通向的不是之前那条走廊，甚至不是同一类走廊。他们进入了一段短通道，墙壁由某种光滑的材料构成，几乎有打磨金属般的光泽，触感滑腻冰凉。事实上，整个地方都冷飕飕的，冷得像春天的河水。

===Original===
Still herding the prisoner before him, Ross came to the nearest door and looked within, to be faced by incomprehensible frames of metal rods and boxes. Rossa of the traders marveled and stared, but again, he realized that what he saw was not altogether strange. Part of one wall was a board on which small lights flashed and died, to flash again in winks of bright color. A mysterious object made of wire and disks hung across the back of a chair standing nearby.

===Chinese===
罗斯继续押着俘虏前行，来到最近的一扇门前朝里张望，映入眼帘的是无法理解的金属杆框架和箱体。商人罗萨惊叹地瞪大了眼睛，但又一次，他意识到眼前所见并非全然陌生。一面墙上有一块板子，上面的小灯闪烁明灭，以鲜艳的色彩频频闪动。附近一把椅子的靠背上挂着一件由金属线和圆盘制成的神秘物件。

===Original===
The bound man lurched for the chair and fell, rolling toward the wall. Ross pushed him on until he was hidden behind one of the metal boxes. Then he made the rounds of the room, touching nothing, but studying what he could not understand. Puffs of warm air came in through grills near the floor, but the room had the same general chill as the hall outside.

===Chinese===
被绑着的人踉跄着扑向椅子，摔倒后朝墙边滚去。罗斯把他推到一个金属箱后面藏好。然后他在房间里转了一圈，什么也不碰，只是仔细打量那些他看不懂的东西。地板附近的格栅里冒出一股股暖风，但房间整体和外面的走廊一样阴冷。

===Original===
Meanwhile the lights on the board had become more active, flashing on and off in complex patterns. Ross now heard a buzzing, as if a swarm of angry insects were gathered for an attack. Crouching beside his captive, Ross watched the lights, trying to discover the source of the sound.

===Chinese===
与此同时，板子上的灯光变得更加活跃，以复杂的图案明灭闪烁。罗斯此时听到了一阵嗡鸣，像是一群愤怒的昆虫正在集结准备进攻。他蹲在俘虏旁边，盯着灯光，试图找出声音的来源。

===Original===
The buzz grew shriller, almost demanding. Ross heard the tramp of heavy footgear in the corridor, and a man entered the room, crossing purposefully to the chair. He sat down and drew the wire-and-disk frame over his head. His hands moved under the lights, but Ross could not guess what he was doing.

===Chinese===
嗡鸣越来越尖锐，几乎像是在催促。罗斯听到走廊里传来沉重的靴子踏地声，一个男人走进房间，径直走到椅子前坐下，把那个金属线和圆盘制成的框架罩到头上。他的手在灯光下移动，但罗斯猜不出他在做什么。

===Original===
The captive at Murdock's side tried to stir, but Ross's hand pinned him quiet. The shrill noise which had originally summoned the man at the lights was interrupted by a sharp pattern of long-and-short sounds, and his hands flew even more quickly while Ross took in every detail of the other's clothing and equipment. He was neither a shaggy tribesman nor a trader. He wore a dull-green outer garment cut in one piece to cover his arms and legs as well as his body, and his hair was so short that his round skull might have been shaven. Ross rubbed the back of his wrist across his eyes, experiencing again that dim other memory. Odd as this man looked, Murdock had seen his like before somewhere, yet the background had not been Gurdi's post on the southern river. Where and when had he, Rossa, ever been with such strange beings? And why could he not remember it all more clearly?

===Chinese===
默多克身边的俘虏试图动弹，但罗斯的手牢牢按住了他。原本召唤板前那人的尖锐噪音被一阵长短交替的急促信号打断了，那人的手在灯光下移动得更快了。罗斯将对方的衣着和装备的每一个细节都看在眼里。他既不是蓬头散发的部落民，也不是商人。他穿着一件暗绿色的连体外衣，一体裁剪覆盖了手臂、腿和躯干；头发剃得极短，浑圆的脑袋看上去像是刮过的。罗斯用手腕背擦了擦眼睛，那种朦胧的"另一个记忆"再次袭来。尽管这人的样子很古怪，默多克以前在什么地方见过同类，但背景不是南方河边古尔迪的贸易站。他——罗萨——究竟在何时何地与这样奇怪的人打过交道？为什么他不能把这些记得更清楚些？

===Original===
Boots sounded once more in the hall, and another figure strode in. This one wore furs, but he, too, was no woods hunter, Ross realized as he studied the newcomer in detail. The loose overshirt of thick fur with its hood thrown back, the high boots, and all the rest were not of any primitive fashioning. And the man had four eyes! One pair were placed normally on either side of his nose, and the other two, black-rimmed and murky, were set above on his forehead.

===Chinese===
走廊里又传来靴子声，另一个身影大步走进来。这人穿着毛皮，但罗斯仔细打量后意识到他也绝非林中猎人。宽松的厚毛皮罩衫兜帽翻在脑后，高筒靴子，所有的一切都不是任何原始工艺能做出来的。而且这人有四只眼睛！一双正常地长在鼻子两侧，另一双黑边浑浊的则长在额头上方。

===Original===
The fur-clad man tapped the one seated at the board. He freed his head partially from the wire cage so that they could talk together in a strange language while lights continued to flash and the buzzing died away. Ross's captive wriggled with renewed vigor and at last thrashed free a foot to kick at one of the metal installations. The resulting clang brought both men around. The one at the board tore his head cage off as he jumped to his feet, while the other brought out a gun.

===Chinese===
穿毛皮的人拍了拍坐在板前的那人。后者把头从金属线笼中半解放出来，两人用一种陌生的语言交谈起来，与此同时灯光继续闪烁，嗡鸣声逐渐消失。罗斯的俘虏又开始拼命挣扎，终于挣脱出一只脚，踢在一件金属设备上。金属碰撞声让两人同时转过身来。坐在板前的人猛地扯掉头上的金属笼跳起来，另一个则掏出了一把枪。

===Original===
Gun? One little fraction of Ross's mind wondered at his recognition of that black thing and of the danger it promised, even as he prepared for battle. He pushed his captive across the path of the man in fur and threw himself in the other direction. There was a blast to make a torment in his head as he hurled toward the door.

===Chinese===
枪？罗斯意识的一小角在惊讶自己竟然认出了那个黑色物体及其预示的危险，同时他已经准备好了战斗。他把俘虏推向穿毛皮之人的必经之路，自己朝另一个方向扑去。他冲向门口时，一声爆响在脑中炸开了剧痛。

===Original===
So intent was Ross upon escape that he did not glance behind but skidded out on his hands and knees, thus fortunately presenting a poor target to the third man coming down the hall. Ross's shoulder hit the newcomer at thigh level, and they tangled in a struggling mass which saved Ross's life as the others burst out behind them.

===Chinese===
罗斯一门心思只想逃跑，没有回头看，手脚并用地滑了出去——这一来倒幸运地让走廊上迎面而来的第三个人难以瞄准。罗斯的肩膀撞上新来者的大腿高度，两人纠缠成一团扭打在一起，这救了罗斯的命——因为其他人紧跟着冲了出来。

===Original===
Ross fought grimly, his hands and feet moving in blows he was not conscious of planning. His opponent was no easy match and at last Ross was flattened, in spite of his desperate efforts. He was whirled over, his arms jerked behind him, and cold metal rings snapped about his wrists. Then he was rolled back, to lie blinking up at his enemies.

===Chinese===
罗斯拼命搏斗，手脚不由自主地挥击着他根本来不及计划的动作。对手并不好对付，尽管罗斯拼尽全力，最终还是被撂倒了。他被翻了过去，双臂被扭到身后，冰冷的金属环扣上了他的手腕。然后他被翻回来，躺在那里眨着眼仰望敌人。

===Original===
All three men gathered over him, barking questions which he could not understand. One of them disappeared and returned with Ross's former captive, his mouth a straight line and a light in his eyes Ross understood far better than words.

===Chinese===
三个男人围在他上方，连珠炮似的抛出他听不懂的问题。其中一个走开了，带着罗斯先前的俘虏回来——那人嘴唇抿成一条线，眼神里的光芒罗斯比任何话语都更读懂了。

===Original===
"You are the trader prisoner?" The man who looked like Assha leaned over Murdock, patches of red on his tanned skin where the gag and wrist bonds had been.

===Chinese===
"你就是那个商人俘虏？"那个长得像阿莎的人俯视着默多克，被堵嘴和绑手腕的地方在晒黑的皮肤上留下了红印。

===Original===
"I am Rossa, son of Gurdi, of the traders," Ross returned, meeting what he read in the other's expression with a ready defiance. "I was a prisoner, yes. But you did not keep me one for long then, nor shall you now."

===Chinese===
"我是古尔迪之子罗萨，商人，"罗斯回敬道，以现成的强硬态度迎上对方表情中读出的东西。"我以前是俘虏，没错。但你们当时没能关住我太久，现在也一样关不住。"

===Original===
The man's thin upper lip lifted. "You have done yourself ill, my young friend. We have a better prison here for you, one from which you shall not escape."

===Chinese===
那人薄薄的上唇向上掀起。"你这是自讨苦吃，年轻人。我们这里有一座更好的牢房给你，你逃不出去的。"

===Original===
He spoke to the other men, and there was the ring of an order in his voice. They pulled Ross to his feet, pushing him ahead of them. During the short march Ross used his eyes, noticing things he could not identify in the rooms through which they passed. Men called questions and at last they paused long enough, Ross firmly in the hold of the fur-clad guard, for the other two to put on similar garments.

===Chinese===
他向另外两人说了什么，语气里带着命令的威严。他们把罗斯拉起来，推着他往前走。在这段短促的行进中，罗斯用眼睛扫视四周，留意到经过的房间里那些他辨认不出的东西。有人高声询问着什么，最后他们停顿了足够长的时间——罗斯被穿毛皮的看守牢牢抓住——好让另外两人也穿上类似的衣物。

===Original===
Ross had lost his cloak in the fight, but no fur shirt was given him. He shivered more and more as the chill which clung to that warren of rooms and halls bit into his half-clad body. He was certain of only one thing about this place; he could not possibly be in the crude buildings of the valley village. However, he was unable to guess where he was and how he had come there.

===Chinese===
罗斯在搏斗中丢了斗篷，但没有人给他一件毛皮衬衫。盘踞在这座房间走廊迷宫里的寒气咬噬着他半裸的身体，他越抖越厉害。关于这个地方他唯一能确定的只有一件事——他不可能还在山谷村落那些粗糙的建筑里。然而，他无法猜出自己身在何处，也无法推测是怎么来到这里的。

===Original===
Finally, they went down a narrow room filled with bulky metal objects of bright scarlet or violet that gleamed weirdly and were equipped with rods along which all the colors of the rainbow ringed. Here was a round door, and when one of the guards used both hands to tug it open, the cold that swept in at them was a frigid breath that burned as it touched bare skin.

===Chinese===
最后，他们进入了一个狭窄的房间，里面堆满了亮猩红色或紫色的笨重金属物件，散发着诡异的光泽，上面装着环绕彩虹全部色彩的杆状物。这里有一扇圆形的门，当一个看守双手用力拉开它时，灌进来的寒气如同一股冰冷的呼吸，碰到裸露的皮肤便灼烧般刺痛。

## XI

===Original===
It took Ross a while to learn that the dirty-white walls of this tunnel which were almost entirely opaque, with dark objects showing dimly through them here and there, were of solid ice. A black wire was hooked overhead and at regular intervals hung with lights which did nothing to break the sensation of glacial cold about them.

Ross shuddered. Every breath he drew stung in his lungs; his bare shoulders and arms and the exposed section of thigh between kilt and boot were numb. He could only move on stiffly, pushed ahead by his guards when he faltered. He guessed that were he to lose his footing here and surrender to the cold, he would forfeit the battle entirely and with it his life.

He had no way of measuring the length of the boring through the solid ice, but they were at last fronted by another opening, a ragged one which might have been hacked with an ax. They emerged from it into the wildest scene Ross had ever seen. Of course, he was familiar with ice and snow, but here was a world surrendered completely to the brutal force of winter in a strange, abnormal way. It was a still, dead white-gray world in which nothing moved save the wind which curled the drifts.

===Chinese===
罗斯（Ross）花了一段时间才明白，这条隧道那脏白色、几乎完全不透光的墙壁——暗处隐约透出几处黑影——是实心的冰。头顶上方挂着一根黑色电线，每隔一段吊着几盏灯，却丝毫驱不散周身那刺骨的寒意。

罗斯打了个寒颤。每次呼吸都刺痛肺腑；裸露的肩膀、手臂，以及短裙与靴子之间露出的大腿部分都已麻木。他只能僵硬地往前挪，看守在他踉跄时推上一把。他猜想，如果此刻失足跌倒、向寒冷投降，他将输掉整场战斗，连同自己的性命。

他无法丈量在实心冰层中穿行的长度，但最终面前出现了一个新的洞口，参差不齐，像是用斧头劈开的。他们从那里走出来，进入了罗斯此生见过的最荒凉的景象。当然，他对冰雪并不陌生，但这里是一个完全被冬日的蛮暴力量以一种奇异、反常的方式吞噬的世界。一片死寂的灰白世界，除了卷起雪堆的风，什么也不动。

===Original===
His guards covered their eyes with the murky lenses they had worn pushed up on their foreheads within the shelter, for above them sunlight dazzled on the ice crest. Ross, his eyes smarting, kept his gaze centered on his feet. He was given no time to look about. A rope was produced, a loop of it flipped in a noose about his throat, and he was towed along like a leashed dog. Before them was a path worn in the snow, not only by the passing of booted feet, but with more deeply scored marks as if heavy objects had been sledded there. Ross slipped and stumbled in the ruts, fearing to fall lest he be dragged. The numbness of his body reached into his head. He was dizzy, the world about him misting over now and again with a haze which arose from the long stretches of unbroken snow fields.

Tripping in a rut, he went down upon one knee, his flesh too numbed now to feel the additional cold of the snow, snow so hard that its crust delivered a knife’s cut. Unemotionally, he watched a thin line of red trickle in a sluggish drop or two down the blue skin of his leg. The rope jerked him forward, and Ross scrambled awkwardly until one of his captors hooked a fur mitten in his belt and heaved him to his feet once more.

The purpose of that trek through the snow was obscure to Ross. In fact, he no longer cared, save that a hard rebel core deep inside him would not let him give up as long as his legs could move and he had a scrap of conscious will left in him. It was more difficult to walk now. He skidded and went down twice more. Then, the last time he slipped, he sledded past the man who led him, sliding down the slope of a glass-slick slope. He lay at the foot, unable to get up. Through the haze and deadening blanket of the cold he knew that he was being pulled about, shaken, generally mishandled; but this time he could not respond. Someone snapped open the rings about his wrists.

===Chinese===
看守在庇护所里时把浑浊的护目镜推在额头上，此刻他们把它拉下来遮住眼睛，因为上方的阳光在冰脊上耀眼地闪烁。罗斯眼睛刺痛，只能盯着自己的脚看。他没有四下张望的时间。一根绳子被掏出来，绳套往他脖子上一套，他就这样像一条拴着绳的狗一样被拖着走。前面是一条被踩出来的雪路，不仅有靴子踩过的痕迹，还有更深的犁痕，仿佛有重物在此被雪橇拖过。罗斯在车辙里滑倒、绊倒，生怕摔下去会被拖行。他身体的麻木已经蔓延到头部。他头昏眼花，四周的世界不时被长距离不间断的雪原升起的雾霭遮蔽。

脚被车辙绊住，他单膝跪倒在地，皮肤已经麻木得感觉不到雪额外的冰寒，雪面硬得像刀割。他面无表情地看着一道细细的红线从青紫的皮肤上缓缓淌下一两滴血珠。绳子猛地拽他往前，罗斯狼狈地挣扎着，直到一个看守用毛皮手套钩住他的腰带，又把他拽了起来。

穿越雪地跋涉的目的，罗斯一无所知。事实上，他已不再在乎，只是内心深处那颗坚硬的反叛核心不允许他放弃——只要他的腿还能动，只要他还有一丝清醒的意志。行走变得更加困难。他又滑倒了两次。最后一次滑倒时，他沿着一条玻璃般光滑的斜坡从带路人身边滑了下去。他躺在坡底，无法起身。透过朦胧的意识和寒冷的麻木屏障，他知道自己正在被拖来拖去、摇晃、粗暴对待；但这一次他无力反抗。有人打开了他手腕上的铐环。

===Original===
There was a call, echoing eerily across the ice. The fumbling about his body changed to a tugging and once more he was sent rolling down the slope. But the rope was now gone from his throat, and his arms were free. This time when he brought up hard against an obstruction he was not followed.

Ross’s conscious mind—that portion of him that was Rossa, the trader—was content to lie there, to yield to the lethargy born of the frigid world about him. But the subconscious Ross Murdock of the Project prodded at him. He had always had a certain cold hatred which could crystalize and become a spur. Once it had been hatred of circumstances and authority; now it became hatred for those who had led him into this wilderness with the purpose, as he knew now, of leaving him to freeze and die.

Ross pulled his hands under him. Though there was no feeling in them, they obeyed his will clumsily. He levered himself up and looked around. He lay in a narrow crevicelike cut, partly walled in by earth so frozen as to resemble steel. Crusted over it in long streaks from above were tongues of ice. To remain here was to serve his captors’ purpose.

Ross inched his way to his feet. This opening, which was intended as his grave, was not so deep as the men had thought it in their haste to be rid of him. He believed that he could climb out if he could make his body answer to his determination.

===Chinese===
一声呼唤在冰原上怪异地回荡。在他身上的摸索变成了推搡，他再次被推下斜坡。但绳子已从脖子上取下，双臂也自由了。这一次撞上障碍物后，身后没有人再追上来。

罗斯的意识——那个罗萨（Rossa），比克尔商人（Beaker trader）的部分——乐于躺在这里，屈服于极寒世界带来的昏沉。但潜意识中的项目特工罗斯·默多克（Ross Murdock）推了他一把。他一直有一种冷酷的仇恨，可以凝结成鞭策的力量。从前是对环境和权威的仇恨；现在变成了对那些把他引入荒野的人的仇恨——正如他现在所知，他们的目的是把他丢在这里冻死。

罗斯把手缩到身下。尽管毫无知觉，手却笨拙地服从了他的意志。他撑起身子环顾四周。他躺在一条狭长的裂缝状凹地里，一侧是冻得像钢一样坚硬的岩壁。上方长长地垂着几道冰舌。留在这里，就是遂了看守们的心愿。

罗斯一寸一寸地站了起来。这处本该成为他坟墓的开口，并没有那些急于摆脱他的人所想的那么深。他相信，只要身体能听从意志的支配，他就能爬出去。

===Original===
Somehow Ross made that supreme effort and came again to the rutted path from which they had tumbled him. Even if he could, there was no sense in going along that rutred trail, for it led back to the ice-encased building from which he had been brought. They had thrust him out to die; they would not take him in.

But a road so well marked must have some goal, and in hopes that he might find shelter at the other end, Ross turned to the left. The trace continued down the slope. Now the towering walls of ice and snow were broken by rocky teeth as if they had bitten deep upon this land, only to be gnawed in return. Rounding one of those rock fangs, Ross looked at a stretch of level ground. Snow lay here, but the beaten-down trail led straight through it to the rounded side of a huge globe half buried in the ground, a globe of dark material which could only be man-made.

Ross was past caution. He must get to warmth and shelter or he was done for, and he knew it. Wavering and weaving, he went on, his attention fixed on the door ahead—a closed oval door. With a sob of exhausted effort, Ross threw himself against it. The barrier gave, letting him fall forward into a queer glimmering radiance of bluish light.

===Chinese===
罗斯竭尽全力，终于爬回了那条他们推他下来的车辙路。即便能走，沿着这条路也没意义——它通回那座冰封的建筑，就是他们把他带来的地方。他们把他推出来送死；不会把他再收回去。

但一条如此清晰的路必定通向某个终点。罗斯抱着在另一端找到庇护所的希望，转身向左。痕迹沿斜坡向下延伸。此刻，高耸的冰墙被嶙峋的岩石断开，仿佛曾深深咬入这片土地，又被反噬回来。绕过一座岩石尖刺，罗斯看到了一片平坦的地面。这里覆盖着积雪，但踩实的小路直通前方，通向一个半埋在地下的巨大球体的圆弧侧面——那球体是深色材质，只能是人造的。

罗斯已顾不上警惕。他必须找到温暖和庇护，否则就完了，他很清楚这一点。他踉踉跄跄地往前走，目光紧紧锁住前方那扇门——一扇紧闭的椭圆形门。罗斯发出一声力竭的呜咽，把自己撞向门扉。门开了，他向前跌入一片泛着微光的幽蓝光芒之中。

===Original===
The light rousing him because it promised more, he crawled on past another door which was flattened back against the inner wall. It was like making one’s way down a tube. Ross paused, pressing his lifeless hands against his bare chest under the edge of his tunic, suddenly realizing that there was warmth here. His breath did not puff out in frosty streamers before him, nor did the air sear his lungs when he ventured to draw in more than shallow gulps.

With that realization a measure of animal caution returned to him. To remain where he was, just inside the entrance, was to court disaster. He must find a hiding place before he collapsed, for he sensed he was very near the end of his ability to struggle. Hope had given him a flash of false strength, the impetus to move, and he must make the most of that gift.

His path ended at a wide ladder, coiling in slow curves into gloom below and shadows above. He sensed that he was in a building of some size. He was afraid to go down, for even looking in that direction almost finished his sense of balance, so he climbed up.

===Chinese===
那光芒唤醒了他，因为它预示着更多可能，他继续往前爬，经过另一扇贴在内壁上的门。仿佛在管道中穿行。罗斯停下来，把麻木的手贴在束腰外衣下裸露的胸口上，突然意识到这里有温暖。他的呼吸不再在眼前凝成霜雾，大胆深吸几口气，空气也不会灼伤肺腑了。

意识到这一点后，某种动物般的警觉回到了他身上。留在原地、停在入口处只会招致灾祸。他必须在倒下之前找到藏身之处，因为他感觉到自己已经接近体力的极限。希望给了他一瞬间虚假的力量、前进的动力，他必须充分利用这份馈赠。

他的路尽头是一架宽阔的梯子，以舒缓的弧度向下盘旋入黑暗，向上延伸入阴影。他感觉到自己身处一座规模不小的建筑里。他不敢往下走，因为光是朝那个方向看一眼，平衡感就几乎崩溃，于是他向上攀爬。

===Original===
Step by step, Ross made that painful journey, passing levels from which three or four hallways ran out like the radii of a spider’s web. He was close to the end of his endurance when he heard a sound, echoed, magnified, from below. It was someone moving. He dragged his body into the fourth level where the light was very faint, hoping to crawl far enough into one of the passages to remain unseen from the stair. But he had gone only part-way down his chosen road when he collapsed, panting, and fell back against the wall. His hands pawed vainly against that sleek surface. He was falling through it!

Ross had a second, perhaps two, of stupefied wonder. Lying on a soft surface, he was enfolded by a warmth which eased his bruised and frozen body. There was a sharp prick in his thigh, another in his arm, and the world was a hazy dream until he finally slept in the depths of exhaustion.

There were dreams, detailed ones, and Ross stirred uneasily as his sleep thinned to waking. He lay with his eyes closed, fitting together odd bits of—dreams? No, he were certain that they were memories. Rossa of the Beaker traders and Ross Murdock of the project were again fused into one and the same person. How it had happened he did not know, but it was true.

===Chinese===
罗斯一步一步痛苦地攀爬，经过一层又一层，每层都有三四条走廊像蜘蛛网的辐条一样向外延伸。他几乎到了极限时，听到下方传来一个声音，被回声放大了。有人在走动。他拖着身体进入第四层，那里的光线非常微弱，他希望能爬进某条通道深处，不被楼梯上的人发现。但刚走到一半，他就瘫倒了，喘着粗气，背靠在墙上。他的手徒劳地在光滑的表面上摸索。他正在穿过墙壁！

罗斯有一两秒钟处于震惊的茫然中。他躺在柔软的表面上，被一股温暖包裹着，缓解了他冻伤和瘀伤的身体。大腿上有一记刺痛，手臂上又是一记，世界变成了一片朦胧的梦境，直到他终于在极度疲惫中沉沉睡去。

他做了梦，细节分明。罗斯在浅眠中不安地翻动。他闭着眼睛躺着，将零碎片段拼凑在一起——梦？不，他确信那是记忆。比克尔商人（Beaker traders）罗萨（Rossa）和项目特工罗斯·默多克（Ross Murdock）再次融合成了同一个人。他不知道这是怎么发生的，但事实如此。

===Original===
Opening his eyes, he noticed a curved ceiling of soft blue which misted at the edges into gray. The restful color acted on his troubled, waking mind like a soothing word. For the first time since he had been struck down in the night his headache was gone. He raised his hand to explore that old hurt near his hairline that had been so tender only yesterday that it could not bear pressure. There remained only a thin, rough line like a long-healed scar, that was all.

Ross lifted his head to look about him. His body lay supported in a cradlelike arrangement of metal, almost entirely immersed in a red gelatinous substance with a clean, aromatic odor. Just as he was no longer cold, neither was he hungry. He felt as fit as he ever had in his life. Sitting up in the cradle, he stroked the jelly away from his shoulders and chest. It fell from him cleanly, leaving no trace of grease or dampness on his skin.

There were other fixtures in the small cylinderlike chamber besides that odd bed in which he had lain. Two bucket-shaped seats were placed at the narrow fore part of the room and before those seats was a system of controls he could not comprehend.

===Chinese===
他睁开眼睛，注意到一片柔和蓝色的弧形天花板，边缘处渐渐化入灰色。这种宁静的颜色对他刚醒来、纷乱的思绪如同温柔的抚慰。自从夜间被击倒以来，这是他第一次不再头痛。他抬手去摸发际线附近那处旧伤，就在昨天还疼得不能碰。如今只剩一道细细的、粗糙的纹路，像一道愈合已久的疤痕，仅此而已。

罗斯抬起头环顾四周。他的身体被安置在一个摇篮状的金属装置里，几乎完全浸在一种有清洁芳香气味的红色胶状物质中。他既不再寒冷，也不再饥饿。他感觉身体状态从未如此好过。他从摇篮里坐起来，用手抹去肩膀和胸口的凝胶。它干净地从皮肤上脱落，没有留下任何油腻或潮湿的痕迹。

这个小型圆柱状舱室里还有其他装置，不止他躺过的那张奇特的床。房间前部的窄端放着两个桶状座椅，座椅前方是他看不懂的一套控制系统。

===Original===
As Ross swung his feet to the floor there was a click from the side which brought him around, ready for trouble. But the noise had been caused by the opening of a door into a small cupboard. Inside the cupboard lay a fat package. Obviously this was an invitation to investigate the offering.

The package contained a much folded article of fabric, compressed and sealed in a transparent bag which he fumbled twice before he succeeded in releasing its fastening. Ross shook out a garment of material such as he had never seen before. Its sheen and satin-smooth surface suggested metal, but its stuff was as supple as fine silk. Color rippled across it with every twist and turn he gave to the length—dark blue fading to pale violet, accented with wavering streaks of vivid and startling green.

Ross experimented with a row of small, brilliant-green studs which made a transverse line from the right shoulder to the left hip, and they came apart. As he climbed into the suit the stuff modeled to his body in a tight but perfect fit. Across the shoulders were bands of green to match the studs, and the stockinglike tights were soled with a thick substance which formed a cushion for his feet.

===Chinese===
罗斯刚把脚放到地板上，侧面传来"咔哒"一声，他立刻转身戒备。但声音来自一扇小储物柜的门。柜子里放着一个鼓鼓囊囊的包裹。显然，这是邀请他查看这件礼物。

包裹里是一件反复折叠的织物，压缩后密封在一个透明袋子里。他笨拙地摸索了两次才打开袋口。罗斯抖出一件他从未见过的衣物。它的光泽和缎子般光滑的表面让人联想到金属，但质地却像上等丝绸一样柔软。随着他每一次扭转，颜色在上面流转——深蓝渐变成淡紫，点缀着闪烁而惊艳的绿色条纹。

罗斯试了一排小而亮的绿色纽扣，它们从右肩斜向左胯排成一行，可以解开。他穿上这套衣服，衣料紧紧贴合身体，完美合身。肩部有与纽扣相配的绿色条纹，裤袜状的紧身裤底部有一层厚实的物质，形成脚底的衬垫。

===Original===
He pressed the studs together, felt them lock, and then stood smoothing that strange, beautiful fabric, unable to account for either it or his surroundings. His head was clear; he could remember every detail of his flight up to the time he had fallen through the wall. And he was certain that he had passed through not only one, but two, of the Red time posts. Could this be the third? If so, was he still a captive? Why would they leave him to freeze in the open country one moment and then treat him this way later?

He could not connect the ice-encased building from which the Reds had taken him with this one. At the sound of another soft noise Ross glanced over his shoulder just in time to see the cradle of jelly, from which he had emerged, close in upon itself until its bulk was a third of its former size. Compact as a box, it folded up against the wall.

Ross, his cushioned feet making no sound, advanced to the bucket-chairs. But lowering his body into one of them for a better look at what vaguely resembled the control of a helicopter—like the one in which he had taken the first stage of his fantastic journey across space and time—he did not find it comfortable. He realized that it had not been constructed to accommodate a body shaped precisely like his own.

===Chinese===
他把纽扣按在一起，感觉到它们锁住了，然后站起身抚平那件奇异而美丽的衣物，却无法解释这件衣服，也无法解释自己的处境。他的头脑清醒了；从逃离到穿过墙壁那一刻的每一个细节都历历在目。他确信自己穿过了不止一个、而是两个赤色分子（Reds）的时间哨站（time posts）。这是第三个吗？如果是，他还是俘虏吗？他们为什么先把他丢在旷野里冻死，现在又这样对待他？

他无法将赤色分子把他带来的那座冰封建筑与这座建筑联系起来。又一声轻响，罗斯回头一看，恰好看到他刚离开的那个凝胶摇篮正在收缩，直到体积只剩原来的三分之一。它紧凑得像一个盒子，折叠起来贴在墙上。

罗斯脚踩软底，无声地走向桶状座椅。他坐进其中一个，仔细端详那套酷似直升机操控系统的装置——就像他第一次踏上那场穿越时空的奇幻旅程时乘坐的那种飞行器——但并不舒服。他意识到这椅子并非为与他体型完全相同的身躯设计的。

===Original===
A body like his own… That jelly bath or bed or whatever it was… The clothing which adapted so skillfully to his measurements…

Ross leaned forward to study the devices on the control board, confirming his suspicions. He had made the final jump of them all! He was now in some building of that alien race upon whose existence Millaird and Kelgarries had staked the entire project. This was the source, or one of the sources, from which the Reds were getting the knowledge which fitted no modern pattern.

A world encased in ice and a building with strange machinery. This thing—a cylinder with a pilot’s seat and a set of controls. Was it an alien place? But the jelly bath—and the rest of it… Had his presence activated that cupboard to supply him with clothing? And what had become of the tunic he was wearing when he entered?

Ross got up to search the chamber. The bed-bath was folded against the wall, but there was no sign of his Beaker clothing, his belt, the hide boots. He could not understand his own state of well being, the lack of hunger and thirst.

===Chinese===
一具和他一样的身体……那凝胶浴或者床，或者不管它是什么……如此灵巧地适应他尺寸的衣物……

罗斯俯身研究控制面板上的装置，证实了他的猜想。他完成了他们所有人最终的跳跃！他现在身处那支外星种族（alien race）的某座建筑中——米莱尔德（Millaird）和凯尔加里斯（Kelgarries）正是赌上了他们的存在才启动了整个项目。这里就是赤色分子获取那些不符合任何现代模式的知识的来源——或者说来源之一。

一个被冰封的世界，一座装有奇异机器的建筑。这东西——一个带驾驶座和操控系统的圆柱体。它是一个外星构造吗？但凝胶浴——还有其他一切……他的出现激活了那个储物柜为他提供衣物？他进来时穿的那件束腰外衣去哪了？

罗斯起身搜查舱室。床浴折叠贴在墙上，但他的比克尔衣物（Beaker clothing）、腰带和皮靴都不见了。他无法理解自己为何如此康健，为何不再饥渴。

===Original===
There were two possible explanations for it all. One was that the aliens still lived here and for some reason had come to his aid. The other was that he stood in a place where robot machinery worked, though those who had set it up were no longer there. It was difficult to separate his memory of the half-buried globe he had seen from his sickness of that moment. Yet he knew that he had climbed and crawled through emptiness, neither seeing nor hearing any other life. Now Ross restlessly paced up and down, seeking the door through which he must have come, but there was not even a line to betray such an opening.

"I want out," he said aloud, standing in the center of the cramped room, his fists planted on his hips, his eyes still searching for the vanished door. He had tapped, he had pushed, he had tried every possible way to find it. If he could only remember how he had come in! But all he could recall was leaning against a wall which moved inward and allowed him to fall. But where had he fallen? Into that jelly bath?

Ross, stung by a sudden idea, glanced at the ceiling. It was low enough so that by standing on tiptoes he could drum his fingers on its surface. Now he moved to the place directly above where the cradle had swung before it had folded itself away.

===Chinese===
对此有两种可能的解释。一种是外星人仍然住在这里，出于某种原因来帮他。另一种是他身处一个只有机器自动运转的地方，尽管设置它的人早已不在。他很难将自己看到的半埋球体的记忆与当时的身体不适区分开来。但他确信自己曾攀爬穿过一片虚空，既没看见也没听见任何其他生命。现在罗斯焦躁地来回踱步，寻找他进来的那扇门，但连一道泄露入口的痕迹都没有。

"我要出去，"他大声说，站在狭小房间的中央，双手叉腰，眼睛仍在搜寻消失的门。他敲过、推过，尝试了所有能找到它的方法。要是能记起自己是怎么进来的就好了！但他只记得靠着一堵向内移动的墙壁跌落下来。但他掉到了哪里？掉进了那凝胶浴？

罗斯被一个突然的念头刺了一下，抬头看向天花板。天花板很低，踮起脚尖就能用手指敲到表面。他走到凝胶摇篮折叠起来之前所在位置的正上方。

===Original===
Rapping and poking, his efforts were rewarded at last. The blue curve gave under his assault. He pushed now, rising on his toes, though in that position he could exert little pressure. Then as if some faulty catch had been released, the ceiling swung up so that he lost his footing and would have fallen had he not caught the back of one of the bucket-seats.

He jumped and by hooking his hands over the edge of the opening, was able to work his way up and out, to face a small line of light. His fingers worked at that, and he opened a second door, entering a familiar corridor.

Holding the door open, Ross looked back, his eyes widening at what he saw. For it was plain now that he had just climbed out of a machine with the unmistakable outline of a snub-nosed rocket. The small flyer—or a jet, or whatever it was—had been fitted into a pocket in the side of the big structure as a ship into a berth, and it must have been set there to shoot from that enclosing chamber as a bullet is shot from a rifle barrel. But why?

===Chinese===
他敲敲打打，努力终于有了回报。蓝色弧面在他的冲击下凹陷了。他踮起脚尖推，尽管这个姿势使不上太大劲。然后，仿佛某个故障的卡扣被释放了，天花板向上弹开，他脚下一滑，若非及时抓住一张桶状座椅的靠背，就摔下去了。

他跳起来，双手扣住开口的边缘，设法爬了上去，眼前出现了一道细长的光缝。他的手指摸索着打开，推开了第二扇门，进入了一条熟悉的走廊。

罗斯没有关门，回头一看，双眼因眼前的景象而睁大。因为很显然，他刚刚爬出来的，是一台外形酷似短粗火箭的机器。这架小型飞行器——或者喷气机，或者不管它是什么——被嵌入巨大建筑侧面的一个凹槽里，就像船驶入船坞一样；它被安置在那里，一定是为了从这个封闭的腔室中发射出去，就像子弹从枪管中射出。但为什么？

===Original===
Ross’s imagination jumped from fact to theory. The torpedo craft could be an atomic jet. All right, he had been in bad shape when he fell into it by chance and the bed machine had caught him as if it had been created for just such a duty. What kind of a small plane would be equipped with a restorative apparatus? Only one intended to handle emergencies, to transport badly injured living things who had to leave the building in a hurry.

In other words, a lifeboat!

But why would a building need a lifeboat? That would be rather standard equipment for a ship. Ross stepped into the corridor and stared about him with open and incredulous wonder. Could this be some form of ship, grounded here, deserted and derelict, and now being plundered by the Reds? The facts fitted! They fitted so well with all he had been able to discover that Ross was sure it was true. But he determined to prove it beyond all doubt.

===Chinese===
罗斯的想象力从事实跳跃到理论。这艘鱼雷状飞行器可能是一架原子喷气机。好吧，他碰巧跌进去时状态很糟，而那张床一般的装置接住了他，仿佛专门为这种职责而生。什么样的小型飞机会配备修复装置？只有那些需要处理紧急情况、运送严重受伤且必须紧急撤离建筑的生物的飞行器。

换句话说，一艘救生艇！

但一座建筑为什么需要救生艇？那应该是船上的标准装备。罗斯走进走廊，带着掩饰不住的惊奇四下张望。这会不会是某种船只，搁浅在这里，被遗弃、破损，现在正被赤色分子掠夺？事实完全吻合！与他所能发现的一切如此契合，罗斯确信这是真的。但他决心要证明得毫无怀疑。

===Original===
He closed the door leading to the lifeboat berth, but not so securely that he could not open it again. That was too good a hiding place. On his cushioned feet he padded back to the stairway, and he stood there listening. Far below were sounds, a rasp of metal against metal, a low murmur of muted voices. But from above there was nothing, so he would explore above before he ventured into that other danger zone.

Ross climbed, passing two more levels, to come out into a vast room with a curving roof which must fill the whole crown of the globe. Here was such a wealth of machines, controls, things he could not understand that he stood bewildered, content for the moment merely to look. There were—he counted slowly—five control boards like those he had seen in the small escape ship. Each of these was faced by two or three of the bucket-seats, only these swung in webbing. He put his hand on one, and it bobbed elastically.

The control boards were so complicated that the one in the lifeboat might have been a child’s toy in comparison. The air in the ship had been good; in the lifeboat it had held the pleasant odor of the jelly; but here Ross sniffed a faint but persistent hint of corruption, of an old malodor.

===Chinese===
他关上通往救生艇停泊处的门，但没有锁死，以便再打开。那是个绝佳的藏身处。他踩着软底靴子无声地走回楼梯，在那里站着倾听。远处深处传来声音，金属摩擦金属的刺耳声，低沉模糊的低语声。但上方一片寂静，于是他在冒险进入那个危险区域之前先探索上方。

罗斯向上攀爬，经过又两层楼，来到一个巨大的房间，弧形的屋顶一定覆盖了球体的整个顶部。这里摆满了机器、控制装置和他无法理解的东西，他站在那里困惑不已，暂时只满足于观看。有——他慢慢数——五块控制面板，和他在那艘小型逃生船里看到的一样。每块面板前都有两三张桶状座椅，只是这些座椅悬挂在网状吊带中。他伸手碰了碰一张座椅，它弹性地晃动了一下。

控制面板复杂得令人咋舌，相比之下救生艇里的那个简直像儿童玩具。船里的空气清新宜人；救生艇里有凝胶的芬芳；但在这里，罗斯嗅到了一股微弱但持续的腐臭，一种陈年的霉味。

===Original===
He left the vantage point by the stairs and paced between the control boards and their empty swinging seats. This was the main control room, of that he was certain. From this point all the vast bulk beneath him had been set in motion, sailed here and there. Had it been on the sea, or through the air? The globe shape suggested an airborne craft. But a civilization so advanced as this would surely have left some remains. Ross was willing to believe that he could be much farther back in time than 2000 BC, but he was still sure that traces of those who could build a thing like this would have existed in the twentieth century AD.

Maybe that was how the Reds had found this. Something they had turned up within their country—say, in Siberia, or some of the forgotten corners of Asia—had been a clue.

Having had little schooling other than the intensive cramming at the base and his own informal education, the idea of the race who had created this ship overawed Ross more than he would admit. If the project could find this, turn loose on it the guys who knew about such things… But that was just what they were striving for, and he was the only project man to have found the prize. Somehow, someway, he had to get back—out of this half-buried ship and its icebound world—back to where he could find his own people. Perhaps the job was impossible, but he had to try. His survival was considered impossible by the men who had had thrown him into the crevice, but here he was. Thanks to the men who had built this ship, he was alive and well.

===Chinese===
他离开楼梯口的优势位置，在控制面板和空荡荡的吊椅之间踱步。他确信这里就是主控室。从这里，他脚下那庞然大物曾被驱动，四下航行。它是在海上，还是在空中？球形外观暗示这是飞行器。但如此先进的文明肯定会留下一些遗迹。罗斯愿意相信他可能比公元前2000年更久远，但他仍然确信，能够建造这种东西的文明的痕迹，应该在公元二十世纪就已存在。

也许赤色分子就是这样找到这里的。他们在自己国内——比如说在西伯利亚（Siberia）或亚洲某些被遗忘的角落——发现的某样东西，成了线索。

除了基地的强化速成培训和自己的非正规教育外，罗斯没有受过多少正规教育，创造这艘飞船的那个种族的想法令他敬畏得超出了他愿意承认的程度。如果项目组能找到这里，派懂行的人来……但这正是他们努力的目标，而他是唯一找到这个宝藏的项目人员。他必须想办法回去——离开这艘半埋的飞船和它冰封的世界——回到能找到自己同伴的地方。也许这任务不可能完成，但他必须尝试。把他推入裂缝的人认为他不可能生还，但他活下来了。多亏了建造这艘飞船的人，他才活着、安好。

===Original===
Ross sat down in one of the uncomfortable seats to think and thus avoided immediate disaster, for he was hidden from the stairs on which sounded the tap of boots. A climber, maybe two, were on their way up, and there was no other exit from the control cabin.

===Chinese===
罗斯在一张不舒服的座椅上坐下来思考，因此躲过了一场即刻的灾难，因为楼梯上传来靴子的敲击声，他被遮挡住了。一个攀爬者——也许两个——正在上行，而控制舱没有其他出口。

## XII / XII
===Original===
Ross dropped from the web-slung chair to the floor and made himself as small as possible under the platform at the front of the cabin. Here, where there was a smaller control board and two seats placed closely together, the odd, unpleasant odor clung and became stronger to Ross’s senses as he waited tensely for the climbers to appear. Though he had searched, there was nothing in sight even faintly resembling a weapon. In a last desperate bid for freedom he crept back to the stairwell.

===Chinese===
罗斯（Ross）从绳网悬挂的椅子上滑落到地板上，尽量蜷缩在船舱前部平台的下方。这里有一个较小的控制台，两张座椅紧挨在一起，那股怪异难闻的气味在此处挥之不去，随着罗斯紧张地等待攀登者出现，气味愈发浓烈。尽管他仔细搜寻过，视野中却没有任何像武器的东西。在最后孤注一掷的求生尝试中，他蹑手蹑脚地爬回了楼梯井。

===Original===
He had been taught a blow during his training period, one which required a precise delivery and, he had been warned, was often fatal. He would use it now. The climber was very close. A cropped head arose through the floor opening, and Ross struck, knowing as his hand chopped against the folds of a fur hood that he had failed.

===Chinese===
他在训练期间学过一种击打技巧，需要精准施展，而且教官警告过，这种击打往往是致命的。他现在就要用上它。攀登者已经很近了。一个剃着短发的脑袋从地板开口处冒出来，罗斯猛地出手，但当他的手掌劈在毛皮兜帽的褶皱上时，他知道自己失手了。

===Original===
But the impetus of that unexpected blow saved him after all. With a choked cry the man disappeared, crashing down upon the one following him. A scream and shouts were heard from below, and a shot ripped up the well as Ross scrambled away from it. He might have delayed the final battle, but they had him cornered. He faced that fact bleakly. They need only sit below and let nature take its course. His session in the lifeboat had restored his strength, but a man could not live forever without food and water.

===Chinese===
但这出其不意的一击带来的冲力最终救了他。那人发出一声窒息的叫喊，消失了，重重地砸在紧跟其后的人身上。下面传来尖叫和呼喊声，一声枪响撕裂了楼梯井，罗斯慌忙躲开。他也许拖延了最后的决战，但他们已经将他逼入了绝境。他冷酷地面对着这个事实。他们只需守在下面，任由自然规律发挥作用。他在救生艇里的那段时间恢复了体力，但一个人没有食物和水是不可能永远活下去的。

===Original===
However, he had bought himself perhaps a yard of time which must be put to work. Turning to examine the seats, Ross discovered that they could be unhooked from their webbing swings. Freeing all of them, he dragged their weight to the stairwell and jammed them together to make a barricade. It could not hold long against any determined push from below, but, he hoped, it would deflect bullets if some sharpshooter tried to wing him by ricochet. Every so often there was the crash of a shot and some shouting, but Ross was not going to be drawn out of cover by that.

===Chinese===
然而，他为自己争取到了也许一码的时间，必须好好利用。罗斯转身检查座椅，发现它们可以从绳网悬挂处解开。他把所有座椅都解了下来，拖到楼梯井边，将它们紧紧塞在一起，做成了一道路障。这路障挡不住下方任何坚决的推进，但他希望，如果哪个神枪手想用跳弹击伤他，这至少能偏转一下子弹。时不时有枪声和叫喊声传来，但罗斯不会因此被引出掩体。

===Original===
He paced around the control cabin, still hunting for a weapon. The symbols on the levers and buttons were meaningless to him. They made him feel frustrated because he imagined that among that countless array were some that might help him out of the trap if he could only guess their use.

===Chinese===
他在控制室里踱来踱去，仍在寻找武器。控制杆和按钮上的符号对他来说毫无意义。这让他感到沮丧，因为他想象在那无数的按钮中，也许有一些能帮他摆脱困境，只要他能猜出它们的用途。

===Original===
Once more he stood by the platform thinking. This was the point from which the ship had been sailed—in the air or on some now frozen sea. These control boards must have given the ship’s master the means not only of propelling the vast bulk, but of unloading and loading cargo, lighting, heating, ventilation, and perhaps defense! Of course, every control might be dead now, but he remembered that in the lifeboat the machines had worked successfully, fulfilled expertly the duty for which they had been constructed.

===Chinese===
他再次站在平台边思考。这里是飞船起航的地方——在空中，或者在如今已冰封的海洋上。这些控制台一定让飞船的主人不仅能够驱动这庞然大物，还能装卸货物、照明、供暖、通风，甚至可能还有防御功能！当然，现在每个控制装置可能都失灵了，但他记得在救生艇里，那些机器运作良好，完美地履行了它们被制造出来的使命。

===Original===
The only step remaining was to try his luck. Having made his decision, Ross simply shut his eyes as he had in a very short and almost forgotten childhood, turned around three times, and pointed. Then he looked to see where luck had directed him.

===Chinese===
剩下的唯一一步就是碰碰运气。做出决定后，罗斯像他短暂且几乎被遗忘的童年时那样闭上眼睛，转了三圈，然后指了一个方向。接着他睁开眼，看看运气指向了哪里。

===Original===
His finger indicated a board before which there had been three seats, and he crossed to it slowly, with a sense that once he touched the controls he might inaugurate a chain of events he could not stop. The crash of a shot underlined the fact that he had no other recourse.

===Chinese===
他的手指指向一个控制台，那里曾摆放着三张座椅。他缓缓走过去，心中有一种预感：一旦他触碰控制装置，可能会引发一连串他无法阻止的事件。一声枪响提醒着他，他已经别无选择。

===Original===
Since the symbols meant nothing, Ross concentrated on the shapes of the various devices and chose one which vaguely resembled the type of light switch he had always known. Since it was up, he pressed it down, counting to twenty slowly as he waited for a reaction. Below the switch was an oval button marked with two wiggles and a double dot in red. Ross snapped it level with the panel, and when it did not snap back, he felt somehow encouraged. When the two levers flanking that button did not push in or move up and down, Ross pulled them out without even waiting to count off.

===Chinese===
既然符号毫无意义，罗斯便专注于各种装置的形状，挑选了一个看起来有点像他一直熟悉的那种电灯开关的东西。因为它向上翘起，他便向下按去，一边等待反应，一边慢慢数到二十。开关下方是一个椭圆形按钮，上面有两个波浪线和两个红点。罗斯把它按得与面板齐平，当它没有弹回来时，他莫名感到有些鼓舞。当按钮两侧的两个控制杆推不进去也无法上下移动时，罗斯甚至没等数数就把它们拔了出来。

===Original===
This time he had results! A crackling of noise with a singsong rhythm, the volume of which, low at first, arose to a drone filled the cabin. Ross, deafened by the din, twisted first one lever and then the other until he had brought the sound to a less piercing howl. But he needed action, not just noise; he moved from behind the first chair to the next one. Here were five oval buttons, marked in the same vivid green as that which trimmed his clothing—two wiggles, a dot, a double bar, a pair of entwined circles, and a crosshatch.

===Chinese===
这一次他得到了结果！一阵噼啪作响的噪音，带着唱歌般的节奏，起初音量不大，但逐渐升高成一种充斥整个船舱的嗡嗡声。罗斯被这嘈杂声震得耳鸣，他先扭动一个控制杆，再扭动另一个，直到把声音调成不那么刺耳的嚎叫。但他需要的是行动，不仅仅是噪音；他从第一张椅子后面挪到下一张。这里有五个椭圆形按钮，颜色与他服装上的滚边一样的鲜绿色——两个波浪线、一个点、一条双横线、一对相交的圆圈和一个网格。

===Original===
Why make a choice? Recklessness bubbled to the surface, and Ross pushed all the buttons in rapid succession. The results were, in a measure, spectacular. Out of the top of the control board rose a triangle of screen which steadied and stood firm while across it played a rippling wave of color. Meanwhile the singsong became an angry squawking as if in protest.

===Chinese===
何必做出选择？鲁莽的情绪涌上心头，罗斯迅速按下了所有按钮。结果在某种程度上是壮观的。一个三角形屏幕从控制台顶部升起，稳定下来，一道道彩色的波纹在其上荡漾。与此同时，那唱歌般的声音变成了愤怒的嘎嘎叫，仿佛在抗议。

===Original===
Well, he had something, even if he didn’t know what it was! And he had also proved that the ship was alive. However, Ross wanted more than a squawk of exasperation, which was exactly what the noise had become. It almost sounded, Ross decided as he listened, as if he were being expertly chewed out in another language. Yes, he wanted more than a series of squawks and a fanciful display of light waves on a screen.

===Chinese===
好吧，他弄出了点东西，尽管他不知道那是什么！他也证明了这艘飞船是活的。然而，罗斯想要的不仅仅是愤怒的嘎嘎叫，而这正是那噪音变成的样子。他一边听一边断定，那听起来几乎就像有人在用另一种语言熟练地训斥他。是的，他想要的不仅仅是一连串的嘎嘎叫和屏幕上光怪陆离的光波显示。

===Original===
At the section of board before the third and last seat there was less choice—only two switches. As Ross flicked up the first the pattern on the screen dwindled into a brown color shot with cream in which there was a suggestion of a picture. Suppose one didn’t put the switch all the way up? Ross examined the slot in which the bar moved and now noted a series of tiny point marks along it. Selective? It would not do any harm to see. First he hurried back to the cork of chairs he had jammed into the stairwell. The squawks were now coming only at intervals, and Ross could hear nothing to suggest that his barrier was being forced.

===Chinese===
在第三张也是最后一张座椅前的控制台区域，选择更少——只有两个开关。当罗斯向上拨动第一个开关时，屏幕上的图案缩小成一种夹杂着乳白色的棕色，隐约显现出一幅图像。如果开关不完全推上去会怎样？罗斯检查了控制杆滑动的槽口，注意到上面有一系列细小的刻度。是可调节的吗？试试看也无妨。他先急忙跑回塞在楼梯井里的椅子堆。嘎嘎声现在只是间歇传来，罗斯没有听到任何迹象表明他的路障正被强行突破。

===Original===
He returned to the lever and moved it back two notches, standing open-mouthed at the immediate result. The cream-and-brown streaks were making a picture! Moving another notch down caused the picture to skitter back and forth on the screen. With memories of TV tuning to guide him, Ross brought the other lever down to a matching position, and the dim and shadowy images leaped into clear and complete focus. But the color was still brown, not the black and white he had expected.

===Chinese===
他回到控制杆旁，将其向后调了两格，眼前的景象让他目瞪口呆。乳白色和棕色的条纹正在构成一幅图像！再向下调一格，图像在屏幕上快速地前后移动。借助调电视的记忆，罗斯将另一个控制杆也调到相应位置，模糊暗淡的影像瞬间变得清晰而完整。但颜色依然是棕色，并非他所预期的黑白。

===Original===
Only, he was also looking into a face! Ross swallowed, his hand grasping one of the strings of chair webbing for support. Perhaps because in some ways it did resemble his own, that face was more preposterously nonhuman. The visage on the screen was sharply triangular with a small, sharply pointed chin and a jaw line running at an angle from a broad upper face. The skin was dark, covered largely with a soft and silky down, out of which hooked a curved and shining nose set between two large round eyes. On top of that astonishing head the down rose to a peak not unlike a cockatoo’s crest. Yet there was no mistaking the intelligence in those eyes, nor the other’s amazement at sight of Ross. They might have been staring at each other through a window.

===Chinese===
然而，他正凝视着一张脸！罗斯咽了口唾沫，一只手抓住椅子绳网的一根绳子以支撑自己。也许因为在某些方面它确实与他自己的脸相似，那张脸才显得更加荒谬地非人。屏幕上的面孔呈尖锐的三角形，下巴小巧而尖，宽阔的上半张脸下方，下颌线斜斜延伸。皮肤黝黑，大部分覆盖着柔软如丝的细毛，从中伸出一只弯曲发亮的鼻子，位于两只大而圆的眼睛之间。在这令人惊叹的头顶上，细毛竖起形成一个尖顶，如同凤头鹦鹉的冠羽。然而，那双眼睛里的智慧不容置疑，对方看到罗斯时的惊讶也同样明显。他们就像隔着一扇窗户互相凝视。

===Original===
Squawk… squeek… squawk… The creature in the mirror—on the vision plate—or outside the window—moved its absurdly small mouth in time to those sounds. Ross swallowed again and automatically made answer.

===Chinese===
嘎嘎……吱吱……嘎嘎……镜子里的生物——或者说在视觉板上——或者说在窗外——用那小得离谱的嘴巴配合着这些声音动着。罗斯又咽了口唾沫，不由自主地回应道。

===Original===
“Hello.” His voice was a weak whistle, and perhaps it did not reach the furry-faced one, for he continued his questions if questions they were. Meanwhile Ross, over his first stupefaction, tried to see something of the creature’s background. Though the objects were slightly out of focus, he was sure he recognized fittings similar to those about him. He must be in communication with another ship of the same type and one which was not deserted!

===Chinese===
“你好。”他的声音像微弱的哨声，也许并没有传到毛茸茸的面孔那里，因为对方继续说着，如果那些算是问题的话。与此同时，罗斯从最初的震惊中恢复过来，试图看清那生物的背景。虽然物体略微模糊，但他确信自己认出了与周围类似的设备。他一定是在与另一艘同型号的飞船通讯，而且那艘飞船并非废弃的！

===Original===
Furry-face had turned his head away to squawk rapidly over his shoulder, a shoulder which was crossed by a belt or sash with an elaborate pattern. Then he got up from his seat and stood aside to make room for the one he had summoned.

===Chinese===
毛茸茸的面孔把头扭到一边，对着身后快速地嘎嘎叫，那肩上斜挎着一条有精致图案的腰带或饰带。然后他从座位上站起来，让到一旁，为他召唤来的那位腾出地方。

===Original===
If Furry-face had been a startling surprise, Ross was now to have another. The man who now faced him on the screen was totally different. His skin registered as pale—cream-colored—and his face was far more human in shape, though it was hairless as was the smooth dome of his skull. When one became accustomed to that egg slickness, the stranger was not bad-looking, and he was wearing a suit which matched the one Ross had taken from the lifeboat.

===Chinese===
如果说毛茸茸的面孔是个惊人的意外，那么罗斯现在将要面对另一个。现在屏幕上面对他的人截然不同。他的皮肤看起来苍白——乳白色——脸型也更接近人类，尽管他和光滑的头颅一样没有毛发。当你习惯了那种蛋壳般的光洁，这个陌生人长得并不难看，而且他穿的制服与罗斯从救生艇里拿的那件相配。

===Original===
This one did not attempt to say anything. Instead, he stared at Ross long and measuringly, his eyes growing colder and less friendly with every second of that examination. Ross had resented Kelgarries back at the project, but the major could not match Baldy for the sheer weight of unpleasant warning he could pack into a look. Ross might have been startled by Furry-face, but now his stubborn streak arose to meet this implied challenge. He found himself breathing hard and glaring back with an intensity which he hoped would get across and prove to Baldy that he would not have everything his own way if he proposed to tangle with Ross.

===Chinese===
这个人没有试图说话。相反，他久久地、审视地盯着罗斯，目光随着审视的每一秒变得愈发冰冷和不友好。罗斯在项目里曾对凯尔加里斯少校（Major Kelgarries）心怀不满，但那位少校在用眼神传达令人不快的警告方面，远不及这个光头男子（Baldy）。罗斯也许曾被毛茸茸的面孔吓到，但现在他性格中倔强的一面挺身而出，迎向这无声的挑战。他发现自己呼吸急促，并以强烈的目光回瞪过去，希望这目光能传达过去，让光头男子明白，如果他想和罗斯纠缠，绝不会占到便宜。

===Original===
His preoccupation with the stranger on the screen betrayed Ross into the hands of those from below. He heard their attack on the barricade too late. By the time he turned around, the cork of seats was heaved up and a gun was pointing at his middle. His hands went up in small reluctant jerks as that threat held him where he was. Two of the fur-clad Reds climbed into the control chamber.

===Chinese===
他专注于屏幕上陌生人的那一刻，将罗斯出卖给了下面那些人。他听到他们攻击路障时已经太晚了。等他转过身，那堆椅子已被掀翻，一支枪正指着他。在那威胁之下，他不情愿地举起双手。两名身穿毛皮的赤色分子（Reds）爬进了控制室。

===Original===
Ross recognized the leader as Ashe’s double, the man he had followed across time. He blinked for just an instant as he faced Ross and then shouted an order at his companion. The other spun Murdock around, bringing his hands down behind him to clamp his wrists together. Once again Ross fronted the screen and saw Baldy watching the whole scene with an expression suggesting that he had been shocked out of his complacent superiority.

===Chinese===
罗斯认出领头的是阿什（Ashe）的替身，那个他穿越时间追踪的男人。他面对罗斯时只是眨了一下眼，然后朝同伴喊了一声命令。另一个转过默多克（Murdock）的身子，把他的双手拉到背后铐在一起。罗斯再次面对屏幕，看到光头男子正观看着整个场面，脸上的表情仿佛他那自鸣得意的优越感已被震惊打破。

===Original===
“Ah…” Ross’s captors were staring at the screen and the unearthly man there. Then one flung himself at the control panel and his hands whipped back and forth, restoring to utter silence both screen and room.

===Chinese===
“啊……”罗斯的俘虏们正盯着屏幕和那个非同寻常的人。接着其中一个扑向控制台，双手飞快地来回操作，屏幕和房间顿时陷入一片死寂。

===Original===
“What are you?” The man who might have been Ashe spoke slowly in the Beaker tongue, drilling Ross with his stare as if by the force of his will alone he could pull the truth out of his prisoner.

===Chinese===
“你是什么人？”那个可能是阿什的人用比克尔语（Beaker tongue）慢慢说道，用目光死死盯着罗斯，仿佛仅凭意志力就能从他的俘虏口中逼出真相。

===Original===
“What do you think I am?” Ross countered. He was wearing the uniform of Baldy, and he had clearly established contact with the time owners of this ship. Let that worry the Red!

===Chinese===
“你认为我是什么人？”罗斯反问道。他穿着光头男子的制服，并且显然与这艘飞船过去的主人建立了联系。让那个赤色分子去操心吧！

===Original===
But they did not try to answer him. At a signal he was led to the stair. To descend that ladder with his hands behind him was almost impossible, and they had to pause at the next level to unclasp the handcuffs and let him go free. Keeping a gun on him carefully, they hurried along, trying to push the pace while Ross delayed all he could. He realized that in his recognition of the power of the gun back in the control chamber, his surrender to its threat, he had betrayed his real origin. So he must continue to confuse the trail to the project in every possible way left to him. He was sure that this time they would not leave him in the first convenient crevice.

===Chinese===
但他们没有试图回答他。得到一个信号后，他被押向楼梯。双手被反绑在背后爬下梯子几乎不可能，他们不得不在下一层停下来，解开手铐让他恢复自由。他们小心翼翼地用枪指着他，催促着赶路，试图加快速度，而罗斯则尽可能地拖延。他意识到，在控制室里他对枪支威力的承认，对威胁的屈服，已经暴露了他真正的来历。所以，他必须继续用尽一切可能的办法，混淆通往项目的踪迹。他确信，这一次他们不会把他丢在第一个方便的缝隙里。

===Original===
He knew he was right when they covered him with a fur parka at the entrance to the ship, once more manacling his hands and dropping a noose leash on him.

===Chinese===
当他们在飞船入口处给他套上一件毛皮大衣，再次铐住他的双手，并给他套上一条绞索皮带时，他知道自己猜对了。

===Original===
So, they were taking him back to their post here. Well, in the post was the time transporter which could return him to his own kind. It would be, it must be possible to get to that! He gave his captors no more trouble but trudged, outwardly dispirited, along the rutted way through the snow up the slope and out of the valley.

===Chinese===
那么，他们是要把他带回这里的哨所。好吧，哨所里有时间传送器，能把他送回自己人那里。到达那里是可能的，必须是可能的！他不再给俘虏们添麻烦，表面上垂头丧气地沿着雪地里车辙印出的道路，跋涉上坡，走出了山谷。

===Original===
He did manage to catch a good look at the globe-ship. More than half of it, he judged, was below the surface of the ground. To be so buried it must either have lain there a long time or, if it were an air vessel, crashed hard enough to dig itself that partial grave. Yet Ross had established contact with another ship like it, and neither of the creatures he had seen were human, at least not human in any way he knew.

===Chinese===
他确实设法好好看了一眼这艘球形飞船。他判断，超过一半的部分埋在地面之下。要埋得这么深，它要么在那里躺了很久，要么如果它是飞行器的话，就是坠毁得足够猛烈，才挖出了这样半个坟墓。然而罗斯与另一艘类似的飞船建立了联系，而且他看到的两个生物都不像人类，至少不是他所知的人类。

===Original===
Ross chewed on that as he walked. He believed that those with him were looting the ship of its cargo, and by its size, that cargo must be a large one. But cargo from where? Made by what hands, what *kind* of hands? Enroute to what port? And how had the Reds located the ship in the first place? There were plenty of questions and very few answers. Ross clung to the hope that somehow he had endangered the Reds’ job here by activating the communication system of the derelict and calling the attention of its probable owners to its fate.

===Chinese===
罗斯一边走一边咀嚼着这个想法。他相信和他在一起的那些人正在洗劫这艘飞船的货物，从其大小来看，那批货物一定数量庞大。但货物来自哪里？由什么手制造，什么*种类*的手？运往哪个港口？而且赤色分子最初是如何找到这艘飞船的？问题很多，答案很少。罗斯紧抓着一丝希望：他通过激活这艘废弃飞船（derelict）的通讯系统，引起了其可能主人对其命运的关注，从而在某种程度上威胁到了赤色分子在这里的任务。

===Original===
He also believed that the owners might take steps to regain their property. Baldy had impressed him deeply during those few moments of silent appraisal, and he knew he would not like to be on the receiving end of any retaliation from the other. Well, now he had only one chance, to keep the Reds guessing as long as he could and hope for some turn of fate which would allow him to try for the time transport. How the plate operated he did not know, but he had been transferred here from the Beaker age and if he could return to that time, escape might be possible. He had only to reach the river and follow it down to the sea where the sub was to make rendezvous at intervals. The odds were overwhelmingly against him, and Ross knew it. But there was no reason, he decided, to lie down and roll over dead to please the Reds.

===Chinese===
他还相信，飞船的主人可能会采取措施夺回他们的财产。光头男子在那短暂的无声审视中给他留下了深刻的印象，他知道自己绝不想成为对方任何报复行动的承受者。好吧，现在他只有一个机会，尽可能长时间地让赤色分子猜测，并祈求命运的转折能让他有机会尝试使用时间传送器。他不知道那个传送板如何运作，但他就是从比克尔时期（Beaker age）被传送到这里的，如果他能回到那个时代，逃跑也许是可能的。他只需到达河流，顺流而下到海边，潜艇（sub）会定期在那里会合。胜算对他极其不利，罗斯心知肚明。但他断定，没有理由躺下来打滚装死来取悦赤色分子。

===Original===
As they approached the post Ross realized how much skill had gone into its construction. It looked as if they were merely coming up to the outer edge of a glacier tongue. Had it not been for the track in the snow, there would have been no reason to suspect that the ice covered anything but a thick core of its own substance. Ross was shoved through the white-walled tunnel to the building beyond.

===Chinese===
当他们接近哨所时，罗斯意识到它的建造凝聚了多少技巧。看起来他们似乎只是来到了一条冰川舌的外缘。如果不是雪地里的车辙印，人们完全没有理由怀疑冰层下面埋着任何不是冰本身厚实内核的东西。罗斯被推过一条白色墙壁的隧道，来到了后方的建筑。

===Original===
He was hurried through the chain of rooms to a door and thrust through, his hands still fastened. It was dark in the cubby and colder than it had been outside. Ross stood still, waiting for his eyes to adjust to the gloom. It was several moments after the door had slammed shut that he caught a faint thud, a dull and hollow sound.

===Chinese===
他被匆匆带过一连串房间，来到一扇门前，被推了进去，双手仍然被绑着。小隔间里一片漆黑，比外面还要冷。罗斯静静地站着，等待眼睛适应黑暗。门砰地一声关上好一会儿后，他才听到一声微弱的闷响，一种沉闷而空洞的声音。

===Original===
“Who is here?” he used the Beaker speech, determining to keep to the rags of his cover, which probably was a cover no longer. There was no reply, but after a pause that distant beat began again. Ross stepped cautiously forward, and by the simple method of running fullface into the walls, discovered that he was in a bare cell. He also discovered that the noise lay behind the left-hand wall, and he stood with his ear flat against it, listening. The sound did not have the regular rhythm of a machine in use—there were odd pauses between some blows, others came in a quick rain. It was as if someone were digging!

===Chinese===
“谁在这里？”他用比克尔语说道，决心坚持他那可能已不再是掩护的伪装。没有回应，但停顿之后，那遥远的敲击声再次响起。罗斯小心翼翼地向前走，通过直接撞上墙壁这种简单的方法，发现自己身处一个空荡荡的牢房。他还发现噪音来自左手边的墙壁后面，于是他把耳朵紧紧贴在墙上倾听。那声音没有机器运转时的规律节奏——有些敲击之间有奇怪的停顿，另一些则像急雨般密集。好像有人在挖掘！

===Original===
Were the Reds engaged in enlarging their icebound headquarters? Having listened for a considerable time, Ross doubted that, for the sound was too irregular. It seemed almost as if the longer pauses were used to check up on the result of labor—was it the extent of the excavation or the continued preservation of secrecy?

===Chinese===
赤色分子是在扩建他们冰封的总部吗？听了相当长一段时间后，罗斯对此表示怀疑，因为声音太不规律了。较长的停顿似乎几乎是在检查劳动成果——是为了确认挖掘的进度，还是为了持续保密？

===Original===
Ross slipped down along the wall, his shoulders still resting against it, and rested with his head twisted so he could hear the tapping. Meanwhile he flexed his wrists inside the hoops which confined them, and folding his hands as small as possible, tried to slip them through the rings. The only result was that he chafed his skin raw to no advantage. They had not taken off his parka, and in spite of the chill about him, he was too warm. Only that part of his body covered by the suit he had taken from the ship was comfortable; he could almost believe that it possessed some built-in conditioning device.

===Chinese===
罗斯沿着墙壁滑坐下去，肩膀仍然靠着墙，歪着头以便能听到敲击声。与此同时，他活动着手腕，在束缚他的手铐环内弯曲双手，尽可能缩小，试图让它们滑过铁环。唯一的结果是他把皮肤磨得生疼，却毫无用处。他们没有脱掉他的大衣，尽管周围寒气逼人，他却觉得太热了。只有他从飞船上拿的那件制服覆盖的部分身体感觉舒适；他几乎相信它内置了某种温控装置。

===Original===
With no hope of relief Ross rubbed his hands back and forth against the wall, scraping the hoops on his wrists. The distant pounding had ceased, and this time the pause lengthened into so long a period that Ross fell asleep, his head falling forward on his chest, his raw wrists still pushed against the surface behind him.

===Chinese===
在没有获救希望的情况下，罗斯把手在墙上前后摩擦，刮擦着手腕上的手铐环。远处的敲击声停止了，这一次停顿持续了很长时间，以至于罗斯睡着了，头向前耷拉在胸前，磨破的手腕仍然抵在身后的墙面上。

===Original===
He was hungry when he woke up, and with that hunger his rebellion sparked into flame. Awkwardly he got to his feet and lurched along to the door through which he had been thrown, where he proceeded to kick at the barrier. The cushiony stuff forming the soles of his tights muffled most of the force of those blows, but some noise was heard outside, for the door opened and Ross faced one of the guards.

===Chinese===
他醒来时感到饥饿，伴随着饥饿，他的反抗精神被点燃了。他笨拙地站起身，蹒跚地走向被扔进来的那扇门，然后开始猛踢那道屏障。构成他紧身裤底的软垫材料吸收了大部分踢打的力量，但外面还是听到了一些声响，因为门开了，罗斯面对着一名守卫。

===Original===
“Food! I want to eat!” He put into the Beaker language all the resentment boiling in him.

===Chinese===
“食物！我要吃东西！”他用比克尔语喊出了心中沸腾的所有怨恨。

===Original===
The fellow ignoring him, reached in a long arm, and nearly tossing the prisoner off balance, dragged him out of the cell. Ross was marched into another room to face what appeared to be a tribunal. Two of the men there he knew—Ashe’s double and the quiet man who had questioned him back in the other time station. The third, clearly one of greater authority, regarded Ross bleakly.

===Chinese===
那家伙没理他，伸进一只长臂，差点把俘虏拽倒，将他拖出了牢房。罗斯被押进另一个房间，面对一个看似审判庭的地方。他认识其中两人——阿什的替身和那个在另一个时间站审问过他的安静男人。第三个人显然更有权威，冷酷地审视着罗斯。

===Original===
“Who are you?” the quiet man asked.

===Chinese===
“你是什么人？”安静的男人问道。

===Original===
“Rossa, son of Gurdi. And I would eat before I make talk with you. I have not done any wrong that you should treat me as a barbarian who has stolen salt from the trading post—”

===Chinese===
“罗萨（Rossa），古尔迪（Gurdi）之子。我想先吃饱再跟你们说话。我并没有做错什么，你们不该这样对待我，就像对待一个从贸易站偷盐的野蛮人——”

===Original===
“You are an agent,” the leader corrected him dispassionately, “of whom you will tell us in due time. But first you shall speak of the ship, of what you found there, and why you meddled with the controls… Wait a moment before you refuse, my young friend.” He raised his hand from his lap, and once again Ross faced an automatic. “Ah, I see that you know what I hold—odd knowledge for an innocent Bronze Age trader. And please have no doubts about my hesitation to use this. I shall not kill you, naturally,” the man continued, “but there are certain wounds which supply a maximum of pain and little serious damage. Remove his parka, Kirschov.”

===Chinese===
“你是一个特工，”领头者不动声色地纠正道，“你很快就会告诉我们你是为谁工作的。但首先你要告诉我们关于那艘飞船的事，你在里面发现了什么，以及你为什么要摆弄那些控制装置……在你拒绝之前，请等一等，我年轻的朋友。”他从膝盖上抬起手，罗斯再次面对一把自动手枪。“啊，我看得出你知道我手里拿的是什么——对于一个清白的青铜时代商人来说，这知识可不寻常。而且请不要怀疑我使用它的决心。我自然不会杀你，”那人继续说道，“但有些伤口能带来最大的痛苦，却没什么严重的伤害。脱掉他的大衣，基尔绍夫（Kirschov）。”

===Original===
Once more Ross was unmanacled, the fur stripped from him. His questioner carefully studied the suit he wore under it. “Now you will tell us exactly what we wish to hear.”

===Chinese===
罗斯再次被解开手铐，毛皮被从他身上剥下。审问者仔细研究着他里面穿的那件制服。“现在，你要一五一十地告诉我们我们想听的内容。”

===Original===
There was a confidence in that statement which chilled Ross; Major Kelgarries had displayed its like. Ashe had it in another degree, and certainly it had been present in Baldy. There was no doubt that the speaker meant exactly what he said. He had at his command methods which would wring from his captive the full sum of what he wanted, and there would be no consideration for that captive during the process.

===Chinese===
那句话里的自信让罗斯不寒而栗；凯尔加里斯少校也曾表露出类似的自信。阿什在某种程度上也有，光头男子当然也有。毫无疑问，说话者字字当真。他掌握着能从俘虏口中榨出他所要的一切的方法，而且在这个过程中不会对俘虏有丝毫怜悯。

===Original===
His implied threat struck as cold as the glacial air, and Ross tried to meet it with an outward show of uncracked defenses.
