He decided to pick and choose from his information, feeding them scraps to stave off the inevitable. Hope dies very hard, and Ross having been pushed into corners long before his work at the project, had had considerable training in verbal fencing with hostile authority. He would volunteer nothing… Let it be pulled from him reluctant word by word! He would spin it out as long as he could and hope that time might fight for him.

===Chinese===
他那含蓄的威胁像冰川空气一样冰冷刺骨，罗斯试图用表面上坚不可摧的防御姿态来应对。他决定从自己的信息中挑选一些，喂给他们一些零碎的线索，以推迟那不可避免的结局。希望是不会轻易死去的，罗斯早在参与项目之前就曾多次被逼入绝境，在与敌对权威的言语交锋中受过大量训练。他不会主动交代任何事情……让每一句话都极不情愿地从他口中被逼出来吧！他会尽可能拖延时间，希望时间能为他而战。

===Original===
“You are an agent…”

===Chinese===
“你是一个特工……”

===Original===
Ross accepted this statement as one he would neither affirm nor deny.

===Chinese===
罗斯既不承认也不否认这句话。

===Original===
“You came to spy under the cover of a barbarian trader,” smoothly, without pause, the man changed language in mid-sentence, slipping from the Beaker speech into English.

===Chinese===
“你假扮野蛮商人前来刺探情报，”那人流畅地、毫无停顿地在句子中途转换了语言，从比克尔语滑入了英语。

===Original===
But long experience in meeting the dangerous with an expression of complete lack of comprehension was Ross’s weapon now. He stared somewhat stupidly at his interrogator with that bewildered, boyish look he had so long cultivated to bemuse enemies in his past.

===Chinese===
但长期以来用完全茫然的表情面对危险人物的经验，现在成了罗斯的武器。他有些呆滞地盯着审问者，脸上带着那种困惑而稚气的表情，那是他过去为了迷惑敌人而长期培养出来的。

===Original===
Whether he could have held out long against the other’s skill—for Ross possessed no illusions concerning the type of examiner he now faced—he was never to know. Perhaps the drastic interruption that occurred the next moment saved for Ross a measure of self-esteem.

===Chinese===
他是否能在对方的技巧面前坚持很久——罗斯对自己现在面对的审讯者类型不抱任何幻想——他永远也不会知道了。也许下一刻发生的剧烈中断为罗斯挽回了一些自尊。

===Original===
There was a distant boom, hollow and thunderous. Underneath and around them the floor, walls, and ceiling of the room moved as if they had been pried from their setting of ice and were being rolled about by the exploring thumb and forefinger of some impatient giant.

===Chinese===
远处传来一声轰鸣，沉闷如雷。在他们脚下和周围，房间的地板、墙壁和天花板都在移动，仿佛它们已从冰层的镶嵌中被撬了出来，正被某个不耐烦的巨人的拇指和食指翻来拨去。

## XIII

===Original===
Ross swayed against a guard, was fended off, and bounced against the wall as the man shouted words Ross could not understand. A determined roar from the leader brought a semblance of order, but it was plain that they had not been expecting this. Ross was hustled out of the room back to his cell. His guards were opening the cell door when a second shock was felt and he was thrust into safekeeping with no ceremony.

He half crouched against the questionable security of the wall, waiting through two more twisting earth waves, both of which were accompanied or preceded by dull sounds. Bombing! That last wrench was really bad. Ross found himself lying on the floor, feeling tremors rippling along the earth. His stomach knotted convulsively with a fear unlike any he had known before. It was as if the very security of the world had been jerked from under him.

But that last explosion—if it was an explosion—appeared to be the end. Ross sat up gingerly after several long moments during which no more shocks moved the floor and walls. A line of light marked the door, showing cracks where none had previously existed. Ross, not yet ready to try standing erect, was heading toward it on his hands and knees when a sharp noise behind him brought him to a stop.

===Chinese===
罗斯（Ross）撞上一个守卫，被推开，又弹到墙上，那人喊着罗斯听不懂的话。领头的一声威严的吼叫勉强维持了秩序，但显然他们没料到会出这种事。罗斯被匆忙押出房间，送回牢房。守卫刚打开牢门，第二次震动就传来了，他被毫不客气地塞了进去。

他半蹲着靠在那面靠不太住的墙边，熬过了又两波扭曲的地震，每一波都伴随着沉闷的声响——或是先有闷响再有震动。是轰炸！最后一波摇撼尤其猛烈。罗斯发现自己已经躺在地上，感受着大地沿地表传来的阵阵余颤。一种从未体验过的恐惧令他的胃剧烈痉挛，仿佛整个世界的安稳都被从脚下一把抽走了。

但最后一次爆炸——如果那算爆炸的话——似乎就是结束了。在漫长的几秒钟里，地板和墙壁不再震动，罗斯小心翼翼地坐了起来。门上出现了一道光线，照出此前并不存在的裂缝。罗斯还没打算站起来，正手脚并用地朝门爬去时，身后一声尖锐的响动让他停了下来。

===Original===
There was no light to see by, but he was certain that the scrape of metal against metal sounded from the far side of the wall. He crawled back and put his ear to the surface. Now he heard not only that scraping, but an undercurrent of clicks, chippings…

Under his exploring hands the surface remained as smooth as ever. Then suddenly, perhaps a foot from his head, there sounded a rip of metal. The wall was being holed from the other side! Ross caught a flicker of very weak light, and moving in it was the point of a tool pulling at the smooth surface of the wall. It broke away with a brittle sound, and a hand holding a light reached through the aperture.

Ross wondered if he should catch that wrist, but the hope that the digger might just possibly be an ally kept him motionless. After the hand with the light whipped back beyond the wall, a wide section gave way and a hunched figure crawled through, followed by a second. In the limited glow he saw the first tunneler clearly enough.

"Assha!"

===Chinese===
没有光可以照明，但他确信从墙的另一侧传来了金属刮擦金属的声音。他爬回去，把耳朵贴在墙面上。现在他听到的不只是刮擦声，还有一阵阵细碎的咔嗒声、凿削声……

他摸索的双手所触及的墙面依然光滑如故。然而突然间，在离他头顶大约一英尺的地方，响起一声金属撕裂的声响。有人正从另一侧凿穿这堵墙！罗斯捕捉到一缕极其微弱的光线，光中有工具的尖端正在撬拉墙面的光滑表面。墙体碎裂发出一声脆响，一只握着手电筒的手从开口处伸了进来。

罗斯犹豫是否应该抓住那只手腕，但一想到挖掘者或许是盟友，他便按兵不动。那只握灯的手缩回墙后，一大片墙体塌了下来，一个弓着身子的人影爬了进来，后面紧跟着第二个。借着有限的光线，他看清了第一个掘进者的面孔。

"阿莎（Assha）！"

===Original===
Ross was unprepared for what followed his cry. The lean brown man moved with a panther's striking speed, and Ross was forced back. A hand like a steel ring on his throat shut the breath away from his bursting lungs; the other's muscular body held him flat in spite of his struggles. The light of the small flash glowed inches beyond his eyes as he fought to fill his lungs. Then the hand on his throat was gone and he gasped, a little dizzy.

"Murdock! What are you doing—?" Ashe's clipped voice was muffled by another sudden explosion. This time the earth tremors not only hurled them from their feet, but seemed to run along the walls and across the ceiling. Ross, burying his face in the crook of his arm, could not rid himself of the fear that the building was being slowly twisted into scrap. When the shock was over he raised his head.

"What's going on?" He heard McNeil ask.

"Attack." That was Ashe. "But why, and by whom—don't ask me! You are a prisoner, I suppose, Murdock?"

"Yes, sir." Ross was glad that his voice sounded normal enough.

He heard someone sigh and guessed it was McNeil. "Another digging party." There was tired disgust in that.

===Chinese===
罗斯没料到自己这一声喊叫会招来什么后果。那个精瘦的棕肤男人以猎豹般的速度扑来，罗斯被推得连连后退。一只钢铁般的手扼住他的喉咙，令他爆满的肺无法呼吸；对方肌肉结实的身体死死将他按住，任凭他怎样挣扎都无济于事。小手电的光在他眼前几英寸处晃动，他拼命想吸一口气。随后那只扼住喉咙的手松开了，他大口喘着气，有些头晕。

"默多克（Murdock）！你在干什么——？"阿什（Ashe）短促的嗓音被又一次突如其来的爆炸淹没了。这一次，地震般的摇撼不仅把他们掀翻在地，还仿佛沿着墙壁和天花板蔓延开来。罗斯把脸埋进臂弯，无法摆脱一个念头：这栋建筑正在被缓缓拧成废铁。震动过去后，他抬起了头。

"怎么回事？"他听到麦克尼尔（McNeil）问。

"袭击。"那是阿什的声音。"但为什么，被谁——别问我！你大概是被关起来了，默多克？"

"是的，长官。"罗斯庆幸自己的声音听起来还算正常。

他听到有人叹了口气，猜是麦克尼尔。"又是一支掘进队。"语气中满是疲惫的厌恶。

===Original===
"I don't understand," Ross appealed to that section of the dark where Ashe had been. "Have you been here all the time? Are you trying to dig your way out? I don't see how you can cut out of this glacier that we're parked under—"

"Glacier!" Ashe's exclamation was as explosive as the tremors. "So we're inside a glacier! That explains it. Yes, we've been here—"

"On ice!" McNeil commented and then laughed. "Glacier—ice—that's right, isn't it?"

"We're collaborating," Ashe continued. "Supplying our dear friends with a lot of information they already have and some flights of fancy they never dreamed about. However, they didn't know we had a few surprise packets of our own strewn about. It's amazing what the boys back at the project can pack away in a belt, or between layers of hide in a boot. So we've been engaged in some research of our own—"

"But I didn't have any escape gadgets." Ross was struck by the unfairness of that.

"No," Ashe agreed, his voice even and cold, "they are not entrusted to first-run men. You might slip up and use them at the wrong moment. However, you appear to have done fairly well…"

===Chinese===
"我不明白，"罗斯朝着黑暗中阿什先前所在的方向说。"你一直在这里吗？你们在挖地道逃出去？可我看不出你们怎么从这座冰川里凿出去——我们被压在冰川下面——"

"冰川！"阿什的惊呼和那些震动一样具有爆发力。"所以我们在冰川里面！这就说得通了。对，我们一直在这儿——"

"在冰上！"麦克尼尔接了一句，然后笑了一声。"冰川——冰——没错吧？"

"我们在合作，"阿什继续说道。"给我们亲爱的朋友们提供大量他们本来就知道的信息，外加一些他们做梦也想不到的异想天开。不过他们不知道我们还暗藏了一些惊喜包。项目基地的伙计们能塞进腰带里的东西、塞进靴子夹层里的东西，真是了不起。所以我们一直在做一些自己的研究——"

"可我什么逃生装备都没有。"罗斯对此感到极不公平。

"没错，"阿什语气平平冷冷地答道，"那些东西不会交给新手。你可能会搞砸，用错时候。不过你看起来干得还不错……"

===Original===
The heat of Ross's rising anger was chilled by the noise which cracked over their heads, ground to them through the walls, flattened and threatened them. He had thought those first shocks the end of this ice burrow and the world; he knew that this one was.

And the silence that followed was as threatening in its way as the clamor had been. Then there was a shout, a shriek. The space of light near the cell door was widening as that barrier, broken from its lock, swung open slowly. The fear of being trapped sent the men in that direction.

"Out!"

Ross was ready enough to respond to that order, but they were stopped by a crackle of sound that could be only one thing—rapid-fire guns. Somewhere in this warren a fight was in progress. Ross, remembering the arrogant face of the bald ship's officer, wondered if this was not an attack in force—the aliens against the looting Reds. If so, would the ship people distinguish between those found here. He feared not.

The room outside was clear, but not for long. As they lay watching, two men backed in, then whirled to stare at each other. A voice roared from beyond as if ordering them back to some post. One of them took a step forward in reluctant obedience, but the other grabbed his arm and pulled him away. They turned to run, and an automatic cracked.

===Chinese===
罗斯正在升腾的怒火被头顶上方劈裂而来的一声巨响浇灭了——声音穿过墙壁碾压过来，要将他们压扁、吞噬。他原以为最初的几下震动就意味着这座冰窟和整个世界的终结；他知道这一次是真的了。

随之而来的沉默，在某种意义上比喧嚣更令人恐惧。然后一声喊叫、一声尖叫响起。牢门附近的光亮在扩大，那扇门已经脱出锁扣，正在缓缓打开。被困的恐惧驱使所有人朝那个方向涌去。

"出去！"

罗斯当然乐意执行这道命令，但一阵噼啪声拦住了他们——那只有一种可能：速射枪。在这座地堡的某个角落，战斗正在进行。罗斯想起那个秃头飞船军官傲慢的面孔，不禁怀疑这是不是一次大规模进攻——外星人对付那些掠夺的赤色分子。如果是这样，飞船人会区分在这里发现的人吗？他觉得不会。

外面的房间暂时是空的，但不会太久。他们匍匐观察时，两个男人退了进来，然后猛地转身对视。外面有个声音在咆哮，像是命令他们回到某个岗位。其中一个不情愿地迈步向前，但另一个抓住他的胳膊把他拽了回来。两人转身要跑，自动武器响了。

===Original===
The man nearest Ross gave a queer little cough and folded forward to his knees, sprawling on his face. His companion stared at him wildly for an instant, and then skidded into the passage beyond, escaping by inches a shot which clipped the door as he lunged through it.

No one followed, for outside there was a crescendo of noise—shouting, cries of pain, an unidentifiable hissing. Ashe darted into the room, taking cover by the body. Then he came back, the fellow's gun in his hand, and with a jerk of his head summoned the other two. He motioned them on in a direction away from the sounds of battle.

"I don't get all this," McNeil commented as they reached the next passage. "What's going on? Mutiny? Or have our boys gotten through?"

"It must be the ship people," Ross answered.

"What ship?" Ashe caught him up swiftly.

"The big one the Reds have been looting—"

"Ship?" echoed McNeil. "And *where* did you get that rig?" In the bright light it was easy to see Ross's alien dress. McNeil fingered the elastic material wonderingly.

"From the ship," Ross returned impatiently. "But if the ship people are attacking, I don't think they will notice any difference between us and the Reds…"

===Chinese===
离罗斯最近的那人发出一声怪异的小咳，向前扑倒，双膝着地，脸朝下趴在地上。他的同伴惊恐地瞪了他一瞬，然后滑入后面的通道，勉强躲过一发子弹——子弹在他冲过去时削中了门框。

没人追来，因为外面的嘈杂声在不断升高——喊叫声、痛苦的惨叫、辨不清来路的嘶嘶声。阿什冲进房间，以那具尸体为掩体。然后他回来，手里握着那人的枪，朝另外两人一摆头示意。他指向远离交战声音的方向。

"搞不懂，"麦克尼尔走到下一条通道时说道。"到底怎么回事？兵变？还是我们的人打穿了？"

"一定是飞船人。"罗斯回答。

"什么飞船？"阿什立刻追问。

"赤色分子一直在洗劫的那艘大飞船——"

"飞船？"麦克尼尔应声说。"那你这身行头是哪儿弄来的？"光线明亮，罗斯那身异族装束一览无余。麦克尼尔不可思议地捏了捏那弹性材料。

"从飞船上，"罗斯没好气地答道。"但如果飞船人正在进攻，我觉得他们不会区分我们和赤色分子有什么不同……"

===Original===
There was a burst of earsplitting sound. For the third time Ross was thrown from his feet. This time the burrow lights flickered, dimmed, and went out.

"Oh, fine," commented McNeil bitterly out of the dark. "I never did care for blindman's buff."

"The transfer plate—" Ross clung to his own plan of escape—"if we can reach that—"

The light which had served Ashe and McNeil in their tunneling clicked on. Since the earth shocks appeared to be over for a while, they moved on, with Ashe in the lead and McNeil bringing up the rear. Ross hoped Ashe knew the way. The sound of fighting had died out, so one side or the other must have gained the victory. They might have only a few moments left to pass undetected.

Ross's sense of direction was fairly acute, but he could not have gone so unerringly to what he sought as Ashe did. Only he did not lead them to the room with the glowing plate, and Ross stifled a protest as they came instead to a small record room.

On a table were three spools of tape which Ashe caught up avidly, thrusting two in the front of his baggy tunic, passing the third to McNeil. Then he sped about trying the cupboards on the walls, but all were locked. His hand falling from the last latch, Ashe came back to the door where Ross waited.

"To the plate!" Ross urged.

===Chinese===
一阵震耳欲聋的巨响爆发了。罗斯第三次被掀翻在地。这一次，坑道里的灯光闪烁了几下，暗了下去，随即熄灭。

"好极了，"麦克尼尔在黑暗中苦涩地嘲讽道。"我向来就讨厌捉瞎子游戏。"

"转移板——"罗斯紧抓着自己的逃生计划——"只要我们能到那里——"

阿什和麦克尼尔掘进时用的那盏手电亮了起来。既然地震似乎暂时告一段落，他们继续前进，阿什领头，麦克尼尔殿后。罗斯希望阿什认得路。打斗声已经停息，说明某一方必定已经胜出。他们可能只剩下几分钟时间不被发现就通过。

罗斯的方向感相当敏锐，但他不可能像阿什那样毫不犹豫地直奔目标。只不过阿什并没有带他们去那个有发光转移板的房间，而是来到了一间小小的档案室，罗斯强忍住没有抗议。

桌上放着三盘记忆磁带（tapes），阿什急切地一把抓起，将两盘塞进宽松束腰外衣的前胸，第三盘递给麦克尼尔。然后他飞快地试遍了墙上的所有柜子，但全都上了锁。他的手从最后一把锁扣上滑落，回到门口罗斯等待的地方。

"去转移板！"罗斯催促道。

===Original===
Ashe surveyed the cupboards once more regretfully. "If we could have just ten minutes here—"

McNeil snorted. "Listen, you may yearn to be the filling in an ice sandwich, but I don't! Another shock and we'll be buried so deep even a drill couldn't find us. Let's get out now. The kid is right about that—if we still can."

Once more Ashe took the lead and they wove through ghostly rooms to what must have been the heart of the post—the transfer point. To Ross's unvoiced relief the plate was glowing. He had been nagged by the fear that when the lights blew out the transfer plate might also have been affected. He jumped for the plate.

Neither Ashe nor McNeil wasted time in joining him there. As they clung together there was a cry from behind them, underlined by a shot. Ross, feeling Ashe sag against him, caught him in his arms. By the reflected glow of the plate he saw the Red leader of the post and behind him, his hairless face hanging oddly bodiless in the gloom, was the alien. Were those two now allies? Before Ross could be sure that he had really seen them, the wracking of space time caught him and the rest of the room faded away.

===Chinese===
阿什恋恋不舍地又看了一眼柜子。"只要给我们十分钟——"

麦克尼尔哼了一声。"听着，你也许甘心当冰三明治的馅料，但我不！再来一次震动，我们就得被埋到钻头都够不着的地方。赶紧撤吧。那孩子说得对——如果我们还来得及的话。"

阿什再次领头，他们穿过一间间幽灵般的房间，走向哨所（the post）的核心——转移点。让罗斯暗暗松了一口气的是，转移板还在发光。他一直担心灯光熄灭时转移板也跟着失灵。他纵身跳上了转移板。

阿什和麦克尼尔也毫不迟疑地跳了上来。三人刚在板上站稳，身后就传来一声喊叫，紧接着是一声枪响。罗斯感到阿什身体一软靠过来，便伸手将他抱住。借着转移板反射的光芒，他看到了哨所的赤色分子头目，而在那人身后，阴暗中悬浮着一颗没有毛发的面孔，显得怪异而无躯——那是外星人。这两人现在结盟了吗？罗斯还没来得及确认自己是否真的看清了他们，空间时间的剧烈扭曲就攫住了他，其余的景物全都消散了。

===Original===
"… free. Get a move on!"

Ross glanced across Ashe's bowed shoulders to McNeil's excited face. The other was pulling at Ashe, who was only half-conscious. A stream of blood from a hole in his bare shoulder soaked the upper edge of his Beaker tunic, but as they steadied him between them, he gained some measure of awareness and moved his feet as they pulled him off the plate.

Well, they were free if only for a few seconds, and there was no reception committee waiting for them. Ross gave thanks silently for those two small favors. But if they were now returned to the Bronze Age village, they were still in enemy territory. With Ashe wounded, the odds against them were so high it was almost hopeless.

Working hurriedly with strips torn from McNeil's kilt, they managed to stop the flow of blood from Ashe's wound. Although he was still groggy, he was fighting, driven by the fear which whipped them all—time was one of their foremost enemies. Ross, Ashe's gun in hand, kept watch on the transfer plate, ready to shoot at anything appearing there.

"That will have to do!" Ashe pulled free from McNeil. "We must move." He hesitated, and then pulling the spools of tape from his bloodstained tunic, passed them to McNeil. "You'd better carry these."

"All right," the other answered almost absently.

"Move!" The force of that order from Ashe sent them into the corridor beyond. "The plate…"

===Chinese===
"……出来了。快走！"

罗斯越过阿什低垂的肩膀，看到麦克尼尔兴奋的面孔。麦克尼尔正拽着半昏迷的阿什。阿什裸露的肩膀上有个弹孔，鲜血不断涌出，浸透了比克尔（Beaker）束腰外衣的上缘，但当他们左右搀扶稳住他时，他恢复了一些意识，被他们拉下转移板时也能迈步了。

好吧，他们自由了，哪怕只是几秒钟，而且没有人等着接待他们。罗斯默默感谢这小小的两桩幸事。但他们如果回到了青铜时代的村庄，就仍在敌占区。阿什受了伤，他们的处境几乎绝望。

他们匆忙用从麦克尼尔苏格兰短裙上撕下的布条，设法止住了阿什伤口的血。虽然他仍然晕乎乎的，但还在坚持，被一种所有人都感受到的恐惧驱动着——时间是他们最大的敌人之一。罗斯手持阿什的枪，盯着转移板，随时准备向出现的任何东西开火。

"只能先这样了！"阿什挣脱了麦克尼尔。"我们必须走了。"他犹豫了一下，然后从血迹斑斑的束腰外衣里掏出那几盘记忆磁带，递给麦克尼尔。"你来带着这些。"

"好的。"麦克尼尔几乎心不在焉地答道。

"走！"阿什这一声命令的力量推着他们冲进了前方的走廊。"转移板……"

===Original===
But the plate remained clear. And Ross noted that they must have returned to the proper time, for the walls about them were the logs and stone of the village he remembered.

"Someone coming through?"

"Should be—soon."

They fled, the hide boots of the other two making only the faintest whisper of sound, Ross's foam-soled feet none at all. He could not have found the door to the outer world, but again Ashe guided them, and only once did they have to seek cover. At last they faced a barred door. Ashe leaned against the wall, McNeil supporting him, as Ross pulled free the locking beam. They let themselves out into the night.

"Which way?" McNeil ask.

To Ross's surprise Ashe did not turn to the gate in the outer stockade. Instead he gestured at the mountain wall in the opposite direction. "They'll expect us to try for the valley pass. So we had better go up the slope there."

"That has the look of a tough climb," ventured McNeil.

Ashe stirred. "When it becomes too tough for me"—his voice was dry—"I shall say so, never fear."

===Chinese===
但转移板依然清澈。罗斯注意到他们必定已经回到了正确的时间层，因为四周的墙壁正是他记忆中那个村庄的原木和石头。

"有人跟过来吗？"

"应该有——快了。"

他们奔跑起来，另外两人的兽皮靴子发出微弱的窸窣声，而罗斯的泡沫鞋底则悄无声息。他自己找不到通往外界的门，但阿什再次带路，他们只躲藏了一次。终于，他们面对着一扇上了门栓的门。阿什靠在墙上，麦克尼尔扶着他，罗斯用力抽出门栓。他们走进了黑夜。

"往哪走？"麦克尼尔问。

令罗斯惊讶的是，阿什没有转向外围栅栏的大门，而是指向相反方向的山壁。"他们会料到我们走山谷隘口。所以我们最好从那边的山坡上去。"

"那看起来很难爬。"麦克尼尔试探地说。

阿什动了动。"等我实在爬不动的时候，"他的声音干涩，"我会说出来的，别担心。"

===Original===
He started out with some of his old ease of movement, but his companions closed in on either side, ready to offer aid. Ross often wondered later if they could have won free of the village on their own efforts that night. He was sure their resolution would have been equal to the attempt, but their escape would have depended upon a fabulous run of luck such as men seldom encounter.

As it was, they had just reached a pool of shadow beside a small hut some two buildings away from the one they had fled, when the fireworks began. As if on signal the three fugitives threw themselves flat. From the roof of the building at the center of the village a pencil of brilliant-green light pointed straight up into the sky, and around that spear of radiance the roof sprouted tongues of more natural red-and-yellow flames. Figures shot from doors as the fire lapped down the peak of the roof.

"Now!" In spite of the rising clamor, Ashe's voice carried to his two companions.

The three sprinted for the palisade, mingling with bewildered men who ran out of the other cabins. The waves of fire washed on, providing light, too much light. Ashe and McNeil could pass as part of the crowd, but Ross's unusual clothing might be easily marked.

===Chinese===
他迈步时恢复了一些往日的从容，但同伴们紧贴在他两侧，随时准备施以援手。罗斯后来时常想，那天夜里如果只有他们自己，能不能逃出那个村庄。他相信他们的决心足以一试，但能否成功取决于一种人们难得遇到的好运。

事实上，他们刚逃到离那间屋子大约两栋建筑远的一间小棚旁边的阴影里，焰火就炸响了。仿佛收到了信号，三个逃亡者同时扑倒在地。村子中央那栋建筑的屋顶上，一道亮绿色的光柱笔直射向天空，围绕着那支光矛，屋顶上窜出更自然的红黄色火焰。火舌舔下屋脊时，人影从各个门口窜了出来。

"现在！"尽管嘈杂声越来越大，阿什的声音还是传到了两个同伴耳中。

三人朝栅栏冲去，混入从其他棚屋里惊惶跑出的人群。火焰的波浪继续蔓延，提供了照明，但光线太亮了。阿什和麦克尼尔可以混在人群中，但罗斯那身不寻常的装束很容易被辨认出来。

===Original===
Others were running for the wall. Ross and McNeil boosted Ashe to the top, saw him over in safety. McNeil followed. Ross was just reaching to draw himself up when he was enveloped in a beam of light.

A high, screeching call, unlike any shout he had heard, split the clamor. Frantically Ross tried for a hold, knowing that he was presenting a perfect target for those behind. He gained the top of the stockade, looked down into a black block of shadow, not knowing whether Ashe and McNeil were waiting for him or had gone ahead. Hearing that strange cry again, Ross leaped blindly out into the darkness.

He landed badly, hitting hard enough to bruise, but thanks to the skill he had learned for parachuting, he broke no bones. He got to his feet and blundered on in the general direction of the mountain Ashe had picked as their goal. There were others coming over the wall of the village and moving through the shadows, so he dared not call out for fear of alerting the enemy.

===Chinese===
其他人也在朝围墙跑。罗斯和麦克尼尔把阿什托上墙头，看着他安全翻了过去。麦克尼尔随后跟上。罗斯刚伸手要撑上去，就被一道光束罩住了。

一声尖锐刺耳的呼啸穿透了喧嚣，那是他从未听过的叫声。罗斯拼命抓握，知道自己在身后那些人面前是一个完美的靶子。他攀上栅栏顶部，俯瞰下方一片漆黑，不知道阿什和麦克尼尔是在等他还是已经先走了。再次听到那声怪叫，罗斯纵身跳入了黑暗中。

他落地时姿势很糟，重重摔了一跤，但多亏他跳伞时练就的技巧，没有摔断骨头。他爬起来，跌跌撞撞地朝阿什选定的那座山的方向前进。村子里也有人翻过围墙，在阴影中穿行，所以他不敢出声喊叫，怕惊动敌人。

===Original===
The village had been set in the widest part of the valley. Behind its stockade the open ground narrowed swiftly, like the point of a funnel, and all fugitives from the settlement had to pass through that channel to escape. Ross's worst fear was that he had lost contact with Ashe and McNeil, and that he would never be able to pick up their trail in the wilderness ahead.

Thankful for the dark suit he wore which was protective covering in the night, he twice ducked into the brush to allow parties of refugees to pass him. Hearing them speak the guttural clicking speech he had learned from Ulffa's people, Ross deduced that they were innocent of the village's real purpose. These people were convinced they had been attacked by night demons. Perhaps there had only been a handful of Reds in that hidden retreat.

Ross pulled himself up a hard climb, and pausing to catch his breath, looked back. He was not overly surprised to see figures moving leisurely about the village examining the cabins, perhaps in search of the inhabitants. Each of those searchers was clad in a form-fitting suit that matched his own, and their bulbous hairless heads gleamed white in the firelight. Ross was astonished to see that they passed straight through walls of flame, apparently unconcerned and unsinged by the heat.

===Chinese===
村庄坐落在山谷最宽阔的地方。栅栏后面，开阔地带迅速收窄，形如漏斗的尖口，所有从聚居点逃出的人都必须通过这个通道。罗斯最大的恐惧是他已经和阿什、麦克尼尔失散了，在前方的荒野中恐怕再也找不到他们的踪迹。

他庆幸自己穿着深色的衣服，在夜色中提供了保护。他两次钻进灌木丛，让一队队难民从身旁经过。听到他们说着从乌尔法（Ulffa）族人那里学来的那种喉音重重、咔嗒作响的语言，罗斯推断他们对村庄的真实用途一无所知。这些人坚信自己遭到了夜魔的攻击。也许那个隐秘据点里只有少数几个赤色分子。

罗斯攀上一段艰难的坡，停下来喘了口气，回头望去。看到几个身影在村庄里悠闲地走动，检查那些棚屋，或许在搜寻居民，他并不特别惊讶。每个搜索者都穿着和他身上一样的紧身服，他们那圆鼓鼓的秃头在火光中泛着白色。罗斯惊讶地看到他们径直穿过火焰，显然毫不在意，也未被高温灼伤。

===Original===
The human beings trapped in the town wailed and ran, or lay and beat their heads and hands on the ground, supine before the invaders. Each captive was dragged back to a knot of aliens near the main building. Some were hurled out again into the dark, unharmed; a few others were retained. A sorting of prisoners was plainly in progress. There was no question that the ship people had followed through into this time, and that they had their own arrangements for the Reds.

Ross had no desire to learn the particulars. He started climbing again, finding the pass at last. Beyond, the ground fell away again, and Ross went forward into the full darkness of the night with a vast surge of thankfulness.

Finally, he stopped simply because he was too weary, too hungry, to keep on his feet without stumbling, and a fall in the dark on these heights could be costly. Ross discovered a small hollow behind a stunted tree and crept into it as best he could, his heart laboring against his ribs, a hot stab of pain cutting into his side with every breath he drew.

===Chinese===
被困在镇上的人类哀号着四散奔逃，或是趴在地上用头和手捶打地面，在入侵者面前俯首帖耳。每个俘虏都被拖回主建筑旁外星人聚集的地方。有些人被重新扔回黑暗中，未受伤害；另一些人则被留了下来。显然正在进行一场囚犯分类。毫无疑问，飞船人追踪到了这个时代，并且对赤色分子有他们自己的处置方式。

罗斯无心了解详情。他继续攀爬，终于找到了隘口。山隘那边地面再次下倾，罗斯在彻底的夜色中前行，心中涌起无尽的感恩。

最终他停了下来，纯粹是因为太疲惫、太饥饿，再走下去就会摔倒，而在这些高地的黑暗中跌落可能代价高昂。罗斯在一棵矮树后面发现了一个小凹洞，尽量蜷身钻了进去，心脏在肋骨下沉重地跳动，每吸一口气侧腹就传来一阵灼痛。

===Original===
He awoke all at once with the snap of a fighting man who is alert to ever present danger. A hand lay warm and hard over his mouth, and above it his eyes met McNeil's. When he saw that Ross was awake McNeil withdraw his hand. The morning sunlight was warm about them. Moving clumsily because of his stiff, bruised body, Ross crawled out of the hollow. He looked around, but McNeil stood there alone. "Ashe?" Ross questioned him.

McNeil, showing a haggard face covered with several days' growth of rusty-brown beard, nodded his head toward the slope. Fumbling inside his kilt, he brought out something clenched in his fist and offered it to Ross. The latter held out his palm and McNeil covered it with a handful of coarse-ground grain. Just to look at the stuff made Ross long for a drink, but he mouthed it and chewed, getting up to follow McNeil down into the tree-grown lower slopes.

"It's not good." McNeil spoke jerkily, using Beaker speech. "Ashe is out of his head some of the time. That hole in his shoulder is worse than we thought it was, and there's always the threat of infection. This whole wood is full of people flushed out of that blasted village! Most of them—all I've seen—are natives. But they have it firmly planted in their minds now that there are devils after them. If they see you wearing that suit—"

===Chinese===
他猛然惊醒，带着一个时刻警惕危险的战士特有的警觉。一只温暖而有力的手捂在他嘴上，他的目光越过手指与麦克尼尔的相遇了。看到罗斯醒了，麦克尼尔松开了手。温暖的晨光洒在他们周围。罗斯因为身体僵硬酸痛，笨拙地爬出凹洞。他四下张望，但只有麦克尼尔一个人站在那里。"阿什呢？"罗斯问他。

麦克尼尔面容憔悴，几天没刮的铁锈色胡茬爬满脸庞，朝山坡方向点了点头。他在短裙里摸索了一阵，掏出攥在拳头里的什么东西递给罗斯。罗斯伸出手掌，麦克尼尔将一把粗磨谷粒倒在上面。光看着这东西就让罗斯渴望喝口水，但他还是把谷粒塞进嘴里嚼起来，起身跟着麦克尼尔走向树木稀疏的下坡。

"情况不好。"麦克尼尔断断续续地说，用的是比克尔语。"阿什时不时陷入昏迷。肩膀上的伤口比我们想的严重得多，而且随时有感染的危险。这片林子里到处都是从那个该死的村庄逃出来的人！大部分——我见到的全部——都是本地人。但他们现在死心塌地相信有恶魔在追他们。要是看到你穿着那身衣服——"

===Original===
"I know, and I'd strip if I could," Ross agreed. "But I'll have to get other clothing first; I can't run bare in this cold."

"That might be safer," McNeil growled. "I don't know just what happened back there, but it certainly must have been plenty!"

Ross swallowed a very dry mouthful of grain and then stooped to scoop up some leftover snow in the shadow of a tree root. It was not as refreshing as a real drink, but it helped. "You said Ashe is out of his head. What do we do for him, and what are your plans?"

"We have to reach the river, somehow. It drains to the sea, and at its mouth we are supposed to make contact with the sub."

The proposal sounded impossible to Ross, but so many impossible things had happened lately he was willing to go along with the idea—as long as he could. Gathering up more snow, he stuffed it into his mouth before he followed the already disappearing McNeil.

===Chinese===
"我知道，如果能脱我早脱了，"罗斯说。"但我得先弄到别的衣服；这种天光着膀子跑不了。"

"光着可能还安全些，"麦克尼尔嘟囔道。"我不知道后面到底发生了什么，但肯定够热闹的！"

罗斯咽下一口干涩的谷粒，然后弯腰在树根的阴影里铲了些残留的雪。虽然不如真喝水解渴，但也聊胜于无。"你说阿什时不时昏迷。我们怎么照顾他，你有什么计划？"

"我们无论如何得赶到河边。河流入海，河口处我们按计划要和潜艇（the sub）接头。"

这个提议听起来不可能实现，但近来发生了太多不可能的事，罗斯愿意跟着这个念头走——只要他还走得动。他又抓了几把雪塞进嘴里，然后追上已经走远的麦克尼尔。

## XIV

===Original===
"… that's my half of it. The rest of it you know." Ross held his hands close to the small fire sheltered in the pit he had helped dig and flexed his cold-numbed fingers in the warmth.

From across the handful of flames Ashe's eyes, too bright in a fever-flushed face, watched him demandingly. The fugitives had taken cover in an angle where the massed remains of an old avalanche provided a cave-pocket. McNeil was off scouting in the gray drizzle of the day, and their escape from the village was now some forty-eight hours behind them.

"So the crackpots were right, after all. They only had their times mixed." Ashe shifted on the bed of brush and leaves they had raked together for his comfort.

"I don't understand﻿—"

===Chinese===
"……这就是我那一半的故事。剩下的你都知道了。"罗斯把手凑近藏在他们帮忙挖的坑里的小火堆，在暖意中活动着冻僵的手指。

火堆对面，阿什的眼睛在发烧泛红的脸上显得异常明亮，正带着审视的神色注视着他。几个逃亡者藏身在一处由古老雪崩堆积物形成的洞穴里。麦克尼尔（McNeil）趁着白天灰蒙蒙的细雨出去侦察了，他们从村子逃出来已经大约四十八小时。

"所以那些疯子终究是对的。只是他们把时间搞混了。"阿什在他们为他铺的灌木和树叶床上挪了挪身子。

"我不明白——"

===Original===
"Flying saucers," Ashe returned with an odd little laugh. "It was a wild possibility, but it was on the books from the start. This certainly will make Kelgarries turn red﻿—"

"Flying saucers?"

Ashe must be out of his head from the fever, Ross supposed. He wondered what he should do if Ashe tried to get up and walk away. He could not tackle a man with a bad hole in his shoulder, nor was he certain he could wrestle Ashe down in a real fight.

"That globe-ship was never built on this world. Use your head, Murdock. Think about your furry-faced friend and the baldy with him. Did either look like normal Terrans to you?"

===Chinese===
"飞碟，"阿什回答，带着一声古怪的轻笑。"这是一个疯狂的可能性，但从一开始就列在了备忘录上。这事肯定会让凯尔加里斯（Kelgarries）脸红的——"

"飞碟？"

阿什一定是烧糊涂了，罗斯心想。他盘算着如果阿什试图起身走开，自己该怎么办。他没法扑倒一个肩膀上有个大洞的人，也不确定在真正的搏斗中能否制伏阿什。

"那个球形飞船从来就不是在这个世界上建造的。动动脑子，默多克（Murdock）。想想你那个毛脸朋友和跟他在一起的秃子。他们俩在你看来像正常的地球人吗？"

===Original===
"But﻿—a spaceship!" It was something that had so long been laughed to scorn. When men had failed to break into space after the initial excitement of the satellite launchings, space flight had become a matter for jeers. On the other hand, there was the evidence collected by his own eyes and ears, his own experience. The services of the lifeboat had been techniques outside of his experience.

"This was insinuated once"﻿—Ashe was lying flat now, gazing speculatively up at the projection of logs and earth which made them a partial roof﻿—"along with a lot of other bright ideas, by a gentleman named Charles Fort, who took a lot of pleasure in pricking what he considered to be vastly over-inflated scientific pomposity. He gathered together four book loads of reported incidents of unexplainable happenings which he dared the scientists of his day to explain. And one of his bright suggestions was that such phenomena as the vast artificial earthworks found in Ohio and Indiana were originally thrown up by space castaways to serve as SOS signals. An intriguing idea, and now perhaps we may prove it true."

===Chinese===
"可是——宇宙飞船！"那是一个被嘲笑了太久的东西。当人类在人造卫星发射的最初热潮之后未能冲破太空，太空飞行就变成了一个笑柄。然而另一方面，有他自己的眼睛和耳朵收集到的证据，有他自己的亲身经历。救生艇所运用的技术超出了他的认知范围。

"曾经有人暗示过这一点——"阿什现在平躺着，若有所思地仰望着由原木和泥土构成的半截屋顶——"连同许多其他聪明的想法，提出者是一位名叫查尔斯·福特（Charles Fort）的绅士，他以戳穿那些他认为被严重吹嘘的科学傲慢为乐。他汇集了四大本书的未解事件报告，向他那个时代的科学家们发起挑战，要他们做出解释。他有一个聪明的推测：在俄亥俄和印第安纳发现的那些巨大人造土方工程，最初是太空遇难者用来作为求救信号的。一个耐人寻味的想法，现在我们也许可以证明它是对的。"

===Original===
"But if such spaceships were wrecked on this world, I still don't see why we didn't find traces of them in our own time."

"Because that wreck you explored was bedded in a glacial era. Do you have any idea how long ago that was, counting from our own time? There were at least three glacial periods﻿—and we don't know in which one the Reds went visiting. That age began about a million years before we were born, and the last of the ice ebbed out of New York State some thirty-eight thousand years ago, boy. That was the early Stone Age, reckoning it by the scale of human development, with an extremely thin population of the first real types of man clinging to a few warmer fringes of wilderness."

===Chinese===
"但如果这样的飞船在这个世界上坠毁了，我还是不明白为什么我们在自己的时代没有找到它们的痕迹。"

"因为你探索的那艘残骸埋在一个冰河时期里。你知道那是多久以前的事吗，从我们自己的时代算起？至少经历过三个冰河期——而且我们不知道赤色分子去的是哪一个。那个时代在我们出生之前大约一百万年就开始了，而纽约州最后一片冰层在大约三万八千年前才退去，伙计。按照人类发展的尺度来衡量，那是石器时代早期，极其稀少的最早期真正人类散布在少数几片温暖的荒野边缘。"

===Original===
"Climatic changes, geographical changes, all altered the face of our continents. There was a sea in Kansas; England was part of Europe. So, even though as many as fifty such ships were lost here, they could all have been ground to bits by the ice flow, buried miles deep in quakes, or rusted away generations before the first really intelligent man arrived to wonder at them. Certainly there couldn't be too many such wrecks to be found. What do you think this planet was, a flypaper to attract them?"

"But if ships crashed here once, why didn't they later when men were better able to understand them?" Ross countered.

"For several reasons﻿—all of them possible and able to be fitted into the fabric of history as we know it on this world. Civilizations rise, exist, and fall, each taking with it into the limbo of forgotten things some of the discoveries which made it great. How did the Indian civilizations of the New World learn to harden gold into a useable point for a cutting weapon? What was the secret of building possessed by the ancient Egyptians? Today you will find plenty of men to argue these problems and half a hundred others."

===Chinese===
"气候变化、地理变迁，改变了我们大陆的面貌。堪萨斯曾经有一片海；英格兰曾是欧洲的一部分。所以，即便多达五十艘这样的飞船在这里失事，它们也全都被冰流碾成碎片，被地震埋入数英里深处，或者在第一个真正有智慧的人到来并为之惊叹之前许多代就已经锈蚀殆尽。这种残骸不可能有太多留存至今。你以为这个星球是什么，引它们上钩的粘蝇纸吗？"

"但如果飞船以前坠毁过一次，为什么后来人类更有能力理解它们的时候反而不再坠毁了？"罗斯反驳道。

"原因有几个——每一个都合情合理，都能融入我们所知的这个世界的历史脉络。文明兴起、存续、衰落，每一个都把使其伟大的某些发现一同带入被遗忘的深渊。新世界的印第安文明是怎么学会把黄金硬化成可用的切割武器尖端的？古埃及人掌握的建筑秘密是什么？今天你会找到很多人争论这些问题以及另外五十来个类似问题。"

===Original===
"The Egyptians once had a well-traveled trade route to India. Bronze Age traders opened up roads down into Africa. The Romans knew China. Then came an end to each of these empires, and those trade routes were forgotten. To our European ancestors of the Middle Ages, China was almost a legend, and the fact that the Egyptians had successfully sailed around the Cape of Good Hope was unknown. Suppose our space voyagers represented some star-born confederacy or empire which lived, rose to its highest point, and fell again into planet-bound barbarism all before the first of our species painted pictures on a cave wall?"

"Or take it that this world was an unlucky reef on which too many ships and cargoes were lost, so that our whole solar system was posted, and skippers of star ships thereafter avoided it? Or they might even have had some rule that when a planet developed a primitive race of its own, it was to be left strictly alone until it discovered space flight for itself."

===Chinese===
"埃及人曾经有一条通往印度的繁忙贸易通道。青铜时代的商人们开辟了通往非洲腹地的道路。罗马人知道中国。然后这些帝国一个接一个终结，那些贸易路线被遗忘了。对中世纪我们的欧洲祖先来说，中国几乎是传说，而埃及人曾成功绕过好望角这件事不为人知。假想一下，我们的太空旅行者代表着某个星际联盟或帝国，它兴起、达到巅峰、然后再次跌入行星束缚的蛮荒——所有这一切都发生在我们物种中的第一个在洞穴墙壁上画画之前？"

"或者这样想：这个世界是一座不幸的暗礁，太多飞船和货物在这里损失了，以至于整个太阳系都被标注了禁区，星际飞船的船长们从此避之不及？又或者他们甚至有这样一条规矩：当一颗星球发展出自己的原始种族时，在它自行发现太空飞行之前，必须严格保持距离。"

===Original===
"Yes." Every one of Ashe's suppositions made good sense, and Ross was able to believe them. It was easier to think that both Furry-face and Baldy were inhabitants of another world than to think their kind existed on this planet before his own species was born. "But how did the Reds locate that ship?"

"Unless that information is on the tapes we were able to bring along, we shall probably never know," Ashe said drowsily. "I might make one guess﻿—the Reds have been making an all-out effort for the past hundred years to open up Siberia. In some sections of that huge country there have been great climatic changes almost overnight in the far past. Mammoths have been discovered frozen in the ice with half-digested tropical plants in their stomach. It's as if the beasts were given some deepfreeze treatment instantaneously. If in their excavations the Reds came across the remains of a spaceship, remains well enough preserved for them to realize what they had discovered, they might start questing back in time to find a better one intact at an earlier date. That theory fits everything we know now."

"But why would the aliens attack the Reds now?"

"No ship's officers ever thought gently of pirates." Ashe's eyes closed.

===Chinese===
"是的。"阿什的每一种假设都说得通，罗斯能够接受。与其认为毛脸和秃子那样的生物在他自己的物种诞生之前就存在于这个星球上，倒不如认为他们来自另一个世界更容易接受。"但赤色分子是怎么找到那艘飞船的？"

"除非那个信息在我们带出来的记忆磁带上，否则我们大概永远不会知道了，"阿什昏昏沉沉地说。"我可以猜一猜——赤色分子在过去一百年里一直在全力开发西伯利亚。在这个巨大国家的某些地区，远古时代曾发生过几乎一夜之间的剧烈气候变化。猛犸象被发现冻在冰里，胃里还有半消化的热带植物。就好像那些巨兽被瞬间进行了某种冷冻处理。如果赤色分子在挖掘中发现了飞船残骸，而且保存得足够完好让他们意识到自己发现了什么，他们可能会开始回溯时间，去寻找一艘更早期、更完好的飞船。这个理论符合我们现在所知的一切。"

"但为什么外星人现在要攻击赤色分子？"

"从来没有哪个飞船的船长会对海盗心慈手软。"阿什闭上了眼睛。

===Original===
There were questions, a flood of them, that Ross wanted to ask. He smoothed the fabric on his arm, that stuff which clung so tightly to his skin yet kept him warm without any need for more covering. If Ashe were right, on what world, what kind of world, had that material been woven, and how far had it been brought that he could wear it now?

Suddenly McNeil slid into their shelter and dropped two hares at the edge of the fire.

"How goes it?" he said, as Ross began to clean them.

"Reasonably well," Ashe, his eyes still closed, replied to that before Ross could. "How far are we from the river? And do we have company?"

"About five miles﻿—if we had wings." McNeil answered in a dry tone. "And we have company all right, lots of it!"

===Chinese===
罗斯有一大堆问题想问。他抚平手臂上的衣料，那层紧贴皮肤的织物却无需任何额外的覆盖物就能保暖。如果阿什是对的，这种材料是在什么样的世界上编织出来的？它被带了多远才到了他的身上？

突然，麦克尼尔滑进了他们的藏身处，在火堆边扔下两只野兔。

"情况怎么样？"他一边说，罗斯一边开始清理猎物。

"还算可以，"阿什——眼睛仍然闭着——在罗斯开口之前先回答了。"我们离河边还有多远？有伴儿吗？"

"大约五英里——如果我们有翅膀的话。"麦克尼尔干巴巴地回答。"而且我们确实有伴儿，一大堆！"

===Original===
That brought Ashe up, leaning forward on his good elbow. "What kind?"

"Not from the village." McNeil frowned at the fire which he fed with economic handfuls of sticks. "Something's happening on this side of the mountains. It looks as if there's a mass migration in progress. I counted five family clans on their way west﻿—all in just this one morning."

"The village refugees' stories about devils might send them packing," Ashe mused.

"Maybe." But McNeil did not sound convinced. "The sooner we head downstream, the better. And I hope the boys will have that sub waiting where they promised. We do possess one thing in our favor﻿—the spring floods are subsiding."

"And the high water should have plenty of raft material." Ashe lay back again. "We'll make those five miles tomorrow."

===Chinese===
这下阿什坐起来了，用没受伤的手肘撑着身子。"什么人？"

"不是村子的人。"麦克尼尔皱着眉头往火里添柴，每一把都精打细算。"山的这一侧正在发生什么事。看起来像是一场大规模的迁徙。我数到了五个家族部落往西走——光这个早上就有五个。"

"村里难民讲的那些关于恶魔的故事也许把他们吓跑了，"阿什沉思着说。

"也许吧。"但麦克尼尔的语气并不信服。"越快顺流而下越好。我希望弟兄们会按约定把潜艇等在那儿。我们倒是有一样东西对我们有利——春汛正在退去。"

"而且高水位应该留下了充足的木筏材料。"阿什又躺了回去。"明天我们走完那五英里。"

===Original===
McNeil stirred uneasily and Ross, having cleaned and spitted the hares, swung them over the flames to broil. "Five miles in this country," the younger man observed, "is a pretty good day's march"﻿—he did not add as he wanted to﻿—"for a well man."

"I will make it," Ashe promised, and both listeners knew that as long as his body would obey him he meant to keep that promise. They also knew the futility of argument.

Ashe proved to be a prophet to be honored on two counts. They did make the trek to the river the next day, and there was a wealth of raft material marking the high-water level of the spring flood. The migrations McNeil had reported were still in progress, and the three men hid twice to watch the passing of small family clans. Once a respectably sized tribe, including wounded men, marched across their route, seeking a ford at the river.

===Chinese===
麦克尼尔不安地挪了挪，罗斯清理好野兔，串上烤架，架在火焰上炙烤。"五英里在这种地方，"年轻人说，"是相当不错的日行里程了"——他没有加上他想说的——"对一个健康人而言。"

"我会走完的，"阿什保证道。两个听众都知道，只要他的身体还能听从他的意志，他就打算兑现这个承诺。他们也知道争辩是徒劳的。

阿什证明了自己是一位值得尊敬的先知，而且有两点。第二天他们确实走到了河边，而且春汛高水位处留下了充足的木筏材料。麦克尼尔报告的迁徙仍在进行，三个人两次藏起来观察小家族部落的经过。一次是一个规模可观的部落，包括伤员，从他们面前走过，寻找河上的浅滩。

===Original===
"They've been badly mauled," McNeil whispered as they watched the people huddled along the water's edge while scouts cast upstream and down, searching for a ford. When they returned with the news that there was no ford to be found, the tribesmen then sullenly went to work with flint axes and knives to make rafts.

"Pressure﻿—they are on the run." Ashe rested his chin on his good forearm and studied the busy scene. "These are not from the village. Notice the dress and the red paint on their faces. They're not like Ulffa's kin either. I wouldn't say they were local at all."

"Reminds me of something I saw once﻿—animals running before a forest fire. They can't all be looking for new hunting territory," McNeil returned.

"Reds sweeping them out," Ross suggested. "Or could the ship people﻿—?"

===Chinese===
"他们被打得很惨，"麦克尼尔低声说，他们看着那些人挤在水边，侦察兵上下游搜寻浅滩。当侦察兵回来报告找不到浅滩时，部落人闷闷不乐地开始用燧石斧和刀子制造木筏。

"压力——他们在逃亡。"阿什把下巴搁在没受伤的前臂上，审视着眼前忙碌的景象。"这些人不是村里的人。注意他们的穿着和脸上的红色颜料。他们也不像乌尔法（Ulffa）的族人。我敢说他们根本不是本地人。"

"让我想起以前见过的一幕——动物在森林大火前奔逃。他们不可能全都在寻找新的猎场，"麦克尼尔回应道。

"赤色分子把他们赶出来的，"罗斯推测道。"或者会不会是飞船人（the ship people）——？"

===Original===
Ashe started to shake his head and then winced. "I wonder﻿ ﻿…" The crease between his level brows deepened. "The ax people!" His voice was still a whisper, but it carried a note of triumph as if he had fitted some stubborn jigsaw piece into its proper place.

"Ax people?"

"Invasion of another people from the east. They turned up in prehistory about this period. Remember, Webb spoke of them. They used axes for weapons and tamed horses."

"Tartars"﻿—McNeil was puzzled﻿—"This far west?"

"Not Tartars, no. You needn't expect those to come boiling out of middle Asia for some thousands of years yet. We don't know too much about the ax people, save that they moved west from the interior plains. Eventually they crossed to Britain; perhaps they were the ancestors of the Celts who loved horses too. But in their time they were a tidal wave."

===Chinese===
阿什刚要摇头就疼得皱起了眉头。"我在想……"他平直的眉头间那道皱纹加深了。"战斧人（ax people）！"他的声音仍然是低语，但带着一丝胜利的意味，仿佛他把一块顽固的拼图碎片嵌入了正确的位置。

"战斧人？"

"来自东方的另一支民族的入侵。他们在史前这个时期出现过。记得吗，韦布（Webb）提到过他们。他们用斧头做武器，驯服了马。"

"鞑靼人——"麦克尼尔困惑了——"跑到这么西边来了？"

"不是鞑靼人，不。你不必指望那些人从中亚沸腾而出，那还要再过几千年。我们对战斧人了解不多，只知道他们从内陆平原向西迁移。最终他们渡海到了不列颠（Britain）；也许他们就是同样热爱马匹的凯尔特人（Celtic）的祖先。但在他们的时代，他们是一股浪潮。"

===Original===
"The sooner we head downstream, the better." McNeil stirred restlessly, but they knew that they must keep to cover until the tribesmen below were gone. So they lay in hiding another night, witnessing on the next morning the arrival of a smaller party of the red-painted men, again with wounded among them. At the coming of this rear guard the activity on the river bank rose close to frenzy.

The three men out of time were doubly uneasy. It was not for them to merely cross the river. They had to build a raft which would be water-worthy enough to take them downstream﻿—to the sea if they were lucky. And to build such a sturdy raft would take time, time they did not have now.

In fact, McNeil waited only until the last tribal raft was out of bow shot before he plunged down to the shore, Ross at his heels. Since they lacked even the stone tools of the tribesmen, they were at a disadvantage, and Ross found he was hands and feet for Ashe, working under the other's close direction. Before night closed in they had a good beginning and two sets of blistered hands, as well as aching backs.

===Chinese===
"越快顺流而下越好。"麦克尼尔焦躁地挪动着，但他们知道必须隐蔽到下面的部落人离开为止。于是他们又躲藏了一个夜晚，第二天早上目睹了一小批红色涂面的人到来，其中同样有伤员。这支后卫部队的出现让河岸上的活动几近疯狂。

三个穿越时间的人加倍不安。他们不能仅仅过河了事。他们必须造一只足够结实的木筏，载着他们顺流而下——运气好的话能到大海。而建造这样一艘坚固的木筏需要时间，他们现在没有的时间。

事实上，麦克尼尔只等到最后一艘部落木筏射程之外，就冲下了河岸，罗斯紧跟其后。由于他们连部落人的石制工具都没有，处于劣势，罗斯发现自己成了阿什的手和脚，在对方的密切指挥下劳作。天黑之前，他们有了一个不错的开端，外加两双起泡的手和酸痛的背。

===Original===
When it was too dark to work any longer, Ashe pointed back over the track they had followed. Marking the mountain pass was a light. It looked like fire, and if it was, it must be a big one for them to be able to sight it across this distance.

"Camp?" McNeil wondered.

"Must be," Ashe agreed. "Those who built that blaze are in such numbers that they don't have to take precautions."

"Will they be here by tomorrow?"

"Their scouts might, but this is early spring, and forage can't have been too good on the march. If I were the chief of that tribe, I'd turn aside into the meadow land we skirted yesterday and let the herds graze for a day, maybe more. On the other hand, if they need water﻿—"

"They will come straight ahead!" McNeil finished grimly. "And we can't be here when they arrive."

===Chinese===
天黑得无法再干活时，阿什指向他们来时的路。山口处有一点亮光。看起来像火，如果确实是火，那一定是一团大火，才能从这个距离看到。

"营地？"麦克尼尔猜测。

"肯定是，"阿什同意。"点那么大篝火的人数多到不需要采取防范措施。"

"他们明天能到这儿吗？"

"侦察兵也许能，但现在是初春，行军路上的牧草不会太好。如果我是那个部落的首领，我会转向昨天我们经过的那片草地，让畜群放牧一天，也许更久。另一方面，如果他们需要水——"

"他们会径直朝这边来！"麦克尼尔阴沉地把话说完。"而他们到的时候我们不能在这里。"

===Original===
Ross stretched, grimacing at the twinge of pain in his shoulders. His hands smarted and throbbed, and this was just the beginning of their task. If Ashe had been fit, they might have trusted to logs for support and swum downstream to hunt a safer place for their shipbuilding project. But he knew that Ashe could not stand such an effort.

Ross slept that night mainly because his body was too exhausted to let him lie awake and worry. Roused in the earliest dawn by McNeil, they both crawled down to the water's edge and struggled to bind stubbornly resisting saplings together with cords twisted from bark. They reinforced them at crucial points with some strings torn from their kilts, and strips of rabbit hide saved from their kills of the past few days. They worked with hunger gnawing at them, having no time now to hunt. When the sun was well westward they had a clumsy craft which floated sluggishly. Whether it would answer to either pole or improvised paddle, they could not know until they tried it.

===Chinese===
罗斯伸了个懒腰，肩膀上的刺痛让他龇了龇牙。他的双手又烫又跳痛，而这仅仅是任务的开始。如果阿什身体无恙，他们本可以抱着原木顺流而下，去找一个更安全的地方进行造船工程。但他知道阿什经不起那样的消耗。

罗斯那晚睡着了，主要是因为身体疲惫到不允许他躺着发愁。麦克尼尔在黎明最早的时候叫醒了他，两人爬到水边，费力地把倔强抵抗的树苗用树皮拧成的绳子捆在一起。他们用从束腰外衣上撕下的布条和过去几天猎获的兔皮条在关键位置加固。他们忍着饥饿干活，现在没有时间去打猎。太阳偏西的时候，他们有了一只笨拙的水上载具，浮在水面上慢吞吞的。它是否能用撑杆或临时桨来操控，只有试了才知道。

===Original===
Ashe, his face flushed and his skin hot to the touch, crawled on board and lay in the middle, on the thin heap of bedding they had put there for him. He eagerly drank the water they carried to him in cupped hands and gave a little sigh of relief as Ross wiped his face with wet grass, muttering something about Kelgarries which neither of his companions understood.

McNeil shoved off and the bobbing craft spun around dizzily as the current pulled it free from the shore. They made a brave start, but luck deserted them before they had gotten out of sight of the spot where they embarked.

Striving to keep them in mid-current, McNeil poled furiously, but there were too many rocks and snagged trees projecting from the banks. Sharing that sweep of water with them, and coming up fast, was a full-sized tree. Twice its mat of branches caught on some snag, holding it back, and Ross breathed a little more freely, but it soon tore free again and rolled on, as menacing as a battering ram.

===Chinese===
阿什面色潮红，皮肤摸着发烫，他爬上了木筏，躺在中间他们为他铺好的薄薄一层铺盖上。他急切地喝着他们用手捧来的水，罗斯用湿草给他擦脸时，他轻轻舒了一口气，嘴里嘟囔着什么关于凯尔加里斯的话，两个同伴都没听明白。

麦克尼尔撑离河岸，颠簸的载具在水流的拉动下晕头转向地旋转起来。他们有一个不错的开端，但在出发点还没有完全脱离视线之前，运气就抛弃了他们。

麦克尼尔拼命撑杆试图让他们保持在水流中央，但岸边伸出的岩石和卡住的树木太多了。与他们共享这股水流的，还有一棵完整的大树，正飞速靠近。它的枝叶丛两次被什么东西挂住，拖慢了速度，罗斯稍微松了口气，但它很快又挣脱了，继续翻滚向前，威胁如同攻城槌。

===Original===
"Get closer to shore!" Ross shouted the warning. Those great, twisted roots seemed aimed straight at the raft, and he was sure if that mass struck them fairly, they would not have a chance. He dug in with his own pole, but his hasty push did not meet bottom; the stake in his hands plunged into some pothole in the hidden river bed. He heard McNeil cry out as he toppled into the water, gasping as the murky liquid flooded his mouth, choking him.

Half dazed by the shock, Ross struck out instinctively. The training at the base had included swimming, but to fight water in a pool under controlled conditions was far different from fighting death in a river of icy water when one had already swallowed a sizable quantity of that flood.

Ross had a half glimpse of a dark shadow. Was it the edge of the raft? He caught at it desperately, skinning his hands on rough bark, dragged on by it. The tree! He blinked his eyes to clear them of water, to try to see. But he could not pull his exhausted body high enough out of the water to see past the screen of roots; he could only cling to the small safety he had found and hope that he could rejoin the raft somewhere downstream.

===Chinese===
"靠近岸边！"罗斯大声发出警告。那些巨大而扭曲的树根仿佛直直瞄准了木筏，他确信如果那一大团撞个正着，他们就完了。他急忙用撑杆撑底，但匆忙的一推没有碰到河底；手中的杆子插进了隐藏河床上的某个坑洞。他听到麦克尼尔一声惊叫，人已经翻进了水里，浑浊的河水灌进他的嘴里，他呛得无法呼吸。

冲击让他半眩晕，罗斯本能地划起水来。基地的训练包括游泳，但在受控条件下在水池里和水搏斗，与在冰冷的河水里、在已经吞入大量洪水之后与死亡搏斗，完全是两回事。

罗斯隐约瞥见一个暗影。是木筏的边缘吗？他拼命抓住，手被粗糙的树皮擦破了皮，被它拖着走。是那棵树！他眨了眨眼睛，想把水从眼前抹去，想看清楚。但他无法把精疲力竭的身体抬得足够高，无法越过根须的屏障看到外面；他只能抓住这仅有的安全，希望能在下游某处与木筏重新会合。

===Original===
After what seemed like a very long time he wedged one arm between two water-washed roots, sure that the support would hold his head above the surface. The chill of the stream struck at his hands and head, but the protection of the alien clothing was still effective, and the rest of his body was not cold. He was simply too tired to wrest himself free and trust again to the haphazard chance of making shore through the gathering dusk.

Suddenly a shock jarred his body and strained the arm he had thrust among the roots, wringing a cry out of him. He swung around and brushed footing under the water; the tree had caught on a shore snag. Pulling loose from the roots, he floundered on his hands and knees, falling afoul of a mass of reeds whose roots were covered with stale-smelling mud. Like a wounded animal he dragged himself through the ooze to higher land, coming out upon an open meadow flooded with moonlight.

===Chinese===
过了仿佛很久很久之后，他把一只手臂楔入两根被水冲刷过的树根之间，确信这个支撑能把他的头保持在水面之上。溪水的冰冷刺痛着他的双手和头部，但外星衣料的防护仍然有效，身体其余部分并不冷。他只是太累了，无力挣脱束缚，也不愿再把命运交给暮色中靠运气靠岸的渺茫可能。

突然一阵冲击震颤了他的身体，拉扯了他伸进树根间的手臂，逼得他喊出一声。他转过身来，脚在水下碰到了地面；那棵树卡在了岸边的什么东西上。他从树根中挣脱出来，手脚并用地爬行，陷入了一丛根部覆盖着发臭泥浆的芦苇。像一只受伤的动物一样，他拖着自己穿过泥沼，爬上较高的地面，来到一片洒满月光的开阔草地。

===Original===
For a while he lay there, his cold, sore hands under him, plastered with mud and too tired to move. The sound of a sharp barking aroused him﻿—an imperative, summoning bark, neither belonging to a wolf nor a hunting fox. He listened to it dully and then, through the ground upon which he lay, Ross felt as well as heard the pounding of hoofs.

Hoofs﻿—horses! Horses from over the mountains﻿—horses which might mean danger. His mind seemed as dull and numb as his hands, and it took quite a long time for him to fully realize the menace horses might bring.

Getting up, Ross noticed a winged shape sweeping across the disk of the moon like a silent dart. There was a single despairing squeak out of the grass about a hundred feet away, and the winged shape arose again with its prey. Then the barking sound once more﻿—eager, excited barking.

===Chinese===
他躺在那里好一阵子，冰冷酸痛的手压在身下，浑身糊满泥浆，累得动弹不得。一阵尖锐的犬吠声惊醒了他——那是命令式的、召唤式的叫声，既不属于狼也不属于猎狐。他迟钝地听着，然后，透过他躺着的大地，罗斯既听到了也感觉到了蹄声的震颤。

蹄声——马！从山那边来的马——可能意味着危险的马。他的头脑和双手一样迟钝麻木，花了相当长的时间才完全意识到马匹可能带来的威胁。

站起身时，罗斯注意到一个带翅膀的影子像一支无声的飞镖掠过月盘。大约一百英尺外的草丛中传来一声绝望的吱叫，那带翅膀的影子带着猎物再次升起。然后又是犬吠声——急切的、兴奋的吠叫。

===Original===
Ross crouched back on his heels and saw a smoky brand of light moving along the edge of the meadow where the band of trees began. Could it be a herd guard? Ross knew he had to head back toward the river, but he had to force himself on the path, for he did not know whether he dared enter the stream again. But what would happen if they hunted him with the dog? Confused memories of how water spoiled scent spurred him on.

Having reached the rising bank he had climbed so laboriously before, Ross miscalculated and tumbled back, rolling down into the mud of the reed bed. Mechanically he wiped the slime from his face. The tree was still anchored there; by some freak the current had rammed its rooted end up on a sand spit.

===Chinese===
罗斯蹲回脚跟，看到一团烟熏火把般的亮光沿着草地边缘移动，那里是林带开始的地方。会是牧人吗？罗斯知道他必须回到河边，但他得逼自己上路，因为他不确定自己是否还敢再进那条溪流。但如果他们带着狗来追他呢？关于水能破坏气味的模糊记忆催促着他前进。

到了那道他费力爬过的高岸时，罗斯判断失误，又滚了回去，跌落在芦苇丛的泥地里。他机械地抹去脸上的粘液。那棵树还锚在那里；一个奇怪的意外，水流把它带根的一端冲上了一个沙嘴。

===Original===
Above in the meadow the barking sounded very close, and now it was answered by a second canine belling. Ross wormed his way back through the reeds to the patch of water between the tree and the bank. His few poor efforts at escape were almost half-consciously taken; he was too tired to really care now.

Soon he saw a four-footed shape running along the top of the bank, giving tongue. It was then joined by a larger and even more vocal companion. The dogs drew even with Ross, who wondered dully if the animals could sight him in the shadows below, or whether they only scented his presence. Had he been able, he would have climbed over the log and taken his chances in the open water, but now he could only lie where he was﻿—the tangle of roots between him and the bank serving as a screen, which would be little enough protection when men came with torches.

===Chinese===
上方草地里的犬吠声已经非常近了，现在又有一只狗的叫声加入应和。罗斯蠕动着穿过芦苇丛，回到树和河岸之间的那片水域。他那几次可怜的逃亡尝试几乎是半下意识地做出的；他太累了，已经顾不上了。

很快他看到一个四足的身影沿着岸顶跑过，大声吠叫。接着一只更大、更聒噪的同伴加入了进来。两条狗跑到了和罗斯齐平的位置，他迟钝地想：它们是能在下面的阴影里看到他，还是只嗅到了他的存在？如果他还有一点力气，他会翻过原木到开阔水面上碰碰运气，但现在他只能躺在原地——树根和河岸之间的纠缠体充当了一道屏障，当人们举着火把到来时，这道屏障也保护不了多少。

===Original===
Ross was mistaken, however, for his worm's progress across the reed bed had liberally besmeared his dark clothing and masked the skin of his face and hands, giving him better cover than any he could have wittingly devised. Though he felt naked and defenseless, the men who trailed the hounds to the river bank, thrusting out the torch over the edge to light the sand spit, saw nothing but the trunk of the tree wedged against a mound of mud.

Ross heard a confused murmur of voices broken by the clamor of the dogs. Then the torch was raised out of line of his dazzled eyes. He saw one of the indistinct figures above cuff away a dog and move off, calling the hounds after it. Reluctantly, still barking, the animals went. Ross, with a little sob, subsided limply in the uncomfortable net of roots, still undiscovered.

===Chinese===
然而罗斯错了，因为他匍匐穿过芦苇丛的过程已经把深色衣服涂满了泥浆，掩盖了面部和双手的肤色，给了他比任何有意设计都更好的伪装。尽管他觉得自己赤裸而毫无防备，但那些牵着猎犬来到河岸的人——把火把伸出崖边照亮沙嘴——除了看到一棵卡在泥堆上的树干之外，什么也没看到。

罗斯听到一片混乱的低语声，被狗的喧闹打断。接着火把被举高，离开了刺痛他双眼的视线。他看到上方一个模糊的身影打了一下狗，转身走开，叫猎犬跟上。狗不情愿地、仍然吠叫着跟着走了。罗斯轻轻抽泣了一声，无力地瘫倒在树根那不舒服的网中，仍然没有被发现。

## XV / XV
===Original===
It was such a small thing, a tag of ragged stuff looped about a length of splintered sapling. Ross climbed stiffly over the welter of drift caught on the sand spit and pulled it loose, recognizing the string even before he touched it. That square knot was of McNeil’s tying, and as Murdock sat down weakly in the sand and mud, nervously fingering the twisted cord, staring vacantly at the river, his last small hope died. The raft must have broken up, and neither Ashe nor McNeil could have survived the ultimate disaster.

===Chinese===
那东西如此微小，只是一块破布条，系在一截断裂的幼树枝上。罗斯僵硬地爬过沙嘴上堆积的漂浮物，把它拽了下来，甚至在触摸到它之前就认出了那根绳子。那个方结是麦克尼尔（McNeil）打的。当默多克（Murdock）虚弱地瘫坐在沙泥地里，紧张地摩挲着那根扭曲的绳索，茫然地望着河水时，他最后一点微弱的希望破灭了。木筏肯定解体了，无论是阿什（Ashe）还是麦克尼尔，都不可能在那最终的灾难中幸存。

===Original===
Ross Murdock was alone, marooned in a time which was not his own, with little promise of escape. That one thought blanked out his mind with its own darkness. What was the use of getting up again, of trying to find food for his empty stomach, or warmth and shelter?

===Chinese===
罗斯·默多克（Ross Murdock）孤身一人，流落在一个不属于他的时代，逃脱的希望渺茫。这个念头以其自身的黑暗抹去了他脑海中的所有光亮。再次站起来，为空空如也的胃寻找食物，或是寻找温暖和庇护，又有什么用呢？

===Original===
He had always prided himself on being able to go it alone, had thought himself secure in that calculated loneliness. Now that belief had been washed away in the river along with most of the will power which had kept him going these past days. Before, there had always been some goal, no matter how remote. Now, he had nothing. Even if he managed to reach the mouth of the river, he had no idea of where or how to summon the sub from the overseas post. All three of the time travelers might already have been written off the rolls, since they had not reported in.

===Chinese===
他一直为自己能独自闯荡而自豪，以为自己能在那种刻意的孤独中安然无恙。如今，这份信念连同过去几天支撑他走下去的大部分意志力，都已被河水冲走。以前，总有一个目标，无论多么遥远。现在，他一无所有。即使他设法到达河口，也不知道从何处以及如何召唤海外哨站的潜艇。三个时间旅行者可能都已被除名，因为他们没有报告。

===Original===
Ross pulled the rag free from the sapling and wreathed it in a tight bracelet about his grimed wrist for some unexplainable reason. Worn and tired, he tried to think ahead. There was no chance of again contacting Ulffa’s tribe. Along with all the other woodland hunters they must have fled before the advance of the horsemen. No, there was no reason to go back, and why make the effort to advance?

===Chinese===
罗斯把破布从幼树枝上扯下来，出于某种无法解释的原因，把它紧紧地绕在肮脏的手腕上。他疲惫不堪，试图思考前路。再次联系乌尔法（Ulffa）的部落已无可能。和其他所有林地猎人一样，他们肯定在骑兵推进之前就逃走了。不，没有理由回去，又何必费力前进呢？

===Original===
The sun was hot. This was one of those spring days which foretell the ripeness of summer. Insects buzzed in the reed banks where a green sheen showed. Birds wheeled and circled in the sky, some flock disturbed, their cries reaching Ross in hoarse calls of warning.

===Chinese===
阳光炽热。这是那种预示着盛夏来临的春日。昆虫在呈现绿色光泽的芦苇丛中嗡嗡作响。鸟儿在天空中盘旋，某个鸟群被惊扰，它们的警告叫声嘶哑地传到罗斯耳中。

===Original===
He was still plastered with patches of dried mud and slime, the reek of it thick in his nostrils. Now Ross brushed at a splotch on his knee, picking loose flakes to expose the alien cloth of his suit underneath, seemingly unbefouled. All at once it became necessary to be clean again at least.

===Chinese===
他身上仍沾着一块块干涸的泥巴和黏液，那气味浓重地刺激着他的鼻孔。现在罗斯擦去膝盖上的一块污迹，抠掉松散的碎屑，露出下面那件看似并未被玷污的外星制服布料。突然间，他觉得至少应该把自己弄干净。

===Original===
Ross waded into the stream, stooping to splash the brown water over his body and then rubbing away the resulting mud. In the sunlight the fabric had a brilliant glow, as if it not only drew the light but reflected it. Wading farther out into the water, he began to swim, not with any goal in view, but because it was easier than crawling back to land once more.

===Chinese===
罗斯涉入溪流，弯下腰将棕色的河水泼到身上，然后擦去产生的泥污。在阳光下，那布料闪烁着明亮的光芒，仿佛它不仅能吸收光线，还能反射光线。他向更深处涉去，然后开始游泳，不是有什么目标，而是因为这比再爬回岸上容易。

===Original===
Using the downstream current to supplement his skill, he watched both banks. He could not really hope to see either the raft or indications that its passengers had won to shore, but somewhere deep inside him he had not yet accepted the probable.

===Chinese===
他借助下游的水流节省体力，观察着两岸。他实际上并不指望看到木筏或木筏乘客已成功上岸的迹象，但在内心深处某个地方，他仍未接受这可能的事实。

===Original===
The effort of swimming broke through that fog of inertia which had held him since he had awakened that morning. It was with a somewhat healthier interest in life that Ross came ashore again on an arm of what was a bay or inlet angling back into the land. Here the banks of the river were well above his head, and believing that he was well sheltered, he stripped, hanging his suit in the sunlight and letting the unusual heat of the day soothe his body.

===Chinese===
游泳的努力驱散了自那天早晨醒来以来一直笼罩着他的惰性迷雾。当罗斯再次上岸时，他对生活重新燃起了些许更为健康的兴趣。他上岸的地方是一个海湾或河口的支流，斜斜地伸向内陆。这里的河岸高过他的头顶，他相信自己得到了很好的遮蔽，于是脱掉衣服，将制服挂在阳光下，让那天异常的热量抚慰他的身体。

===Original===
A raw fish, cornered in the shall浅 and scooped out, furnished one of the best meals he had ever tasted. He had reached for the suit draped over a willow limb when the first and only warning that his fortunes had once again changed came, swiftly, silently, and with deadly promise.

===Chinese===
一条生鱼，在浅滩被逼入死角后被他捞起，成了他吃过的最美味的一餐之一。他伸手去够挂在柳树枝上的制服，这时，他命运再次改变的第一个、也是唯一一个警告来临了——迅疾、无声，带着致命的威胁。

===Original===
One moment the willows had moved gently in the breeze, and then a spear suddenly set them all quivering. Ross, clutching the suit to him with a frantic grab, skated about in the sand, going to one knee in his haste.

===Chinese===
柳树前一刻还在微风中轻轻摇曳，下一刻一支长矛突然刺来，震得它们一阵颤抖。罗斯惊慌地一把抓过制服，在沙地上踉跄转身，匆忙中单膝跪倒。

===Original===
He found himself completely at the mercy of the two men standing on the bank well above him. Unlike Ulffa’s people or the Beaker traders, they were very tall, with heavy braids of light or sun-bleached hair swinging forward on their wide chests. Their leather tunics hung to mid-thigh above leggings which were bound to their limbs with painted straps. Cuff bracelets of copper ringed their forearms, and necklaces of animal teeth and beads displayed their personal wealth. Ross could not remember having seen their like on any of the briefing tapes at the base.

===Chinese===
他发现自己完全受制于站在上方河岸上的两个男人。与乌尔法的族人或比克尔（Beaker）商人不同，他们身材非常高大，浅色或晒得褪色的头发编成粗重的辫子，在宽阔的胸前摆动。他们的皮上衣垂至大腿中部，下面是用彩绘皮带绑在腿上的绑腿。铜制的护腕环绕着他们的前臂，动物牙齿和珠子串成的项链展示着个人财富。罗斯想不起来在基地的任何简报录像带中见过他们这样的人。

===Original===
One spear had been a warning, but a second was held ready, so Ross made the age-old signal of surrender, reluctantly dropping his suit and raising his hands palm out and shoulder high.

===Chinese===
一支长矛是警告，另一支也蓄势待发，于是罗斯做出了古老的投降姿态，不情愿地丢下制服，举起双手，掌心向外，与肩同高。

===Original===
“Friend?” Ross asked in the Beaker tongue. The traders ranged far, and perhaps there was a chance they had had contact with this tribe.

===Chinese===
“朋友？”罗斯用比克尔语（Beaker tongue）问道。商人们活动范围很广，也许有机会他们曾与这个部落有过接触。

===Original===
The spear twirled, and the younger stranger effortlessly leaped down the bank, paddling over to Ross to pick up the suit he had dropped, holding it up while he made some comment to his companion. He seemed fascinated by the fabric, pulling and smoothing it between his hands, and Ross wondered if there was a chance of trading it for his own freedom.

===Chinese===
长矛转了一圈，那个年轻的陌生人毫不费力地跳下河岸，蹚水走到罗斯身边，捡起他丢下的制服，一边向同伴说着什么，一边将制服举起来。他似乎被那布料迷住了，用双手拉扯、抚平，罗斯思忖着是否有用它换取自身自由的机会。

===Original===
Both men were armed, not only with the long-bladed daggers favored by the Beaker folk, but also with axes. When Ross made a slight effort to lower his hands the man before him reached to his belt ax, growling what was plainly a warning. Ross blinked, realizing that they might well knock him out and leave him behind, taking the suit with them.

===Chinese===
两人都带着武器，不仅有比克尔人偏爱的长刃匕首，还有斧头。当罗斯试图稍微放下手时，他面前的男人伸手去够腰间的斧头，咆哮着发出明显的警告。罗斯眨了眨眼，意识到他们很可能把他打晕丢下，然后带走制服。

===Original===
Finally, they decided in favor of including him in their loot. Throwing the suit over one arm, the stranger caught Ross by the shoulder and pushed him forward roughly. The pebbled beach was painful to Ross’s feet, and the breeze which whipped about him as he reached the top of the bank reminded him only too forcibly of his ordeal in the glacial world.

===Chinese===
最终，他们决定连同他本人一起作为战利品带走。陌生人将制服搭在一只手臂上，抓住罗斯的肩膀，粗暴地推着他往前走。鹅卵石海滩硌得罗斯的脚生疼，当他到达河岸顶端时，扑面而来的微风让他强烈地回想起在冰河世界的磨难。

===Original===
Murdock was tempted to make a sudden dash out on the point of the bank and dive into the river, but it was already too late. The man who was holding the spear had moved behind him, and Ross’s wrist, held in a vise grip at the small of his back, kept him prisoner as he was pushed on into the meadow. There three shaggy horses grazed, their nose ropes gathered into the hands of a third man.

===Chinese===
默多克（Murdock）曾想突然冲向河岸尽头跳入河中，但已经太迟了。那个持矛的男人已经绕到他身后，罗斯的手腕被紧紧钳在后腰，使他成为俘虏，被推入草地。那里有三匹毛发蓬乱的马在吃草，缰绳握在第三个人手里。

===Original===
A sharp stone half buried in the ground changed the pattern of the day. Ross’s heel scraped against it, and the resulting pain triggered his rebellion into explosion. He threw himself backward, his bruised heel sliding between the feet of his captor, bringing them both to the ground with himself on top. The other expelled air from his lungs in a grunt of surprise, and Ross whipped over, one hand grasping the hilt of the tribesman’s dagger while the other, free of that prisoning wrist-lock, chopped at the fellow’s throat.

===Chinese===
一块半埋在地里的尖锐石头改变了这一天的轨迹。罗斯的脚后跟擦过石头，随之而来的剧痛引爆了他的反抗。他向后猛扑，受伤的脚后跟滑到俘虏的双脚之间，将两人一起带倒在地，罗斯压在上面。那人惊讶地闷哼一声，肺里的空气被挤了出来，罗斯迅速翻身，一只手抓住部落人匕首的柄，另一只手摆脱了手腕的钳制，劈向那人的喉咙。

===Original===
Dagger out and ready, Ross faced the men in a half crouch as he had been drilled. They stared at him in open-mouthed amazement, then too late the spears went up. Ross placed the point of his looted weapon at the throat of the now quiet man by whom he knelt, and he spoke the language he had learned from Ulffa’s people.

===Chinese===
匕首出鞘在手，罗斯像受训时那样半蹲着面对那两人。他们目瞪口呆地看着他，然后长矛才迟缓地举起来。罗斯将缴获的武器尖端抵在他身旁那个已安静下来的男人的喉咙上，用他从乌尔法族人那里学来的语言说道。

===Original===
“You strike—this one dies.”

===Chinese===
“你们动手——他就死。”

===Original===
They must have read the determined purpose in his eyes, for slowly, reluctantly, the spears went down. Having gained so much of a victory, Ross dared more. “Take—” he motioned to the waiting horses—“take and go!”

===Chinese===
他们一定从他眼中读出了决绝的意图，因为长矛缓缓地、不情愿地放下了。取得了如此大的胜利，罗斯胆子更大了。“拿去——”他指着那些等待的马——“拿去，然后走！”

===Original===
For a moment he thought that this time they would meet his challenge, but he continued to hold the dagger above the brown throat of the man who was now moaning faintly. His threat continued to register, for the other man shrugged the suit from his arm, left it lying on the ground, and retreated. Holding the nose rope of his horse, he mounted, waved the herder up also, and both of them rode slowly away.

===Chinese===
有那么一刻，他以为这次他们会接受挑战，但他继续将匕首悬在那个现在正微弱呻吟的棕色喉咙上方。他的威胁持续生效，因为另一个男人从臂上抖落制服，任其落在地上，然后退开了。他牵着马的缰绳翻身上马，也示意牧马人上来，两人缓缓骑马离去。

===Original===
The prisoner was slowly coming around, so Ross only had time to pull on the suit; he had not even fastened the breast studs before those blue eyes opened. A sunburned hand flashed to a belt, but the dagger and ax which had once hung there were now in Ross’s possession. He watched the tribesman carefully as he finished dressing.

===Chinese===
俘虏正慢慢苏醒，所以罗斯只来得及套上制服；他甚至还没扣好胸前的扣子，那双蓝色的眼睛就睁开了。一只晒黑的手闪电般伸向腰带，但那里曾经挂着的匕首和斧头现在已在罗斯手中。他一边穿好衣服，一边警惕地注视着那个部落人。

===Original===
“What you do?” The words were in the speech of the forest people, distorted by a new accent.

===Chinese===
“你做什么？”这话是用森林部族的语言说的，但带着一种新的口音。

===Original===
“You go—” Ross pointed to the third horse the others had left behind—“I go—” he indicated the river—“I take these”—he patted the dagger and the ax. The other scowled.

===Chinese===
“你走——”罗斯指着另外两人留下的第三匹马——“我走——”他指了指河——“我拿这些”——他拍了拍匕首和斧头。那人皱起了眉头。

===Original===
“Not good…”

===Chinese===
“不好……”

===Original===
Ross laughed, a little hysterically. “Not good you,” he agreed, “good—me!”

===Chinese===
罗斯有点歇斯底里地笑了。“对你不好，”他赞同道，“对我——好！”

===Original===
To his surprise the tribesman’s stiff face relaxed, and the fellow gave a bark of laughter. He sat up, rubbing at his throat, a big grin pulling at the corners of his mouth.

===Chinese===
令他惊讶的是，部落人僵硬的脸放松了，那人发出一声短促的笑声。他坐起身，揉着喉咙，咧嘴大笑起来。

===Original===
“You—hunter?” The man pointed northeast to the woodlands fringing the mountains.

===Chinese===
“你——猎人？”那人指着东北方向山麓的林地。

===Original===
Ross shook his head. “Trader, me.”

===Chinese===
罗斯摇摇头。“商人，我是。”

===Original===
“Trader,” the other repeated. Then he tapped one of the wide metal cuffs at his wrist. “Trade—this?”

===Chinese===
“商人，”对方重复道。然后他敲了敲手腕上的一个宽金属护腕。“交易——这个？”

===Original===
“That. More things.”

===Chinese===
“那个。还有更多东西。”

===Original===
“Where?”

===Chinese===
“在哪？”

===Original===
Ross pointed downstream. “By bitter water—trade there.”

===Chinese===
罗斯指着下游。“在苦水边——在那里交易。”

===Original===
The man appeared puzzled. “Why you here?”

===Chinese===
那人显得很困惑。“你为什么在这里？”

===Original===
“Ride river water, like you ride,” he said, pointing to the horse. “Ride on trees—many trees tied together. Trees break apart—I come here.”

===Chinese===
“乘河水漂流，就像你骑马一样，”他指着马说。“坐在木头上——许多木头绑在一起。木头散开了——我就到了这里。”

===Original===
The conception of a raft voyage apparently got across, for the tribesman was nodding. Getting to his feet, he walked across to take up the nose rope of the waiting horse. “You come camp—Foscar. Foscar chief. He like you show trick how you take Tulka, make him sleep—hold his ax, knife.”

===Chinese===
木筏航行的概念显然被理解了，因为部落人点了点头。他站起身，走过去拿起等待的那匹马的缰绳。“你来营地——福斯卡（Foscar）。福斯卡是首领。他喜欢看你展示怎样制服图尔卡（Tulka），让他睡觉——拿走他的斧头、刀子。”

===Original===
Ross hesitated. This Tulka seemed friendly now, but would that friendliness last? He shook his head. “I go to bitter water. My chief there.”

===Chinese===
罗斯犹豫了。这个图尔卡现在看起来很友好，但这种友好会持久吗？他摇摇头。“我去苦水边。我的首领在那里。”

===Original===
Tulka was scowling again. “You speak crooked words—your chief there!” He pointed eastward with a dramatic stretch of the arm. “Your chief speak Foscar. Say he give much these”—he touched his copper cuffs—“good knives, axes—get you back.”

===Chinese===
图尔卡又皱起了眉头。“你说拐弯抹角的话——你的首领在那里！”他戏剧性地伸出手臂指向东方。“你的首领跟福斯卡说过话。说他给很多这个”——他碰了碰自己的铜护腕——“好刀子，斧头——把你带回去。”

===Original===
Ross stared at him without understanding. Ashe? Ashe in this Foscar’s camp offering a reward for him? But how could that be?

===Chinese===
罗斯不解地盯着他。阿什（Ashe）？阿什在这个福斯卡的营地里悬赏捉拿他？但怎么可能？

===Original===
“How you know my chief?”

===Chinese===
“你怎么认识我的首领？”

===Original===
Tulka laughed, this time derisively. “You wear shining skin—your chief wear shiny skin. He say find other shiny skin—give many good things to man who bring you back.”

===Chinese===
图尔卡笑了，这次是嘲笑。“你穿闪亮的皮——你的首领也穿闪亮的皮。他说找到另一张闪亮的皮——给很多好东西给带你回去的人。”

===Original===
Shiny skin! The suit from the alien ship! Was it the ship people? Ross remembered the light on him as he climbed out of the Red village. He must have been sighted by one of the spacemen. But why were they searching for him, alerting the natives in an effort to scoop him up? What made Ross Murdock so important that they must have him? He only knew that he was not going to be taken if he could help it, that he had no desire to meet this “chief” who had offered treasure for his capture.

===Chinese===
闪亮的皮！来自外星飞船的制服！是飞船人（ship people）吗？罗斯记得他爬出赤色分子（Red）村庄时照在他身上的光。他肯定被某个太空人发现了。但他们为什么要搜寻他，通知当地人来抓他？是什么让罗斯·默多克如此重要，以至于他们非得抓到他不可？他只知道，只要力所能及，他就不会被抓，他毫无兴趣去见那个为悬赏捉拿他而提供财宝的“首领”。

===Original===
“You will come!” Tulka went into action, his mount flashing forward almost in a running leap at Ross, who stumbled back when horse and rider loomed over him. He swung up the ax, but it was a weapon with which he had had no training, too heavy for him.

===Chinese===
“你必须来！”图尔卡采取了行动，他的坐骑几乎像跳跃一样冲向罗斯，当马和骑手的阴影笼罩过来时，罗斯踉跄后退。他挥起斧头，但他从未受过使用这种武器的训练，而且斧头对他来说太重了。

===Original===
As his blow met only thin air the shoulder of the mount hit him, and Ross went down, avoiding by less than a finger’s breadth the thud of an unshod hoof against his skull. Then the rider landed on him, crushing him flat. A fist connected with his jaw, and for Ross the sun went out.

===Chinese===
当他的劈砍只击中空气时，马肩撞上了他，罗斯倒了下去，一只没钉蹄铁的马蹄几乎擦着他的头骨砸下，只差不到一指的距离。接着骑手落在他身上，将他压扁。一拳击中他的下巴，罗斯眼前一黑。

===Original===
He found himself hanging across a support which moved with a rocking gait, whose pounding hurt his head, keeping him half dazed. Ross tried to move, but he realized that his arms were behind his back, fastened wrist to wrist, and a warm weight centered in the small of his spine to hold him face down on a horse. He could do nothing except endure the discomfort as best he could and hope for a speedy end to the gallop.

===Chinese===
他发现自己被横搭在一个随着颠簸而晃动的支撑物上，支撑物的撞击让他的头阵阵作痛，使他半昏半醒。罗斯试图动弹，但意识到双臂被反绑在背后，手腕铐在一起，一股温热的重量压在他脊椎下部，将他面朝下固定在一匹马上。他除了尽力忍受不适，并祈盼这疾驰尽快结束外，什么也做不了。

===Original===
Over his head passed the cackle of speech. He caught short glimpses of another horse matching pace to the one that carried him. Then they swept into a noisy place where the shouting of many men made a din. The horse stopped and Ross was pulled from its back and dropped to the trodden dust, to lie blinking up dizzily, trying to focus on the scene about him.

===Chinese===
夹杂不清的说话声从他头顶掠过。他瞥见另一匹马与他所骑的马并驾齐驱。接着他们冲进了一个嘈杂的地方，许多人的喊叫形成一片喧嚣。马停了下来，罗斯被从马背上拽下，丢在踩踏过的尘土里，他眼花缭乱地躺在地上，试图看清周围的景象。

===Original===
They had arrived at the camp of the horsemen, whose hide tents served as a backdrop for the fair long-haired giants and the tall women hovering about to view the captive. The circle about him then broke, and men stood aside for a newcomer. Ross had believed that his original captors were physically imposing, but this one was their master. Lying on the ground at the chieftain’s feet, Ross felt like a small and helpless child.

===Chinese===
他们到达了骑兵的营地，兽皮帐篷为那些浅色长发的巨人们提供了背景，高大的女人们也围拢过来观看俘虏。围在他身边的人群散开了，男人们为一位新来者让路。罗斯原以为他最初的俘虏体格已经很魁梧，但这个人才是他们的主人。躺在首领脚边的地上，罗斯感觉自己像一个弱小无助的孩子。

===Original===
Foscar, if Foscar this was, could not yet have entered middle age, and the muscles which moved along his arms and across his shoulders as he leaned over to study Tulka’s prize made him bear-strong. Ross glared up at him, that same hot rage which had led to his attack on Tulka now urging him to the only defiance he had left—words.

===Chinese===
福斯卡（如果这真是福斯卡）尚未步入中年，当他俯身打量图尔卡的战利品时，手臂和肩部活动的肌肉使他看起来像熊一样强壮。罗斯怒视着他，正是那股曾促使他攻击图尔卡的炽热愤怒，现在催逼着他进行仅剩的反抗——言语。

===Original===
“Look well, Foscar. Free me, and I would do more than *look* at you,” he said in the speech of the woods hunters.

===Chinese===
“好好看看，福斯卡。放开我，我会对你做的就不仅仅是*看*了，”他用林地猎人的语言说道。

===Original===
Foscar’s blue eyes widened and he lowered a fist which could have swallowed in its grasp both of Ross’s hands, linking those great fingers in the stuff of the suit and drawing the captive to his feet, with no sign that his act had required any effort. Even standing, Ross was a good eight inches shorter than the chieftain. Yet he put up his chin and eyed the other squarely, without giving ground.

===Chinese===
福斯卡蓝色的眼睛睁大了，他放低一只足以将罗斯两只手都吞进去的拳头，巨大的手指抓住制服的布料，将俘虏拉了起来，看不出他这个动作费了什么力气。即使站起来，罗斯也比这位首领矮了足有八英寸。然而他抬起下巴，毫不退让地直视着对方。

===Original===
“So—yet still my hands are tied.” He put into that all the taunting inflection he could summon. His reception by Tulka had given him one faint clue to the character of these people; they might be brought to acknowledge the worth of one who stood up to them.

===Chinese===
“所以——但我的手还是被绑着。”他尽可能将嘲讽的语调注入这句话。图尔卡对他的态度给了他一个关于这些人性情的微弱线索；他们可能会认可一个敢于反抗他们的人的价值。

===Original===
“Child—” The fist shifted from its grip on the fabric covering Ross’s chest to his shoulder, and now under its compulsion Ross swayed back and forth.

===Chinese===
“孩子——”拳头从抓着罗斯胸前制服的位置移到了他的肩膀上，在它的压迫下，罗斯前后摇晃起来。

===Original===
“Child?” From somewhere Ross raised that short laugh. “Ask Tulka. I be no child, Foscar. Tulka’s ax, Tulka’s knife—they were in my hand. A horse Tulka had to use to bring me down.”

===Chinese===
“孩子？”罗斯不知从哪里挤出那声短促的笑。“问问图尔卡。我不是孩子，福斯卡。图尔卡的斧头，图尔卡的刀子——它们曾在我手里。图尔卡不得不用一匹马才把我打倒。”

===Original===
Foscar regarded him intently and then grinned. “Sharp tongue,” he commented. “Tulka lost knife—ax? So! Ennar,” he called over his shoulder, and one of the men stepped out a pace beyond his fellows.

===Chinese===
福斯卡专注地看着他，然后咧嘴一笑。“尖牙利嘴，”他评论道。“图尔卡丢了刀子——斧头？好！恩纳尔（Ennar），”他回头喊道，一个男人从同伴中跨出一步。

===Original===
He was shorter and much younger than his chief, with a boy’s rangy slimness and an open, good-looking face, his eyes bright on Foscar with a kind of eager excitement. Like the other tribesmen he was armed with belt dagger and ax, and since he wore two necklaces and both cuff bracelets and upper armlets as did Foscar, Ross thought he must be a relative of the older man.

===Chinese===
他比他的首领矮一些，也年轻得多，有着少年那种修长的身材和一张开朗、英俊的脸，眼睛兴奋地闪亮着望向福斯卡，带着一种热切的期待。和其他部落人一样，他武装着腰间的匕首和斧头，而且因为他像福斯卡一样戴着两条项链、护腕和上臂环，罗斯认为他一定是这位年长者的亲戚。

===Original===
“Child!” Foscar clapped his hand on Ross’s shoulder and then withdrew the hold. “Child!” He indicated Ennar, who reddened. “You take from Ennar ax, knife,” Foscar ordered, “as you took from Tulka.” He made a sign, and someone cut the thongs about Ross’s wrists.

===Chinese===
“孩子！”福斯卡拍了拍罗斯的肩膀，然后松开了手。“孩子！”他指着恩纳尔，后者脸红了。“你从恩纳尔那里拿走斧头、刀子，”福斯卡命令道，“就像你从图尔卡那里拿走一样。”他做了个手势，有人割断了绑着罗斯手腕的皮绳。

===Original===
Ross rubbed one numbed hand against the other, setting his jaw. Foscar had stung his young follower with that contemptuous “child,” so the boy would be eager to match all his skill against the prisoner. This would not be as easy as his taking Tulka by surprise. But if he refused, Foscar might well order him killed out of hand. He had chosen to be defiant; he would have to do his best.

===Chinese===
罗斯用一只手揉搓着麻木的另一只手，咬紧了牙关。福斯卡那句轻蔑的“孩子”刺痛了他年轻的追随者，因此这少年会渴望使出浑身解数来对付俘虏。这不会像他偷袭图尔卡那么容易。但如果他拒绝，福斯卡很可能当场下令杀了他。他选择了反抗；他就必须全力以赴。

===Original===
“Take—ax, knife—” Foscar stepped back, waving at his men to open out a ring encircling the two young men.

===Chinese===
“拿去——斧头，刀子——”福斯卡后退一步，挥手示意他的手下散开，围成一个圈，将两个年轻人圈在中间。

===Original===
Ross felt a little sick as he watched Ennar’s hand go to the haft of the ax. Nothing had been said about Ennar’s not using his weapons in defense, but Ross discovered that there was some sense of sportmanship in the tribesmen, after all. It was Tulka who pushed to the chief’s side and said something which made Foscar roar bull-voiced at his youthful champion.

===Chinese===
罗斯看着恩纳尔的手伸向斧柄，感到一阵恶心。没人说过恩纳尔在防御时不能使用他的武器，但罗斯发现部落人终究还是有些竞技精神。是图尔卡挤到首领身边说了些什么，让福斯卡像公牛一样对着他年轻的冠军咆哮起来。

===Original===
Ennar’s hand came away from the ax hilt as if that polished wood were white-hot, and he transferred his discomfiture to Ross as the other understood. Ennar had to win now for his own pride’s sake, and Ross felt he had to win for his life. They circled warily, Ross watching his opponent’s eyes rather than those half-closed hands held at waist level.

===Chinese===
恩纳尔的手像被烧红的烙铁烫到一样从斧柄上弹开，并将自己的窘迫发泄到罗斯身上，后者领会了这一点。恩纳尔现在为了自己的尊严必须赢，而罗斯觉得他是为了生存必须赢。他们谨慎地绕着圈子，罗斯盯着对手的眼睛，而不是那双垂在腰际、半握着的手。

===Original===
Back at the base he had been matched with Ashe, and before Ashe with the tough-bodied, skilled, and merciless trainers in unarmed combat. He had beaten into his bruised flesh knowledge of holds and blows intended to save his skin in just such an encounter. But then he had been well-fed, alert, prepared. He had not been knocked silly and then transported for miles slung across a horse after days of exposure and hard usage. It remained to be learned—was Ross Murdock as tough as he always thought himself to be? Tough or not, he was in this until he won—or dropped.

===Chinese===
在基地时，他曾与阿什对练，在此之前还与那些身体强健、技术娴熟、毫不留情的徒手格斗教官对练。他将能在这种交锋中保命的擒拿和击打技巧，深深地打入了他伤痕累累的身体记忆中。但那时他吃得好、精神集中、准备充分。他没有被击晕，然后在经历了数日的风吹日晒和艰难跋涉后，被横搭在马背上运了好几英里。有待验证的是——罗斯·默多克是否像他一直认为的那样坚强？无论坚强与否，他已身在此局，必须赢——或者倒下。

===Original===
Comments from the crowd aroused Ennar to the first definite action. He charged, stooping low in a wrestler’s stance, but Ross squatted even lower. One hand flicked to the churned dust of the ground and snapped up again, sending a cloud of grit into the tribesman’s face. Then their bodies met with a shock, and Ennar sailed over Ross’s shoulder to skid along the earth.

===Chinese===
人群中传来的评论声激发了恩纳尔做出第一个明确的攻击动作。他冲了过来，压低身子摆出摔跤手的架势，但罗斯蹲得更低。一只手飞快地扫向被搅动的地面尘土，然后猛地扬起，将一阵沙尘扬向部落人的脸上。接着他们的身体猛烈相撞，恩纳尔从罗斯的肩头飞了出去，在地上滑行。

===Original===
Had Ross been fresh, the contest would have ended there and then in his favor. But when he tried to whirl and throw himself on his opponent he was too slow. Ennar was not waiting to be pinned flat, and it was Ross’s turn to be caught at a disadvantage.

===Chinese===
如果罗斯精力充沛，比赛当场就会以他获胜告终。但当他试图转身扑向对手时，他太慢了。恩纳尔没有等着被按倒在地，轮到罗斯处于劣势了。

===Original===
A hand shot out to catch his leg just above the ankle, and once again Ross obeyed his teaching, falling easily at that pull, to land across his opponent. Ennar, disconcerted by the too-quick success of his attack, was unprepared for this. Ross rolled, trying to escape steel-fingered hands, his own chopping out in edgewise blows, striving to serve Ennar as he had Tulka.

===Chinese===
一只手猛地伸出，抓住了他脚踝上方的小腿，罗斯再次遵循他的教诲，顺着那一拉轻松倒下，正好压在对手身上。恩纳尔被自己攻击的过快成功搞懵了，对此毫无防备。罗斯翻滚着，试图挣脱那双钢铁般的手指，自己的手则以手刀般的侧击劈出，力图像对付图尔卡那样对付恩纳尔。

===Original===
He had to take a lot of punishment, though he managed to elude the powerful bear’s hug in which he knew the other was laboring to engulf him, a hold which would speedily crush him into submission. Clinging to the methods he had been taught, he fought on, only now he knew, with a growing panic, that his best was not good enough. He was too spent to make an end. Unless he had some piece of great good luck, he could only delay his own defeat.

===Chinese===
他不得不承受许多重击，尽管他设法躲开了那熊抱式的强力钳制——他知道对方正竭力想用这招将他困住，一旦被抱住，他会迅速被碾压屈服。他紧抓着所学的方法继续战斗，只是现在他带着越来越大的恐慌知道，他已竭尽全力却仍不够。他已精疲力竭，无法终结对手。除非他交上天大的好运，否则只能拖延自己的失败。

===Original===
Fingers clawed viciously at his eyes, and Ross did what he had never thought to do in any fight—he snapped wolfishly, his teeth closing on flesh as he brought up his knee and drove it home into the body wriggling on his.
