There was a gasp of hot breath in his face as Ross called upon the last few rags of his strength, tearing loose from the other’s slackened hold. He scrambled to one knee. Ennar was also on his knees, crouching like a four-legged beast ready to spring. Ross risked everything on a last gamble. Clasping his hands together, he raised them as high as he could and brought them down on the nape of the other’s neck. Ennar sprawled forward face-down in the dust where seconds later Ross joined him.

===Chinese===
手指凶狠地抠向他的眼睛，罗斯做出了他从未在任何打斗中想到过的事——他像狼一样猛地咬了下去，牙齿陷入皮肉，同时抬起膝盖，狠狠撞进在他身上扭动的躯体。一股灼热的气息喷在他脸上，罗斯耗尽最后几丝力气，挣脱了对方松开的钳制。他挣扎着单膝跪起。恩纳尔也跪着，像一头准备扑击的四足野兽一样蹲伏着。罗斯孤注一掷，押上最后的赌注。他双手紧扣，尽可能高地举起，然后猛击在对方的后颈上。恩纳尔向前扑倒在尘土里，几秒钟后，罗斯也倒在他身边。

## XVI

===Original===
Murdock lay on his back, gazing up at the laced hides which stretched to make the tent roofing. Having been battered just enough to feel all one aching bruise, Ross had lost interest in the future. Only the present mattered, and it was a dark one. He might have fought Ennar to a standstill, but in the eyes of the horsemen he had also been beaten, and he had not impressed them as he had hoped. That he still lived was a minor wonder, but he deduced that he continued to breathe only because they wanted to exchange him for the reward offered by the aliens from out of time, an unpleasant prospect to contemplate.

His wrists were lashed over his head to a peg driven deeply into the ground; his ankles were bound to another. He could turn his head from side to side, but any further movement was impossible. He ate only bits of food dropped into his mouth by a dirty-fingered slave, a cowed hunter captured from a tribe overwhelmed in the migration of the horsemen.

"Ho—taker of axes!" A toe jarred into his ribs, and Ross bit back the grunt of pain which answered that rude bid for his attention. He saw in the dim light Ennar's face and was savagely glad to note the discolorations about the right eye and along the jaw line, the signatures left by his own skinned knuckles.

===Chinese===
默多克（Murdock）仰面躺着，抬头望着交错编织的兽皮——它们搭成了帐篷顶棚。罗斯被打得浑身酸痛，像一块淤青的大肿块，对未来的兴趣已然荡然无存。只有当下还重要，而当下是一片黑暗。他或许和恩纳尔（Ennar）打了个不分胜负，但在骑手们眼里，他同样落了败，没有如他所愿在他们心中留下印象。他居然还活着，这本身就是一个小小的奇迹，但他推测自己之所以还在呼吸，只是因为他们想拿他去换取时间之外的外星人开出的赏金——想到这个前景就不寒而栗。

他的手腕被反绑在头顶，拴在一根深深打入地面的木桩上；脚踝则绑在另一根桩子上。他只能左右转动头部，完全无法做其他动作。他只能靠一个奴隶脏兮兮的手指塞进嘴里的零星食物充饥——那个奴隶是个被俘的猎人，来自一支在骑手迁徙中被征服的部落。

"嘿——夺斧者！"一只脚趾捅进他的肋骨，罗斯强忍住了疼痛的呻咽——那是对这粗鲁的召唤注意的本能反应。昏暗的光线下他看到了恩纳尔的脸，注意到右眼和下颌线上的淤青，那是他自己擦破皮的拳头留下的印记，他对此感到一种凶狠的快意。

===Original===
"Ho—warrior!" Ross returned hoarsely, trying to lade that title with all the scorn he could summon.

Ennar's hand, holding a knife, swung into his limited range of vision. "To clip a sharp tongue is a good thing!" The young tribesman grinned as he knelt down beside the helpless prisoner.

Ross knew a thrill of fear worse than any pain. Ennar might be about to do just what he hinted! Instead, the knife swung up and Ross felt the sawing at the cords about his wrists, enduring the pain in the raw gouges they had cut in his flesh with gratitude that it was not mutilation which had brought Ennar to him. He knew that his arms were free, but to draw them down from over his head was almost more than he could do, and he lay quiet as Ennar loosed his feet.

"Up!"

===Chinese===
"嘿——勇士！"罗斯嘶哑地回敬，试图将这个头衔灌入他能召唤出的全部蔑视。

恩纳尔拿着刀的手晃入他有限的视野。"剪掉一条利舌是件好事！"年轻的部落人咧嘴一笑，在无力反抗的囚徒身旁蹲了下来。

罗斯感到了一种比任何疼痛都更强烈的恐惧。恩纳尔可能真的要做他暗示的事！然而，刀锋向上挥去，罗斯感到手腕上的绳索被锯割，粗糙的绳子在肉里磨出的伤口虽然疼痛，但他心存感激——恩纳尔来找他不是为了施以残害。他知道自己的胳膊已经自由了，但要把它们从头顶放下来几乎是他力所能及的极限，他静静躺着，等恩纳尔解开他的脚。

"起来！"

===Original===
Without Ennar's hands pulling at him, Ross could not have reached his feet. Nor did he stay erect once he had been raised, crashing forward on his face as the other let him go, hot anger eating at him because of his own helplessness.

In the end, Ennar summoned two slaves who dragged Ross into the open where a council assembled about a fire. A debate was in progress, sometimes so heated that the speakers fingered their knife or ax hilts when they shouted their arguments. Ross could not understand their language, but he was certain that he was the subject under discussion and that Foscar had the deciding vote and had not yet given the nod to either side.

Ross sat where the slaves had dumped him, rubbing his smarting wrists, so deathly weary in mind and beaten in body that he was not really interested in the fate they were planning for him. He was content merely to be free of his bonds, a small favor, but one he savored dully.

===Chinese===
没有恩纳尔拽他，罗斯根本站不起来。即使被拉起来，他也站不住，对方一松手他就面朝下摔倒了，灼热的怒火在他心中燃烧——只因自己的无助。

最终，恩纳尔叫来两个奴隶，把罗斯拖到外面，一群人围着火堆召开了一场会议。辩论正在进行，有时激烈到发言者在喊叫时会下意识地摸刀柄或斧柄。罗斯听不懂他们的语言，但他确信讨论的主题就是自己，而福斯卡（Foscar）拥有决定性的投票权，尚未向任何一方点头。

罗斯坐在奴隶把他丢下的地方，揉着刺痛的手腕，心力交瘁、身体溃败，对他们正在为他规划的命运毫无兴趣。能摆脱绳索就已经让他满足了——虽然只是一个小小的恩惠，他也迟钝地品味着。

===Original===
He did not know how long the debate lasted, but at length Ennar came to stand over him with a message. "Your chief—he give many good things for you. Foscar take you to him."

"My chief is not here," Ross repeated wearily, making a protest he knew they would not heed. "My chief sits by the bitter water and waits. He will be angry if I do not come. Let Foscar fear his anger—"

Ennar laughed. "You run from your chief. He will be happy with Foscar when you lie again under his hand. You will not like that—I think it so!"

"I think so, too," Ross agreed silently.

He spent the rest of that night lying between the watchful Ennar and another guard, though they had the humanity not to bind him again. In the morning he was allowed to feed himself, and he fished chunks of venison out of a stew with his unwashed fingers. But in spite of the messiness, it was the best food he had eaten in days.

===Chinese===
他不知道辩论持续了多久，但恩纳尔最终站到他面前，传达了一个消息。"你的首领——他愿出很多好东西换你。福斯卡要把你交给他。"

"我的首领不在这里，"罗斯疲惫地重复道，做着一个他知道无人理会的抗议。"我的首领坐在苦水边等着。如果我不回去，他会发怒的。让福斯卡畏惧他的怒火——"

恩纳尔笑了。"你从你的首领那里逃跑了。当你再次落到他手里时，他会高兴地把你的头领捧着的。你不会喜欢那样——我敢肯定！"

"我也这么想，"罗斯在心里默默同意。

那一晚余下的时间，他躺在警觉的恩纳尔和另一个看守之间度过了——好在他们还算有人性，没有再绑他。早上他被允许自己进食，用没洗过的手从炖肉里捞出大块的鹿肉。尽管邋遢，这却是他好几天来吃到的最好的食物。

===Original===
The trip, however, was not to be a comfortable one. He was mounted on one of the shaggy horses, a rope run under the animal's belly to loop one foot to the other. Fortunately, his hands were bound so he was able to grasp the coarse, wiry mane and keep his seat after a fashion. The nose rope of his mount was passed to Tulka, and Ennar rode beside him with only half an eye for the path of his own horse and the balance of his attention for the prisoner.

They headed northeast, with the mountains as a sharp green-and-white goal against the morning sky. Though Ross's sense of direction was not too acute, he was certain that they were making for the general vicinity of the hidden village, which he believed the ship people had destroyed. He tried to discover something of the nature of the contact which had been made between the aliens and the horsemen.

"How find other chief?" he asked Ennar.

===Chinese===
然而这段旅程并不舒适。他被安置在一匹毛发蓬乱的马上，一根绳子从马肚子下穿过，把他的两只脚连在一起。幸好他的手还能动，可以抓住粗糙硬扎的鬃毛，勉强坐稳。他坐骑的鼻绳交到了图尔卡（Tulka）手里，恩纳尔骑在他旁边，只用一半注意力看路，另一半留给了囚犯。

他们向东北方向行进，山脉在晨光映衬下呈现出鲜明的绿白相间的目标。虽然罗斯的方向感并不敏锐，但他确信他们正朝那座被他认为是飞船人摧毁的隐秘村落的大概方位进发。他试图打探外星人和骑手之间建立的接触的性质。

"怎么找到另一个首领？"他问恩纳尔。

===Original===
The young man tossed one of his braids back across his shoulder and turned his head to face Ross squarely. "Your chief come our camp. Talk with Foscar—two—four sleeps ago."

"How talk with Foscar? With hunter talk?"

For the first time Ennar did not appear altogether certain. He scowled and then snapped, "He talk—Foscar, us. We hear right words—not woods creeper talk. He speak to us good."

Ross was puzzled. How could the alien out of time speak the proper language of a primitive tribe some thousands of years removed from his own era? Were the ship people also familiar with time travel? Did they have their own stations of transfer? Yet their fury with the Reds had been hot. This was a complete mystery.

"This chief—he look like me?"

===Chinese===
年轻人把一条辫子甩回肩上，转过头正对着罗斯。"你的首领来过我们营地。和福斯卡谈过——两——四个觉之前的事。"

"怎么和福斯卡谈的？用猎人的语言？"

恩纳尔第一次显得不那么确定了。他皱起眉头，厉声说："他说话——福斯卡，我们。我们听到了正确的话语——不是林间爬虫的话。他对我们说得好。"

罗斯大惑不解。一个来自时间之外的外星人，怎么会说数千年前这个原始部落的恰当语言？飞船人也熟悉时间旅行？他们有自己的转移站？然而他们对赤色分子的愤怒又确实炽热。这完全是个谜。

"这个首领——他长得像我？"

===Original===
Again Ennar appeared at a loss. "He wear covering like you."

"But was he like me?" persisted Ross. He didn't know what he was trying to learn, only that it seemed important at that moment to press home to at least one of the tribesmen that he *was* different from the man who had put a price on his head and to whom he was to be sold.

"Not like!" Tulka spoke over his shoulder. "You look like hunter people—hair, eyes—Strange chief no hair on head, eyes not like—"

"You saw him too?" Ross demanded eagerly.

"I saw. I ride to camp—they come so. Stand on rock, call to Foscar. Make magic with fire—it jump up!" He pointed his arm stiffly at a bush before them on the trail. "They point little, little spear—fire come out of the ground and burn. They say burn our camp if we not have man. We say—not have man. Then they say many good things for us if we find and bring man—"

===Chinese===
恩纳尔再次显得不知所措。"他穿得和你一样。"

"但他像我吗？"罗斯追问。他不知道自己想打探什么，只知道在这一刻似乎非常重要的是，至少要让一个部落人确信他*与*那个悬赏要他脑袋、要把他卖去的那个人是不同的。

"不像！"图尔卡从肩头说。"你长得像猎人——头发、眼睛——那个怪异的首领头上没头发，眼睛也不像——"

"你也见过他？"罗斯急切地追问。

"见过。我骑马去营地——他们那样来了。站在岩石上，对福斯卡喊话。用火施展魔法——火就跳起来了！"他把手臂僵硬地指向前方小路旁的一丛灌木。"他们指着小小的、小小的矛——火从地里冒出来烧。他们说如果我们不交出那个人就烧我们的营地。我们说——没那个人。然后他们说如果我们找到并交出那个人就给我们很多好东西——"

===Original===
"But they are not my people," Ross cut in. "You see, I have hair, I am not like them. They are bad—"

"You may be taken in war by them—chief's slave." Ennar had a reply to that which was logical according to the customs of his own tribe. "They want slave back—it is so."

"My people strong too, much magic," Ross pushed. "Take me to bitter water and they pay much—more than stranger chief!"

Both tribesmen were amused. "Where bitter water?" asked Tulka.

===Chinese===
"但他们不是我的族人，"罗斯插嘴道。"你看，我有头发，和他们不一样。他们是坏人——"

"你也许是在战争中被他们俘虏的——首领的奴隶。"恩纳尔有一个按自己部落习俗完全合乎逻辑的回应。"他们想把奴隶要回来——就是这样。"

"我的族人也很强，有很多魔法，"罗斯继续争取。"把我带到苦水边，他们会付很多——比那个陌生的首领出的还多！"

两个部落人都被逗乐了。"苦水在哪里？"图尔卡问。

===Original===
Ross jerked his head to the west. "Some sleeps away—"

"Some sleeps!" repeated Ennar jeeringly. "We ride some sleeps, maybe many sleeps where we know not the trails—maybe no people there, maybe no bitter water—all things you say with split tongue so that we not give you back to master. We go this way not even one sleep—find chief, get good things. Why we do hard thing when we can do easy?"

What argument could Ross offer in rebuttal to the simple logic of his captors? For a moment he raged inwardly at his own helplessness. But long ago he had learned that giving way to hot fury was no good unless one did it deliberately to impress, and then only when one had the upper hand. Now Ross had no hand at all.

===Chinese===
罗斯朝西边甩了甩头。"走几个觉的路——"

"几个觉！"恩纳尔嘲弄地重复道。"我们要骑几个觉，也许很多觉，那些路我们根本不熟——也许那里没人，也许没有苦水——全是你说的谎话，好让我们不把你交还给主人。我们走这条路不到一个觉就能找到首领，拿到好东西。能做容易的事为什么要做难的？"

罗斯能用什么论据来反驳俘虏者简单而直白的逻辑？一时间他内心对自己的无助怒火中烧。但他早就明白，放任狂怒毫无益处，除非是刻意为之以震慑对方，而且前提还得自己占着上风。如今罗斯一点上风都没有了。

===Original===
For the most part they kept to the open, whereas Ross and the other two agents had skulked in wooded areas on their flight through this same territory. So they approached the mountains from a different angle, and though he tried, Ross could pick out no familiar landmarks. If by some miracle he was able to free himself from his captors, he could only head due west and hope to strike the river.

At midday their party made camp in a grove of trees by a spring. The weather was as unseasonably warm as it had been the day before, and flies, brought out of cold-weather hiding, attacked the stamping horses and crawled over Ross. He tried to keep them off with swings of his bound hands, for their bites drew blood.

Having been tumbled from his mount, he remained fastened to a tree with a noose about his neck while the horsemen built a fire and broiled strips of deer meat.

===Chinese===
他们大多走在开阔地带，而罗斯和另外两名特工穿越这片区域时走的都是隐蔽的林地。所以他们从不同的方向接近山脉，尽管罗斯努力辨认，却找不到任何熟悉的地标。如果奇迹般地能从俘虏者手中逃脱，他也只能朝正西方向走，寄希望于碰到那条河。

正午时分，一行人在泉边的一片树林里扎营。天气和昨天一样不合时宜地温暖，从冬季蛰伏中被惊醒的苍蝇扑向不停踏蹄的马匹，在罗斯身上爬来爬去。他试图用被绑着的双手挥打来驱赶它们，因为苍蝇的叮咬会吸出血来。

他被从马上摔下来后，脖子上套着绳圈拴在树上，骑手们则生火烤鹿肉条。

===Original===
It would seem that Foscar was in no hurry to get on, since after they had eaten, the men continued to lounge at ease, some even dropping off to sleep. When Ross counted faces he learned that Tulka and another had both disappeared, possibly to contact and warn the aliens they were coming.

It was midafternoon before the scouts reappeared, as unobtrusively as they had gone. They went before Foscar with a report which brought the chief over to Ross. "We go. Your chief waits—"

Ross raised his swollen, bitten face and made his usual protest. "Not my chief!"

Foscar shrugged. "He say so. He give good things to get you back under his hand. So—he your chief!"

===Chinese===
看来福斯卡并不急于赶路，因为吃完饭后众人继续悠闲地闲逛，有些人甚至打起了盹。罗斯数了数面孔，发现图尔卡和另一个人已经不见了，可能去联系并警告外星人他们要来了。

直到午后过半，侦察兵才像离开时一样悄无声息地回来了。他们向福斯卡汇报，首领随即走到罗斯面前。"我们走。你的首领在等着——"

罗斯抬起肿胀的、满是咬痕的脸，做出了惯常的抗议。"不是我的首领！"

福斯卡耸了耸肩。"他是这么说的。他出很多好东西要把你换回去。所以——他就是你的首领！"

===Original===
Once again Ross was boosted on his mount, and bound. But this time the party split into two groups as they rode off. He was with Ennar again, just behind Foscar, with two other guards bringing up the rear. The rest of the men, leading their mounts, melted into the trees. Ross watched that quiet withdrawal speculatively. It argued that Foscar did not trust those he was about to do business with, that he was taking certain precautions of his own. Only Ross could not see how that distrust, which might be only ordinary prudence on Foscar's part, could in any way be an advantage for him.

They rode at a pace hardly above a walk into a small open meadow narrowing at the east. Then for the first time Ross was able to place himself. They were at the entrance to the valley of the village, about a mile away from the narrow throat above which Ross had lain to spy and had been captured, for he had come from the north over the spurs of rising ridges.

===Chinese===
罗斯再次被扶上马，再次被绑住。但这次一行人分成了两组。他又和恩纳尔在一起，紧跟在福斯卡身后，另外两名看守押后。其余的人牵着马匹，隐入树林。罗斯若有所思地看着那次悄然撤离。这说明福斯卡不信任他即将打交道的人，正在采取一些自己的防范措施。只不过罗斯看不出这种不信任——也许只是福斯卡的寻常谨慎——对他自己能有什么好处。

他们以不超过步行速度的节奏骑行，进入了一片向东收窄的小型开阔草地。罗斯这才第一次辨明了位置。他们正处在通往村落山谷的入口处，距那条狭窄的山口约有一英里——罗斯曾在那上面卧底侦察并被俘，他是从北面翻过隆起的山脊来到那里的。

===Original===
Ross's horse was pulled up as Foscar drove his heel into the ribs of his own mount, sending it at a brisker pace toward the neck of the valley. There was a blot of blue there—more than one of the aliens were waiting. Ross caught his lip between his teeth and bit down on it hard. He had stood up to the Reds, to Foscar's tribesmen, but he shrank from meeting those strangers with an odd fear that the worst the men of his own species could do would be but a pale shadow to the treatment he might meet at their hands.

Foscar was now a toy man astride a toy horse. He halted his galloping mount to sit facing the handful of strangers. Ross counted four of them. They seemed to be talking, though there was still a good distance separating the mounted man and the blue suits.

===Chinese===
罗斯的马被勒停，福斯卡用脚跟猛踢坐骑肋下，驱策它以更快的速度奔向山谷的隘口。那里有一团蓝色的身影——不止一个外星人在等着。罗斯把嘴唇咬在牙齿之间，狠狠地咬住。他曾对抗过赤色分子，对抗过福斯卡的部落人，但想到要面对那些陌生人，他却退缩了——一种奇怪的恐惧攫住了他，仿佛人类同胞所能施加的最坏对待，与那些人可能对他做的事相比，不过是苍白的影子。

福斯卡此刻成了玩具人骑在玩具马上。他勒住疾驰的坐骑，面对那一小群陌生人。罗斯数了数，有四个人。他们似乎在交谈，虽然骑马的人和蓝色制服之间还隔着相当一段距离。

===Original===
Minutes passed before Foscar's arm raised in a wave to summon the party guarding Ross. Ennar kicked his horse to a trot, towing Ross's mount behind, the other two men thudding along more discreetly. Ross noted that they were both armed with spears which they carried to the fore as they rode.

They were perhaps three quarters of the way to join Foscar, and Ross could see plainly the bald heads of the aliens as their faces turned in his direction. Then the strangers struck. One of them raised a weapon shaped similarly to the automatic Ross knew, except that it was longer in the barrel.

Ross did not know why he cried out, except that Foscar had only an ax and dagger which were both still sheathed at his belt. The chief sat very still, and then his horse gave a swift sidewise swerve as if in fright. Foscar collapsed, limp, bonelessly, to the trodden turf, to lie unmoving face down.

===Chinese===
几分钟后福斯卡抬起手臂挥了挥，召唤押送罗斯的队伍上前。恩纳尔踢马小跑，拉着罗斯的马跟在后面，另外两人低调地小跑着跟上。罗斯注意到两人都手持长矛，骑马时将矛尖朝前。

他们大约走了四分之三的路程快与福斯卡会合时，罗斯清晰地看到了外星人光秃秃的头颅——他们正转脸朝他的方向看来。然后陌生人动手了。其中一人举起一种武器，形状类似于罗斯所知的自动手枪，但枪管更长。

罗斯不知道自己为什么叫出了声——也许是因为福斯卡只有斧头和匕首，两者都还插在腰间的鞘里。首领一动不动地坐着，接着他的马猛然向一侧闪避，仿佛受到了惊吓。福斯卡瘫软无力地倒下，软塌塌地扑在踩实的草地上，面朝下一动不动。

===Original===
Ennar whooped, a cry combining defiance and despair in one. He reined up with violence enough to set his horse rearing. Then, dropping his hold on the lead rope of Ross's mount, he whirled and set off in a wild dash for the trees to the left. A spear lanced across Ross's shoulder, ripping at the blue fabric, but his horse whirled to follow the other, taking him out of danger of a second thrust. Having lost his opportunity, the man who had wielded the spear dashed by at Ennar's back.

Ross clung to the mane with both hands. His greatest fear was that he might slip from the saddle pad and since he was tied by his feet, lie unprotected and helpless under those dashing hoofs. Somehow he managed to cling to the horse's neck, his face lashed by the rough mane while the animal pounded on. Had Ross been able to grasp the dangling nose rope, he might have had a faint chance of controlling that run, but as it was he could only hold fast and hope.

===Chinese===
恩纳尔发出一声嚎叫，其中混合着挑衅与绝望。他猛拉缰绳，力度之大使马匹扬起了前蹄。随后他松开罗斯坐骑的牵引绳，猛转身向左边的树林狂奔而去。一支矛擦过罗斯的肩膀，撕裂了蓝色衣料，但他的马转身跟随另一匹马，将他从第二记突刺的危险中带离。失去了机会的掷矛者从恩纳尔背后疾驰而过。

罗斯双手紧紧抓住鬃毛。他最大的恐惧是从马鞍垫上滑下去——由于双脚被绑着，一旦跌落就会毫无防护地暴露在疾驰的蹄子下面。他设法紧紧抱住马的脖子，粗糙的鬃毛抽打着他的脸，马匹狂奔不止。如果罗斯能抓住那根晃荡的鼻绳，或许还有一线控制奔逃的可能，但眼下他只能死死抓住，听天由命。

===Original===
He had only broken glimpses of what lay ahead. Then a brilliant fire, as vivid as the flames which had eaten up the Red village, burst from the ground a few yards ahead, sending the horse wild. There was more fire and the horse changed course through the rising smoke. Ross realized that the aliens were trying to cut him off from the thin safety of the woodlands. Why they didn't just shoot him as they had Foscar he could not understand.

The smoke of the burning grass was thick, cutting between him and the woods. Might it also provide a curtain behind which he could hope to escape both parties? The fire was sending the horse back toward the waiting ship people. Ross could hear a confused shouting in the smoke. Then his mount made a miscalculation, and a tongue of red licked too close. The animal screamed, dashing on blindly straight between two of the blazes and away from the blue-clad men.

===Chinese===
他对前方的情景只有一些零碎的瞥见。接着，一团炫目的火焰——如同吞噬赤色分子村庄的火焰一样明亮——在前方几码处从地面爆开，马匹顿时陷入狂乱。更多的火焰腾起，马匹在升腾的烟雾中改变了方向。罗斯意识到外星人正试图切断他与那片稀疏林地的安全之间的联系。他们为什么不直接像射杀福斯卡那样开枪，他无法理解。

燃烧的草冒出浓烟，隔在他和树林之间。但这烟雾是否也能提供一道屏障，让他有机会逃过双方的追捕？大火正将马匹赶向等候在那里的飞船人。罗斯能在烟雾中听到混乱的喊叫。随后他的坐骑判断失误，一条火舌舔得太近。马匹嘶鸣着，盲目地直冲两团火焰之间，远离了穿蓝色制服的人。

===Original===
Ross coughed, almost choking, his eyes watering as the stench of singed hair thickened the smoke. But he had been carried out of the fire circle and was shooting back into the meadowland. Mount and unwilling rider were well away from the upper end of that cleared space when another horse cut in from the left, matching speed to the uncontrolled animal to which Ross clung. It was one of the tribesmen riding easily.

The trick worked, for the wild race slowed to a gallop and the other rider, in a feat of horsemanship at which Ross marveled, leaned from his seat to catch the dangling nose rope, bringing the runaway against his own steady steed. Ross shaken, still coughing from the smoke and unable to sit upright, held to the mane. The gallop slowed to a rocking pace and finally came to a halt, both horses blowing, white-foam patches on their chests and their riders' legs.

===Chinese===
罗斯剧烈咳嗽，几乎窒息，烧焦毛发的恶臭使烟雾更加浓重，熏得他眼泪直流。但他已经被带出了火圈，正疾驰回开阔的草地上。当他和不情愿的骑手远离了那片空地的上端时，另一匹马从左侧切入，与罗斯所骑的失控马匹并驾齐驱。那是一个骑术娴熟的部落人。

这一招奏效了——狂奔减速成了疾驰，另一个骑手以令罗斯惊叹的骑术从马鞍上俯身抓住了晃荡的鼻绳，将失控的马牵向自己稳稳的坐骑旁边。罗斯浑身颤抖，仍在咳嗽，无法坐直，只能抓住鬃毛。疾驰逐渐减速成了摇晃的步伐，最终停下，两匹马都在喘息，胸口和主人腿上沾着白色的泡沫。

===Original===
Having made his capture, the tribesman seemed indifferent to Ross, looking back instead at the wide curtain of grass smoke, frowning as he studied the swift spread of the fire. Muttering to himself, he pulled the lead rope and brought Ross's horse to follow in the direction from which Ennar had brought the captive less than a half hour earlier.

Ross tried to think. The unexpected death of their chief might well mean his own, should the tribe's desire for vengeance now be aroused. On the other hand, there was a faint chance that he could now better impress them with the thought that he was indeed of another clan and that to aid him would be to work against a common enemy.

But it was hard to plan clearly, though wits alone could save him now. The parley which had ended with Foscar's murder had brought Ross a small measure of time. He was still a captive, even though of the tribesmen and not the unearthly strangers. Perhaps to the ship people these primitives were hardly higher in scale than the forest animals.

===Chinese===
完成了抓捕之后，那个部落人似乎对罗斯漠不关心，反而回头看着那片宽广的草烟幕帘，皱着眉审视火势的迅速蔓延。他自言自语地嘟囔着，拽起牵引绳，让罗斯的马跟着他朝恩纳尔不到半小时前带俘虏走来的方向前进。

罗斯试图思考。首领的意外死亡很可能意味着他自己的死——如果部落的复仇欲望被激起的话。另一方面，他也许有一个微弱的机会，能让这些部落人更确信他确实属于另一个部族，帮助他就等于对付一个共同的敌人。

但要理清思路并不容易，尽管此刻只有头脑才能救他。那场以福斯卡被谋杀而告终的谈判，为罗斯争取到了一点时间。他仍然是俘虏——不过是被部落人俘虏，而非被那些来自异世界的陌生人。或许在飞船人眼中，这些原始人与森林里的野兽相比高不了多少。

===Original===
Ross did not try to talk to his present guard, who towed him into the western sun of late afternoon. They halted at last in that same small grove where they had rested at noon. The tribesman fastened the mounts and then walked around to inspect the animal Ross had ridden. With a grunt he loosened the prisoner and spilled him unceremoniously on the ground while he examined the horse. Ross levered himself up to sight the mark of the burn across that roan hide where the fire had blistered the skin.

Thick handfuls of mud from the side of the spring were brought and plastered over the seared strip. Then, having rubbed down both animals with twists of grass, the man came over to Ross, pushed him back to the ground, and studied his left leg.

===Chinese===
罗斯没有试图和当前的看守说话，后者牵着他走向西下的斜阳。他们最终在正午歇息过的那片小树林停了下来。部落人拴好坐骑，然后绕过去检查罗斯骑过的那匹马。他哼了一声，解开囚犯，不客气地把他扔在地上，自己则去检查那匹马。罗斯撑起身子，看到了火焰灼伤花斑马皮的地方留下的一道烧灼痕迹。

他从泉边捧来大把大把的泥巴，厚厚地敷在烧伤带上。然后用草绳把两匹马都擦拭干净后，他走到罗斯身边，把他推倒在地，研究起他的左腿。

===Original===
Ross understood. By rights, his thigh should also have been scorched where the flame had hit, yet he had felt no pain. Now as the tribesman examined him for a burn, he could not see even the faintest discoloration of the strange fabric. He remembered how the aliens had strolled unconcerned through the burning village. As the suit had insulated him against the cold of the ice, so it would seem that it had also protected him against the fire, for which he was duly thankful. His escape from injury was a puzzle to the tribesman, who, failing to find any trace of burn on him, left Ross alone and went to sit well away from his prisoner as if he feared him.

They did not have long to wait. One by one, those who had ridden in Foscar's company gathered at the grove. The very last to come were Ennar and Tulka, carrying the body of their chief. The faces of both men were smeared with dust and when the others sighted the body they, too, rubbed dust into their cheeks, reciting a string of words and going one by one to touch the dead chieftain's right hand.

===Chinese===
罗斯明白了。按理说，火焰灼到他大腿的地方也应该被烧伤，但他没有感到疼痛。如今部落人检查他是否有烧伤，甚至在那件奇异的织物上连最轻微的变色都看不到。他想起外星人曾经若无其事地走过燃烧的村庄。正如这套衣服曾经帮他隔绝了冰天雪地的严寒，看来它也保护了他免于火焰——对此他由衷地感激。他的毫发无伤让部落人大惑不解，找遍了也找不到任何烧伤的痕迹后，部落人丢下罗斯不管，走到离囚犯远远的地方坐下，仿佛害怕他似的。

他们没有等太久。福斯卡一行的骑手们一个接一个地聚集到树林里。最后到来的是恩纳尔和图尔卡，抬着他们首领的尸体。两人的脸上都抹了灰土，当其他人看到尸体时，也纷纷把灰土涂在脸颊上，念诵着一串串词句，逐一上前触碰死去首领的右手。

===Original===
Ennar, resigning his burden to the others, slid from his tired horse and stood for a long moment, his head bowed. Then he gazed straight at Ross and came across the tiny clearing to stand over the man of a later time. The boyishness which had been a part of him when he had fought at Foscar's command was gone. His eyes were merciless as he leaned down to speak, shaping each word with slow care so that Ross could understand the promise—that frightful promise:

"Woods rat, Foscar goes to his burial fire. And he shall take a slave with him to serve him beyond the sky—a slave to run at his voice, to shake when he thunders. Slave-dog, you shall run for Foscar beyond the sky, and he shall have you forever to walk upon as a man walks upon the earth. I, Ennar, swear that Foscar shall be sent to the chiefs in the sky in all honor. And that you, dog-one, shall lie at his feet in that going!"

He did not touch Ross, but there was no doubt in Ross's mind that he meant every word he spoke.

===Chinese===
恩纳尔把负担交给其他人，从疲惫的马背上滑下来，低着头站立了很长时间。然后他直直地看着罗斯，穿过那片小小的空地，站到这个来自后来时代的人面前。那个在福斯卡手下作战时还带着的少年气已经荡然无存。他的眼睛冷酷无情，俯下身来说话，一字一句地缓慢吐出，好让罗斯听清那个誓言——那个可怕的誓言：

"林鼠，福斯卡要去他的焚尸火了。他要带走一个奴隶到天界侍奉他——一个听他号令奔跑、在他震怒时颤抖的奴隶。奴隶狗，你要为福斯卡在天界奔跑，他将永远踩着你，就像人踩在大地上一样。我，恩纳尔，发誓将以全部荣耀将福斯卡送到天界的首领们那里。而你，狗东西，将在那趟行程中躺在他的脚下！"

他没有碰罗斯，但罗斯心中毫不怀疑他说的每一个字都是认真的。

## XVII / XVII

===Original===
The preparations for Foscar's funeral went on through the night. A wooden structure, made up of tied fagots dragged in from the woodland, grew taller beyond the big tribal camp. The constant crooning wail of the women in the tents produced a minor murmur of sound, enough to drive a man to the edge of madness. Ross had been left under guard where he could watch it all, a refinement of torture which he would earlier have believed too subtle for Ennar. Though the older men carried minor commands among the horsemen, because Ennar was the closest of blood kin among the adult males, he was in charge of the coming ceremony.

===Chinese===
福斯卡（Foscar）的葬礼筹备彻夜进行。一个用从林地拖来的捆扎柴火搭成的木制结构，在部落大营外越堆越高。帐篷里女人们持续不断的低吟哀号，汇成一片低沉的嗡鸣，足以把人逼疯。罗斯被留在守卫的看管下，只能看着这一切——这是一种精心设计的折磨，他早先还以为恩纳尔（Ennar）不会有这么细腻的心思。虽然年长的骑手中有人掌握着次要的指挥权，但恩纳尔是成年男性中血缘最近的亲属，因此由他负责即将到来的仪式。

===Original===
The pick of the horse herd, a roan stallion, was brought in to be picketed near Ross as sacrifice number two, and two of the hounds were in turn leashed close by. Foscar, his best weapons to hand and a red cloak lapped about him, lay waiting on a bier. Nearby squatted the tribal wizard, shaking his thunder rattle and chanting in a voice which approached a shriek. This wild activity might have been a scene lifted directly from some tape stored at the project base. It was very difficult for Ross to remember that this was reality, that he was to be one of the main actors in the coming event, with no timely aid from Operation Retrograde to snatch him to safety.

===Chinese===
马群中的佼佼者，一匹杂色的公马，被牵来拴在罗斯附近，作为第二个祭品，两条猎犬也被依次拴在近旁。福斯卡躺在尸架上等待着，手边放着他最好的武器，身上裹着一条红色斗篷。部落的巫师蹲在近旁，摇着雷鸣筒，用近乎尖叫的声音吟唱着。这场狂野的活动，简直像是直接从项目基地存储的某盘记忆磁带中截取的场景。罗斯很难提醒自己这是现实，而他将是即将发生的事件中的主角之一，且不会有逆行行动（Operation Retrograde）的及时援手将他救往安全地带。

===Original===
Sometime during that nightmare he slept, his weariness of body overcoming him. He awoke, dazed, to find a hand clutching his mop of hair, pulling his head up.

"You sleep—you do not fear, Foscar's dog-one?"

Groggily Ross blinked up. Fear? Sure, he was afraid. Fear, he realized with a clear thrust of consciousness such as he had seldom experienced before, had always stalked beside him, slept in his bed. But he had never surrendered to it, and he would not now if he could help it.

===Chinese===
在这噩梦般的场景中，他不知何时睡着了，身体的疲惫压倒了他。他昏昏沉沉地醒来，发现一只手抓住了他那蓬乱的头发，把他的头拎了起来。

"你竟睡着了——你不害怕吗，福斯卡的狗奴？"

罗斯迷迷糊糊地眨了眨眼。害怕？当然，他害怕。恐惧，他带着一种前所未有的清晰意识领悟到，一直潜伏在他身边，与他同床共枕。但他从未向它屈服，只要还能抗争，现在也不会。

===Original===
"I do not fear!" He threw that creed into Ennar's face in one hot boast. He *would* not fear!

"We shall see if you speak so loudly when the fire bites you!" The other spat, yet in that oath there was a reluctant recognition of Ross's courage.

"When the fire bites…" That sang in Ross's head. There was something else—if he could only remember! Up to that moment he had kept a poor little shadow of hope. It is always impossible—he was conscious again with that strange clarity of mind—for a man to face his own death honestly. A man always continues to believe to the last moment of his life that something will intervene to save him.

===Chinese===
"我不害怕！"他将这信条热切地吹嘘到恩纳尔面前。他*不会*害怕！

"等火烧到你身上时，看你还能不能叫得这么大声！"对方啐了一口，但这咒骂中却带着一丝不情愿的承认，承认罗斯的勇气。

"火烧到身上……"这句话在他脑海中回响。还有别的什么——要是他能想起来就好了！直到那一刻，他心中还残留着一丝微弱渺茫的希望。一个人要诚实地面对自己的死亡总是不可能的——他再次带着那种奇异的清明意识到这一点。人总会一直相信，直到生命的最后一刻，总会有某种力量介入来拯救他。

===Original===
The men led the horse to the mound of fagots which was now crowned with Foscar's bier. The stallion went quietly, until a tall tribesman struck true with an ax, and the animal fell. The hounds were also killed and laid at their dead master's feet.

But Ross was not to fare so easily. The wizard danced around him, a hideous figure in a beast mask, a curled fringe of dried snakeskins swaying from his belt. Shaking his rattle, he squawked like an angry cat as they pulled Ross to the stacked wood.

===Chinese===
人们把马牵到那堆柴火前，柴堆顶端现在停放着福斯卡的尸架。公马安静地走着，直到一个高大的部落人精准地挥下一斧，马倒下了。猎犬也被杀死，放在它们死去的主人脚边。

但罗斯不会这么轻易死去。巫师围着他跳舞，戴着野兽面具，模样狰狞，腰带上挂着一串卷曲的干蛇皮。他摇着雷鸣筒，发出像愤怒的猫一样的尖叫，这时人们把罗斯拖到堆好的柴火前。

===Original===
Fire—there was something about fire—if he could only remember! Ross stumbled and nearly fell across one leg of the dead horse they were propping into place. Then he remembered that tongue of flame in the meadow grass which had burned the horse but not the rider. His hands and his head would have no protection, but the rest of his body was covered with the flame-resistant fabric of the alien suit. Could he do it? There was such a slight chance, and they were already pushing him onto that mound, his hands tied. Ennar stooped, and bound his ankles, securing him to the brush.

===Chinese===
火——关于火有什么东西——只要他能想起来！罗斯绊了一下，差点摔倒在那匹正被扶正的死马的一条腿上。接着他想起了草地上那道火焰，它烧死了马，却没有烧到骑手。他的手和头将得不到保护，但身体的其他部分被外星人（aliens）防火面料制成的服装覆盖着。他能做到吗？机会如此渺茫，而他们已经把他推上了柴堆，双手被绑着。恩纳尔弯下腰，绑住他的脚踝，将他固定在柴火上。

===Original===
So fastened, they left him. The tribe ringed around the pyre at a safe distance, Ennar and five other men approaching from different directions, torches aflame. Ross watched those blazing knots thrust into the brush and heard the crackle of the fire. His eyes, hard and measuring, studied the flash of flame from dried brush to seasoned wood.

===Chinese===
就这样被绑住后，他们丢下了他。部落的人在安全的距离外围成一圈，恩纳尔和另外五个人从不同方向走近，火把燃着。罗斯看着那些燃烧的火把被塞进柴火堆，听着火焰的噼啪声。他那双冷硬而审慎的眼睛，观察着火焰从干柴蔓延到干透木材的闪光。

===Original===
A tongue of yellow-red flame licked up at him. Ross hardly dared to breathe as it wreathed about his foot, his hide fetters smoldering. The insulation of the suit did not cut all the heat, but it allowed him to stay put for the few seconds he needed to make his escape spectacular.

===Chinese===
一道黄红色的火舌舔向他。火焰缠绕上他的脚，皮制的束缚物冒出青烟，罗斯几乎不敢呼吸。服装的隔热层并未隔绝所有热量，但足以让他坚持几秒钟——他需要这点时间让他的逃脱显得惊心动魄。

===Original===
The flame had eaten through his foot bonds, and yet the burning sensation on his feet and legs was no greater than it would have been from the direct rays of a bright summer sun. Ross moistened his lips with his tongue. The impact of heat on his hands and his face was different. He leaned down, held his wrists to the flame, taking in stoical silence the burns which freed him.

===Chinese===
火焰烧穿了他脚上的束缚，然而双脚和双腿上的灼痛感，并不比盛夏阳光直射带来的更强烈。罗斯用舌头润了润嘴唇。热量对手和脸的冲击则不同。他弯下腰，将手腕伸向火焰，以坚忍的沉默承受着那解脱了他的烧灼。

===Original===
Then, as the fire curled up so that he seemed to stand in a frame of writhing red banners, Ross leaped through that curtain, protecting his bowed head with his arms as best he could. But to the onlookers it seemed he passed unhurt through the heart of a roaring fire.

===Chinese===
接着，火焰盘旋而起，仿佛他正站在一面扭动的红色旗帜构成的画框中，罗斯纵身跃过那道火帘，尽可能用双臂护住低垂的头。但在旁观者看来，他仿佛毫发无伤地穿过了熊熊烈火的中心。

===Original===
He kept his footing and stood facing that part of the tribal ring directly before him. He heard a cry, perhaps of fear, and a blazing torch flew through the air and struck his hip. Although he felt the force of the blow, the burning bits of the head merely slid down his thigh and leg, leaving no mark on the smooth blue fabric.

===Chinese===
他稳住身形，面对着正前方那部分部落人群。他听到一声喊叫，或许是恐惧的喊叫，一支燃烧的火把飞过空中，击中了他的髋部。虽然他感受到了撞击的力量，但燃烧的碎片只是顺着他的大腿和小腿滑落，在那光滑的蓝色布料上没留下任何痕迹。

===Original===
"Ahhhhhhh!"

Now the wizard capered before him, shaking his rattle to make a deafening din. Ross struck out, slapping the sorcerer out of his path, and stooped to pick up the smoldering brand which had been thrown at him. Whirling it about his head, though every movement was torture to his scorched hands, he set it flaming once more. Holding it in front of him as a weapon, he stalked directly at the men and women before him.

===Chinese===
"啊——！"

现在，巫师在他面前蹦跳着，摇着雷鸣筒发出震耳欲聋的噪音。罗斯出手了，一巴掌将巫师扇到一边，然后弯腰捡起那支扔向他的、还在闷烧的火把。他将火把在头顶挥舞，尽管每一个动作都灼痛着他那被烧伤的双手，他让它再次熊熊燃烧起来。他将火把当作武器举在身前，径直走向面前的男人和女人们。

===Original===
The torch was a poor enough defense against spears and axes, but Ross did not care—he put into this last gamble all the determination he could summon. Nor did he realize what a figure he presented to the tribesmen. A man who had crossed a curtain of fire without apparent hurt, who appeared to wash in tongues of flame without harm, and who now called upon fire in turn as a weapon, was no man but a demon!

===Chinese===
这根火把作为抵御矛和斧的防御简直不堪一击，但罗斯不在乎——他将能召唤出的所有决心都倾注在这最后的赌注中。他也没有意识到自己在部落人眼中呈现的是怎样的形象。一个毫发无伤地穿过火帘、仿佛沐浴在火舌中而无损、现在又反过来以火为武器的人，绝非凡人，而是恶魔！

===Original===
The wall of people wavered and broke. Women screamed and ran; men shouted. But no one threw a spear or struck with an ax. Ross walked on, a man possessed, looking neither to the right or left. He was in the camp now, stalking toward the fire burning before Foscar's tent. He did not turn aside for that either, but holding the torch high, strode through the heart of the flames, risking further burns for the sake of insuring his ultimate safety.

===Chinese===
人墙动摇并溃散了。女人们尖叫着跑开；男人们呼喊着。但没有人投掷长矛或挥斧劈砍。罗斯继续前行，如同着了魔一般，既不向左也不向右看。他现在走进了营地，径直走向福斯卡帐篷前燃烧的篝火。他也没有避开那堆火，而是高举着火把，大步穿过火焰中心，为了确保最终的安全，甘愿冒着再次被烧伤的风险。

===Original===
The tribesmen melted away as he approached the last line of tents, with the open land beyond. The horses of the herd, which had been driven to this side to avoid the funeral pyre, were shifting nervously, the scent of burning making them uneasy.

===Chinese===
当他靠近最后一排帐篷，外边就是开阔地时，部落的人纷纷退避。马群为了避开火葬堆被赶到了这一边，此刻正不安地躁动着，燃烧的气味让它们焦躁不安。

===Original===
Once more Ross whirled the dying torch about his head. Recalling how the aliens had sent his horse mad, he tossed it behind him into the grass between the tents and the herd. The tinder-dry stuff caught immediately. Now if the men tried to ride after him, they would have trouble.

===Chinese===
罗斯再次将那支快要熄灭的火把在头顶挥舞。他想起外星人（aliens）曾如何让他的马发狂，便将火把向后抛进了帐篷和马群之间的草丛。极其干燥的草立刻燃烧起来。现在，如果那些人想骑马追他，就会有麻烦了。

===Original===
Without hindrance he walked across the meadow at the same even pace, never turning to look behind. His hands were two separate worlds of smarting pain; his hair and eyebrows were singed, and a finger of burn ran along the angle of his jaw. But he was free, and he did not believe that Foscar's men would be in any haste to pursue him. Somewhere before him lay the river, the river which ran to the sea. Ross walked on in the sunny morning while behind him black smoke raised a dark beacon to the sky.

===Chinese===
他不受阻碍地以同样平稳的步伐穿过草地，一次也没有回头看。他的双手是两个独立的、刺痛的世界；头发和眉毛都烧焦了，一道灼伤的痕迹沿着下颌线蔓延。但他自由了，而且他不认为福斯卡的人会急于追赶他。在他前方某处，流淌着那条通往大海的河流。罗斯在阳光明媚的早晨继续前行，身后黑色的浓烟如一座黑暗的信标升向天空。

===Original===
Afterward he guessed that he must have been lightheaded for several days, remembering little save the pain in his hands and the fact that it was necessary to keep moving. Once he fell to his knees and buried both hands in the cool, moist earth where a thread of stream trickled from a pool. The muck seemed to draw out a little of the agony while he drank with a fever thirst.

===Chinese===
后来他猜想，自己一定是头晕了好几天，除了双手的疼痛和必须不断前进这一事实外，几乎什么都不记得了。有一次他跪倒在地，将双手埋进清凉湿润的泥土中，那里有一道细细的溪流从水池中淌出。当他带着发烧般的干渴饮水时，那泥浆似乎减轻了一点痛苦。

===Original===
Ross seemed to move through a haze which lifted at intervals during which he noted his surroundings, was able to recall a little of what lay behind him, and to keep to the correct route. However, the gaps of time in between were forever lost to him. He stumbled along the banks of a river and fronted a bear fishing. The massive beast rose on its hind legs, growled, and Ross walked by it uncaring, unmenaced by the puzzled animal.

===Chinese===
罗斯似乎穿行在一片薄雾中，雾气不时散去，在那些间歇里，他能注意到周围的环境，能回想起一点身后的事，并保持正确的路线。然而，其间那些时间的间隙，对他而言永远地失落了。他沿着河岸蹒跚而行，迎面遇上一头正在捕鱼的熊。这头巨兽用后腿站起来，咆哮着，罗斯毫不在意地走了过去，那困惑的动物并未威胁到他。

===Original===
Sometimes he slept through the dark periods which marked the nights, or he stumbled along under the moon, nursing his hands against his breast, whimpering a little when his foot slipped and the jar of that mishap ran through his body. Once he heard singing, only to realize that it was himself who sang hoarsely a melody which would be popular thousands of years later in the world through which he wavered. But always Ross knew that he must go on, using that thick stream of running water as a guide to his final goal, the sea.

===Chinese===
有时他在标志着夜晚的黑暗时段里沉睡，有时他在月光下蹒跚前行，将双手抱在胸前护着，当脚滑了一下，那一跤的震动传遍全身时，他会低声呜咽。有一次他听到歌声，才意识到那是他自己在沙哑地唱着一首旋律，这首歌几千年后将在他正踉跄穿行的这个世界里流行。但罗斯始终知道，他必须继续前进，以那条湍急的河流为指引，走向他最终的目标——大海。

===Original===
After a long while those spaces of mental clarity grew longer, appearing closer together. He dug small shelled things from under stones along the river and ate them avidly. Once he clubbed a rabbit and feasted. He sucked birds' eggs from a nest hidden among some reeds—just enough to keep his gaunt body going, though his gray eyes were now set in what was almost a death's-head.

===Chinese===
过了很长一段时间，那些神志清醒的间隙变得更长，出现得也更频繁了。他从河边石头下挖出带壳的小东西，贪婪地吃掉。有一次他用棍子打死了一只兔子，饱餐了一顿。他从芦苇丛中隐藏的鸟巢里吸食鸟蛋——仅仅勉强维持着他那瘦骨嶙峋的身体，尽管他那双灰色的眼睛如今深陷在几乎像骷髅一般的脸上。

===Original===
Ross did not know just when he realized that he was again being hunted. It started with an uneasiness which differed from his previous fever-bred hallucinations. This was an inner pulling, a growing compulsion to turn and retrace his way back toward the mountains to meet something, or someone, waiting for him on the backward path.

===Chinese===
罗斯不知道自己是从何时起意识到他又被追捕了。这种感觉始于一种不同于他先前发烧引起的幻觉的不安。这是一种内在的拉力，一种越来越强烈的冲动，迫使他转身，沿着来路返回山中，去会合那个在回头路上等待着他的某物，或某人。

===Original===
But Ross kept on, fearing sleep now and fighting it. For once he had lain down to rest and had wakened on his feet, heading back as if that compulsion had the power to take over his body when his waking will was off guard.

===Chinese===
但罗斯继续前行，现在他害怕睡眠，并与之抗争。因为有一次他躺下休息，醒来时却发现自己站着，正往回走，仿佛那股冲动拥有在他清醒意志放松警惕时接管他身体的力量。

===Original===
So he rested, but he dared not sleep, the desire constantly tearing at his will, striving to take over his weakened body and draw it back. Perhaps against all reason he believed that it was the aliens who were trying to control him. Ross did not even venture to guess why they were so determined to get him. If there were tribesmen on his trail as well, he did not know, but he was sure that this was now purely a war of wills.

===Chinese===
所以他休息，但不敢入睡，那欲望不断撕扯着他的意志，试图接管他虚弱的身体并将他拖回去。或许完全违背理性地，他相信是外星人（aliens）在试图控制他。罗斯甚至不敢猜测他们为何如此执意要抓住他。是否有部落人也在追踪他，他不得而知，但他确信这现在纯粹是一场意志的较量。

===Original===
As the banks of the river were giving way to marshes, he had to wade through mud and water, detouring the boggy sections. Great clouds of birds whirled and shrieked their protests at his coming, and sleek water animals paddled and poked curious heads out of the water as this two-legged thing walked mechanically through their green land. Always that pull was with him, until Ross was more aware of fighting it than of traveling.

===Chinese===
随着河岸逐渐变成沼泽，他不得不跋涉于泥水之中，绕过那些泥泞的区域。成群的鸟儿盘旋着，尖声抗议着他的到来，光滑的水生动物划着水，将好奇的脑袋探出水面，看着这个两足生物机械地穿过它们的绿色家园。那股拉力始终伴随着他，以至于罗斯对与之搏斗的意识，超过了对行走的感知。

===Original===
Why did they want him to return? Why did they not follow him? Or were they afraid to venture too far from where they had come through the transfer? Yet the unseen rope which was tugging at him did not grow less tenuous as he put more distance between himself and the mountain valley. Ross could understand neither their motives nor their methods, but he could continue to fight.

===Chinese===
他们为什么想让他回去？他们为什么不跟着他？或者他们害怕离他们穿越传送门的地方太远？然而，当他与那条山谷的距离越来越远时，那条拉扯着他的无形绳索并未变得微弱。罗斯既无法理解他们的动机，也无法理解他们的方法，但他可以继续抗争。

===Original===
The bog was endless. He found an island and lashed himself with his suit belt to the single willow that grew there, knowing that he must have sleep, or he could not hope to last through the next day. Then he slept, only to waken cold, shaking, and afraid. Shoulder deep in a pool, he was aware that in his sleep he must have opened the belt buckle and freed himself, and only the mishap of falling into the water had brought him around to sanity.

===Chinese===
沼泽无边无际。他找到一个小岛，用腰带将自己绑在岛上唯一生长的柳树上，心知必须睡觉，否则撑不过第二天。然后他睡着了，醒来时却浑身冰冷、颤抖、恐惧。他肩部以下浸在一个水塘里，意识到自己在睡梦中一定解开了腰带扣，挣脱了束缚，只有掉进水里的意外才让他恢复了神智。

===Original===
Somehow he got back to the tree, rehooked the buckle and twisted the belt around the branches so that he was sure he could not work it free until daybreak. He lapsed into a deepening doze, and awoke, still safely anchored, with the morning cries of the birds. Ross considered the suit as he untangled the belt. Could the strange clothing be the tie by which the aliens held to him? If he were to strip, leaving the garment behind, would he be safe?

===Chinese===
他设法爬回树边，重新扣好腰带扣，并将腰带在树枝上缠绕了几圈，确保天亮前自己无法挣脱。他陷入越来越深的瞌睡，醒来时，发现自己仍被牢牢固定着，伴随着清晨的鸟鸣。罗斯一边解开缠绕的腰带，一边审视着那套服装。这件奇怪的衣服会是外星人（aliens）维系对他的控制的纽带吗？如果他脱掉衣服，把它留下，他会安全吗？

===Original===
He tried to force open the studs across his chest, but they would not yield to the slight pressure which was all his seared fingers could exert, and when he pulled at the fabric, he was unable to tear it. So, still wearing the livery of the off-world men, Ross continued on his way, hardly caring where he went or how. The mud plastered on him by his frequent falls was some protection against the swarm of insect life his passing stirred into attack. However, he was able to endure a swollen face and slitted eyes, being far more conscious of the wrenching feeling within him than the misery of his body.

===Chinese===
他试图用力掰开胸前的纽扣，但它们对他那灼伤的手指所能施加的微弱压力无动于衷，当他拉扯布料时，也无法将其撕破。于是，罗斯依然穿着这身异世界（off-world）的衣服继续赶路，几乎不在乎要去哪里或如何去。他频繁摔倒时糊在身上的泥巴，对他经过时激起的成群昆虫的攻击多少有些防护作用。然而，他能忍受肿胀的脸和眯缝的眼睛，比起身体的痛苦，他内心那股撕裂般的感觉要强烈得多。

===Original===
The character of the marsh began to change once more. The river was splitting into a dozen smaller streams, shaping out fanlike. Looking down at this from one of the marsh hillocks, Ross knew a faint surge of relief. Such a place had been on the map Ashe had made them memorize. He was close to the sea at last, and for the moment that was enough.

===Chinese===
沼泽的性质再次开始改变。河流分成了十几条更小的溪流，呈扇形散开。从一个沼泽小丘上俯瞰此景，罗斯感到一丝微弱的宽慰。这样的地方在阿什（Ashe）让他们背下的地图上出现过。他终于靠近大海了，此刻，这就足够了。

===Original===
A salt-sharpened wind cut at him with the force of a fist in the face. In the absence of sunlight the leaden clouds overhead set a winterlike gloom across the countryside. To the constant sound of birdcalls Ross tramped heavily through small pools, beating a path through tangles of marsh grass. He stole eggs from nests, sucking his nourishment eagerly with no dislike for the fishy flavor, and drinking from stagnant, brackish ponds.

===Chinese===
一股带着咸味的刺骨寒风，像拳头一样猛击着他的脸。由于没有阳光，头顶铅灰色的云层给乡村笼罩上一层冬日般的阴沉。伴随着持续不断的鸟鸣声，罗斯沉重地跋涉过一个个小水潭，在纠缠的沼泽草丛中踏出一条路。他从鸟巢里偷蛋，急切地吸食着营养，对那腥味并不反感，还从死水、咸涩的池塘里饮水。

===Original===
Suddenly Ross halted, at first thinking that the continuous roll of sound he heard was thunder. Yet the clouds overhead were massed no more than before and there was no sign of lightning. Continuing on, he realized that the mysterious sound was the pounding of surf—he was near the sea!

===Chinese===
突然罗斯停了下来，起初以为听到的那持续不断的轰隆声是雷声。然而头顶的云层并未比之前更密集，也没有闪电的迹象。继续前行后，他意识到那神秘的声音是海浪的拍击声——他靠近大海了！

===Original===
Willing his body to run, he weaved forward at a reeling trot, pitting all his energy against the incessant pull from behind. His feet skidded out of marsh mud into sand. Ahead of him were dark rocks surrounded by the white lace of spray.

===Chinese===
他强令自己的身体奔跑起来，以一种摇晃的小跑向前穿行，将所有精力都用来对抗身后那不断拉扯的力量。他的双脚从沼泽的泥泞滑到了沙地上。前方是黑色的岩石，周围环绕着蕾丝般白色的浪花。

===Original===
Ross headed straight toward that spray until he stood knee-deep in the curling, foam-edged water and felt its tug on his body almost as strong as that other tug upon his mind. He knelt, letting the salt water sting to life every cut, every burn, sputtering as it filled his mouth and nostrils, washing from him the slime of the bog lands. It was cold and bitter, but it was the sea! He had made it!

===Chinese===
罗斯径直走向那片浪花，直到他站在没膝的、卷曲着白色泡沫边缘的海水中，感觉到海水对身体的拉扯，几乎与那另一种拉扯他心智的力量一样强大。他跪了下来，让咸涩的海水刺痛每一道伤口，每一块烧伤，当海水灌满他的口鼻时，他呛咳着，洗去了他身上沼泽地的污泥。海水冰冷苦涩，但这就是大海！他成功了！

===Original===
Ross Murdock staggered back and sat down suddenly in the sand. Glancing about, he saw that his refuge was a rough triangle between two of the small river arms, littered with the debris of the spring floods which had grounded here after rejection by the sea. Although there was plenty of material for a fire, he had no means of kindling a flame, having lost the flint all Beaker traders carried for such a purpose.

===Chinese===
罗斯·默多克踉跄着退后，突然一屁股坐在沙地上。环顾四周，他发现自己的避难所是两条小河岔之间的一个粗略三角形地带，散落着春季洪水留下的残骸，这些洪水被大海拒绝后在此搁浅。虽然有足够的生火材料，但他没有办法点燃火焰，因为所有比克尔（Beaker）商人都随身携带的那种用于此目的的燧石，他早已丢失。

===Original===
This was the sea, and against all odds he had reached it. He lay back, his self-confidence restored to the point where he dared once more to consider the future. He watched the swooping flight of gulls drawing patterns under the clouds above. For the moment he wanted nothing more than to lie here and rest.

===Chinese===
这就是大海，尽管困难重重，他还是到达了。他向后躺下，自信心恢复到了足以让他再次考虑未来的程度。他看着海鸥在云层下俯冲飞翔，划出种种图案。此刻他只想躺在这里休息。

===Original===
But he did not surrender to this first demand of his over-driven body for long. Hungry and cold, sure that a storm was coming, he knew he had to build a fire—a fire on shore could provide him with the means of signaling the sub. Hardly knowing why—because one part of the coastline was as good as another—Ross began to walk again, threading a path in and out among the rocky outcrops.

===Chinese===
但他并未向这过度透支的身体提出的第一个要求屈服太久。饥寒交迫，确信暴风雨即将来临，他知道必须生一堆火——岸上的火堆可以为他提供向潜艇（sub）发出信号的手段。几乎不知道为什么——因为海岸线的这一段和另一段没什么区别——罗斯又开始行走，在岩石露头之间穿行。

===Original===
So he found it, a hollow between two such windbreaks within which was a blackened circle of small stones holding charred wood, with some empty shells piled nearby. Here was unmistakable evidence of a camp! Ross plunged forward, thrusting a hand impetuously into the black mass of the dead fire. To his astonishment, he touched warmth!

===Chinese===
就这样，他找到了它——两处这样的防风林之间的一个凹地，里面有一个被熏黑的、由小石头围成的圆圈，里面是烧焦的木头，旁边堆着一些空贝壳。这里有不容错辨的露营痕迹！罗斯冲上前去，鲁莽地将一只手插进那堆死火的黑色残骸中。令他惊讶的是，他触到了温暖！

===Original===
Hardly daring to disturb those precious bits of charcoal, he dug around them, then carefully blew into what appeared to be dead ashes. There was an answering glow! He could not have just imagined it.

===Chinese===
他几乎不敢惊扰那些宝贵的木炭碎片，在它们周围挖掘，然后小心地吹向那些看似死灰的灰烬。一丝回应的红光出现了！他不可能只是在想象。

===Original===
From a pile of wood that had been left behind, Ross snatched a small twig, poking it at the coal after he had rubbed it into a brush on the rough rock. He watched, all one ache of hope. The twig caught!

===Chinese===
罗斯从一堆留下的木柴中抓起一根小树枝，在粗糙的岩石上将其一端摩擦成刷状后，把它戳向炭火。他注视着，满心都是痛苦的期望。树枝点燃了！

===Original===
With his stiff fingers so clumsy, he had to be very careful, but Ross had learned patience in a hard school. Bit by bit he fed that tiny blaze until he had a real fire. Then, leaning back against the rock, he watched it.

===Chinese===
手指僵硬而笨拙，他不得不非常小心，但罗斯在艰苦的环境中学会了耐心。他一点一点地喂养那微弱的火苗，直到生起一堆真正的火。然后，他靠在岩石上，注视着火焰。

===Original===
It was now obvious that the placement of the original fire had been chosen with care, for the outcrops gave it wind shelter. They also provided a dark backdrop, partially hiding the flames on the landward side but undoubtedly making them more visible from the sea. The site seemed just right for a signal fire—but to what?

===Chinese===
现在很明显，最初那堆火的选址是经过精心考虑的，因为那些岩石露头为它提供了挡风的庇护。它们也提供了深色的背景，在陆地一侧部分隐藏了火焰，但从海上看无疑让火焰更加显眼。这个地点似乎正适合作为信号火堆——但那是向谁发信号呢？

===Original===
Ross's hands shook slightly as he fed the blaze. It was only too clear why anyone would make a signal on this shore. McNeil—or perhaps both he and Ashe—had survived the breakup of the raft, after all. They had reached this point—abandoned no earlier than this morning, judging by the life remaining in the coals—and put up the signal. Then, just as arranged, they had been collected by the sub, by now on its way back to the hidden North American post. There was no hope of any pickup for him now. Just as he had believed them dead after he had found that rag on the sapling, so they must have thought him finished after his fall in the river. He was just a few hours too late!

===Chinese===
罗斯往火里添柴时，双手微微颤抖。再清楚不过了，为什么有人会在这个海岸上点火。麦克尼尔（McNeil）——或者他和阿什（Ashe）都活着——终究是在木筏解体后幸存了下来。他们到达了这个地点——根据余烬中残存的生命力判断，他们最早也是今天早上才离开的——并点燃了信号火堆。然后，按照约定，他们被潜艇（sub）接走了，此刻潜艇大概正在返回那个隐蔽的北美哨所（post）的途中。现在他已没有任何被接走的希望了。就像他在小树苗上发现那块破布后曾相信他们死了一样，他们在河里目睹他落水后，想必也认定他完了。他仅仅迟到了几个小时！

===Original===
Ross folded his arms across his hunched knees and rested his head on them. There was no possible way he could ever reach the post or his own kind—ever again. Thousands of miles lay between him and the temporary installation in this time.

===Chinese===
罗斯双臂交叉放在弓起的膝盖上，头枕在上面。他再也没有可能到达那个哨所，或者找到自己的同类了——永远不可能了。数千英里的距离横亘在他与这个时代的临时驻地之间。

===Original===
He was so sunk in his own complete despair that he was long unaware of finally being free of the pressure to turn back which had so long haunted him. But as he roused to feed the fire he got to wondering. Had those who hunted him given up the chase? Since he had lost his own race with time, he did not really care. What did it matter?

===Chinese===
他沉浸在彻底的绝望中，以至于很久之后才意识到，那股长久以来困扰着他的、迫使他回头的压力终于消失了。但当他起身添火时，他开始思索。那些追捕他的人放弃追踪了吗？既然他已经输掉了与时间的赛跑，他其实并不在乎了。这又有什么关系呢？

===Original===
The pile of wood was getting low, but he decided that did not matter either. Even so, Ross got to his feet, moving over to the drifts of storm wrack to gather more. Why should he stay here by a useless beacon? But somehow he could not force himself to move on, as futile as his vigil seemed.

===Chinese===
那堆木柴快烧完了，但他决定那也无所谓。即便如此，罗斯还是站起身，走向那些被风暴冲刷上岸的杂物堆，去收集更多的柴火。他为什么要留在这个无用的信号火堆旁？但不知为何，他无法强迫自己离开，尽管他的守望显得如此徒劳。

===Original===
Dragging the sun-dried, bleached limbs of long-dead trees to his half shelter, he piled them up, working until he laughed at the barricade he had built. "A siege!" For the first time in days he spoke aloud. "I might be ready for a siege…" He pulled over another branch, added it to his pile, and kneeled down once more by the flames.

===Chinese===
他将那些早已死去、被阳光晒干漂白的树枝拖到他那半截的避难所旁，堆了起来，一直干到对着自己建起的路障笑出声来。"围攻！"几天来他第一次大声说话。"也许我已经准备好打一场围攻战了……"他又拽过来一根树枝，添进柴堆，然后再次跪在火焰旁。

===Original===
There were fisherfolk to be found along this coast, and tomorrow when he was rested he would strike south and try to find one of their primitive villages. Traders would be coming into this territory now that the Red-inspired raiders were gone. If he could contact them…

===Chinese===
沿着这片海岸能找到渔民，明天休息好后，他会向南走，试着找到他们原始村落中的一个。既然那些由赤色分子（the Reds）煽动的袭击者已经离开，商人们会进入这片区域。如果他能联系上他们……

===Original===
But that spark of interest in the future died almost as soon as it was born. To be a Beaker trader as an agent for the project was one thing, to live the role for the rest of his life was something else.

===Chinese===
但那对未来的一点兴趣，几乎刚一萌生就熄灭了。作为项目（the project）的代理人做一名比克尔（Beaker）商人是一回事，余生都要扮演这个角色则是另一回事。

===Original===
Ross stood by his fire, staring out to sea for a sign he knew he would never see again as long as he lived. Then, as if a spear had struck between his shoulder blades, he was attacked.

===Chinese===
罗斯站在火堆旁，凝视着大海，寻找一个他知道此生再也看不到的信号。接着，仿佛一柄长矛刺入了他的肩胛骨之间，他遭到了攻击。

===Original===
The blow was not physical, but came instead as a tearing, red pain in his head, a pressure so terrible he could not move. He knew instantly that behind him now lurked the ultimate danger.

===Chinese===
这一击并非来自肉体，而是一阵撕裂般的、猩红的剧痛在他脑中炸开，压力如此可怕，他无法动弹。他立刻知道，那终极的危险此刻正潜伏在他身后。

## XVIII

===Original===
Ross fought to break that hold, to turn his head, to face the peril which crept upon him now. Unlike anything he had ever met before in his short lifetime, it could only have come from some alien source. This strange encounter was a battle of will against will! The same rebellion against authority which had ruled his boyhood, which had pushed him into the orbit of the project, stiffened him to meet this attack.

He was going to turn his head; he was going to see who stood there. He *was*! Inch by inch, Ross's head came around, though sweat stung his seared and bitten flesh, and every breath was an effort. He caught a half glimpse of the beach behind the rocks, and the stretch of sand was empty. Overhead the birds were gone—as if they had never existed. Or, as if they had been swept away by some impatient fighter, who wanted no distractions from the purpose at hand.

Having successfully turned his head, Ross decided to turn his body. His left hand went out, slowly, as if it moved some great weight. His palm gritted painfully on the rock and he savored that pain, for it pierced through the dead blanket of compulsion that was being used against him. Deliberately he ground his blistered skin against the stone, concentrating on the sharp torment in his hand as the agony shot up his arm. While he focused his attention on the physical pain, he could feel the pressure against him weaken. Summoning all his strength, Ross swung around in a movement which was only a shadow of his former feline grace.

===Chinese===
罗斯（Ross）奋力要挣脱那股钳制，要转动头颅，要直面正悄然逼近的危险。这威胁不同于他短暂一生中曾遭遇过的任何东西，它只可能来自某个异星源头。这场奇异的遭遇是一场意志对意志的较量！统治他少年时代的那种反叛精神，那种将他推入项目（the project）轨道的叛逆，此刻支撑着他抵御这次攻击。

他要转过头去；他要看看是谁站在那里。他*一定*能做到！罗斯的头一寸一寸地转过去，汗水刺痛了他被灼烧和咬噬过的皮肉，每吸一口气都是煎熬。他隐约瞥见岩石后面的海滩，沙地上空无一人。头顶的鸟儿不见了——仿佛从未存在过。又或者，它们被某个不耐烦的战士扫荡干净了，因为那人不想在眼前的目的上有任何干扰。

成功转过头后，罗斯决定转动身体。他的左手缓缓伸出，仿佛在推动什么沉重的东西。手掌在岩石上磨得生疼，他却品味着这份疼痛——因为痛觉刺穿了施加于他身上的那层死寂的强制力。他刻意让磨破的皮肤碾过石头，把注意力集中在手上剧烈的痛楚上，感受疼痛沿着手臂上涌。当他全神贯注于肉体的痛苦时，他感到施加于自己的压力在减弱。罗斯调动全身力量，猛然转身——那动作不过是他昔日猫一般优雅身姿的一个残影。

===Original===
The beach was still empty, except for the piles of driftwood, the rocks, and the other things he had originally found there. Yet he knew that something was waiting to pounce. Having discovered that for him pain was a defense weapon, he had that one resource. If they took him, it would be after besting him in a fight.

Even as he made this decision, Ross was conscious of a curious weakening of the force bent upon him. It was as if his opponents had been surprised, either at his simple actions of the past few seconds or at his determination. Ross leaped upon that surprise, adding it to his stock of unseen weapons.

He leaned forward, still grinding his torn hand against the rock as a steadying influence, took up a length of dried wood, and thrust its end into the fire. Having once used fire to save himself, he was ready and willing to do it again, although at the same time, another part of him shrank from what he intended.

Holding his improvised torch breast-high, Ross stared across it, searching the land for the faintest sign of his enemies. In spite of the fire and the light he held before him, the dusk prevented him from seeing too far. Behind him the crash of the surf could have covered the noise of a marching army.

===Chinese===
海滩依然空无一人，只有成堆的浮木、岩石以及他最初发现的那些东西。但他知道有什么东西正伺机扑来。既然发现了疼痛对他而言是一种防御武器，他至少有这一手。如果他们要抓他，必须先在战斗中击败他。

就在他做出这个决定的同时，罗斯察觉到施加于他的那股力量出现了奇怪的减弱。仿佛他的对手被惊到了——要么是被他过去几秒钟的简单举动，要么是被他的决心。罗斯抓住了这个惊讶，将其纳入自己无形武器库中。

他俯身向前，仍让受伤的手碾在岩石上以维持清醒，拾起一根干木头，将一端捅进火里。他曾经用火救过自己一命，他随时准备再做一次——尽管与此同时，他内心的一部分在退缩，因为他即将做的事情。

罗斯把自制的火把举到胸前，隔着火焰搜寻地面上敌人最微弱的迹象。尽管有火光在手，暮色还是阻碍了他的视线。身后海浪的轰鸣足以掩盖一支行军部队的声响。

===Original===
"Come and get me!"

He whirled his brand into bursting life and then hurled it straight into the drift among the dunes. He was grabbing for a second brand almost before the blazing head of the first had fallen into the twisted, bleached roots of a dead tree.

He stood tense, a second torch now kindled in his hand. The sharp vise of another's will which had nipped him so tightly a moment ago was easing, slowly disappearing as water might trickle away. Yet he could not believe that this small act of defiance had so daunted his unseen opponent as to make him give up the struggle this easily. It was more likely the pause of a wrestler seeking for a deadlier grip.

The brand in his hand—Ross's second line of defense—was a weapon he was loath to use, but would use if he were forced to it. He kept his hand mercilessly flat against the rock as a reminder and a spur.

===Chinese===
"来抓我啊！"

他猛力一挥，火把腾地燃起，然后径直扔向沙丘间的浮木堆。第一支火把的燃烧端还没落入一棵枯树纠结泛白的根部，他就已经在抓第二根木棒了。

他紧张地站着，手中已经点燃了第二支火把。片刻之前还死死夹住他的那股钢铁般强硬的意志之钳，正在松动，像水一样缓缓流散。但他不敢相信自己这个小小的反抗举动已经令看不见的对手如此灰心，以至于轻易放弃了这场角力。更可能的解释是，摔跤手在寻找一个更致命的把位之前暂时停手。

他手中的木棒——罗斯的第二道防线——是一件他不愿使用、但被逼无奈时会使用的武器。他毫不留情地把手掌按在岩石上，作为提醒和鞭策。

===Original===
Fire twisted and crackled among the driftwood where the first torch had lodged, providing a flickering light yards from where he stood. He was grateful for it in the gloom of the gathering storm. If they would only come to open war before the rain struck…

Ross sheltered his torch with his body as spray, driven inward from the sea, spattered his shoulders and his back. If it rained, he would lose what small advantage the fire gave him, but then he would find some other way to meet them. They would neither break him nor take him, even if he had to wade into the sea and swim out into the lash of the cold northern waves until he could not move his tired limbs any longer.

Once again that steel-edge will struck at Ross, probing his stubbornness, assaulting his mind. He whirled the torch, brought the scorching breath of the flame across the hand resting on the rock. Unable to control his own cry of protest, he was not sure he had the fortitude to repeat such an act.

He had won again! The pressure had fallen away in a flick, almost as if some current had been snapped off. Through the red curtain of his torment Ross sensed a surprise and disbelief. He was unaware that in this queer duel he was using both a power of will and a depth of perception he had never known he possessed. Because of his daring, he had shaken his opponents as no physical attack could have affected them.

===Chinese===
火焰在第一支火把落定的浮木堆中蜿蜒噼啪作响，在他站立之处数码开外提供了一团摇曳的光。在渐浓的暮色和即将来临的暴风雨中，他感激这团火光。要是他们能在暴雨降临之前发动正面攻击就好了……

罗斯用身体护住火把，海浪激起的水沫从海上打来，溅上他的肩膀和后背。如果下雨，火给他的那点微弱优势就会丧失，但那时他会另想办法对付他们。他们既不能打垮他，也不能抓走他——哪怕他不得不涉入大海，在冰冷北方波涛的抽打中游出去，直到疲惫的四肢再也动弹不得。

那钢铁般锋利的意志再次向罗斯袭来，试探他的倔强，侵袭他的头脑。他挥动火把，让灼热的火焰扫过按在岩石上的那只手。他无法控制自己抗议的叫喊，也不确定自己是否有勇气再做一次这样的事。

他又赢了！压力在瞬间崩散，仿佛某根线路被猛然切断。透过痛苦的红色帷幕，罗斯感知到对方的惊讶和难以置信。他不知道在这场奇异的决斗中，他动用了一种前所未有的意志力量和感知深度。正因他的大胆，他动摇了对手——那是任何物理攻击都无法做到的。

===Original===
"Come and get me!" He shouted again at the barren shoreline where the fire ate at the drift and nothing stirred, yet something very much alive and conscious lay hidden. This time there was more than simple challenge in Ross's demand—there was a note of triumph.

The spray whipped by him, striking at his fire, at the brand he held. Let the sea water put both out! He would find another way of fighting. He was certain of that, and he sensed that those out there knew it too and were troubled.

The fire was being driven by the wind along the crisscross lines of bone-white wood left high on the beach, forming a wall of flame between him and the interior, not, however, an insurmountable barrier to whatever lurked there.

Again Ross leaned against the rock, studying the length of beach. Had he been wrong in thinking that they were within the range of his voice? The power they had used might carry over a greater distance.

===Chinese===
"来抓我啊！"他再次朝荒凉的海岸线呐喊，那里火焰吞噬着浮木，一切静止不动，但某种极其鲜活且有意识的东西隐藏其中。这一次，罗斯的叫喊中不只有单纯的挑衅——还多了一丝胜利的意味。

水沫抽打着他，扑向他的火、他手中的木棒。让海水把两者都浇灭好了！他会找到另一种战斗方式。他对此确信不疑，而且他感觉到那些隐藏者也知道这一点，正因此而困扰。

风将火焰沿着海滩高处纵横交错的白骨色木头蔓延开来，在他与内陆之间形成一道火墙——尽管对潜伏在那里的东西来说，这并非不可逾越的屏障。

罗斯再次靠在岩石上，审视着整片海滩。他们是否在他声音所及的范围之外？他们所用的力量也许能传播更远的距离。

===Original===
"Yahhhh—" Instead of a demand, he now voiced a taunting cry, screaming his defiance. Some wild madness had been transmitted to him by the winds, the roaring sea, his own pain. Ready to face the worst they could send against him, he tried to hurl that thought back at them as they had struck with their united will at him. No answer came to his challenge, no rise to counterattack.

Moving away from the rock, Ross began to walk forward toward the burning drift, his torch ready in his hand. "I am here!" he shouted into the wind. "Come out—face me!"

It was then that he saw those who had tracked him. Two tall thin figures, wearing dark clothes, were standing quietly watching him, their eyes dark holes in the white ovals of their faces.

Ross halted. Though they were separated by yards of sand and rock and a burning barrier, he could feel the force they wielded. The nature of that force had changed, however. Once it had struck with a vigorous spear point; now it formed a shield of protection. Ross could not break through that shield, and they dared not drop it. A stalemate existed between them in this strange battle, the like of which Ross's world had not known before.

===Chinese===
"呀啊——"他不再发出质问，而是发出一声嘲弄的呐喊，尖叫着表达自己的反叛。某种狂野的疯狂已通过风、咆哮的海洋、他自身的痛楚传递给了他。准备好面对他们能派出的最恶劣的攻击，他试图将这个念头像他们曾用联合意志打击他那样反弹回去。没有回应他的挑衅，没有人起身反击。

罗斯离开岩石，开始朝燃烧的浮木走去，火把握在手中。"我在这里！"他朝风中喊道。"出来——面对我！"

就在那时，他看到了追踪他的人。两个高瘦的身影，穿着深色衣服，正安静地站着注视他，他们白色面庞上的眼睛是两个黑洞。

罗斯停了下来。尽管隔着数码的沙地、岩石和一道燃烧的屏障，他仍能感受到他们施展的力量。然而那力量的性质已经变了。此前它如锋利的矛尖般猛刺；现在则形成了一面防护盾。罗斯无法击穿这面盾，他们也不敢撤下它。在这场奇异的战斗中，一种僵局存在于他们之间——罗斯的世界此前从未见识过这样的战斗。

===Original===
He watched those expressionless white faces, trying to find some reply to the deadlock. There flashed into his mind the certainty that while he lived and moved, and they lived and moved, this struggle, this unending pursuit, would continue. For some mysterious reason they wanted to have him under their control, but that was never going to happen if they all had to remain here on this strip of water-washed sand until they starved to death! Ross tried to drive that thought across to them.

"Murrrrdock!" That croaking cry borne out of the sea by the wind might almost have come from the bill of a sea bird.

"Murrrrdock!"

Ross spun around. Visibility had been drastically curtailed by the lowering clouds and the dashing spray, but he could see a round dark thing bobbing on the waves. The sub? A raft?

Sensing a movement behind him, Ross wheeled about as one of the alien figures leaped the blazing drift, heedless of the flames, and ran light-footedly toward him in what could only be an all-out attempt at capture. The man had ready a weapon like the one that had felled Foscar. Ross threw himself at his opponent in a reckless dive, falling on him with a smashing impact.

===Chinese===
他注视着那些毫无表情的白色面孔，试图找到打破僵局的办法。一个确信闪过他的脑海：只要他还在活着、在行动，他们也还在活着、在行动，这场角力，这场无尽的追逐，就会持续下去。出于某种神秘的原因，他们想把他置于控制之下，但如果他们所有人都得留在这条被海水冲刷的沙地上直到饿死，那他们永远办不到！罗斯试图把这个念头推送给他们。

"默多克——！"那声从海上被风送来的嘶哑呼唤，简直像是海鸟喙中发出的。

"默多克——！"

罗斯猛然转身。低垂的云层和飞溅的水沫极大地限制了能见度，但他能看到一个圆形的黑色物体在浪涛上起伏。是潜艇？还是筏子？

察觉身后有动静，罗斯猛地转身，只见一个外星人身影纵身跳过燃烧的浮木，全然不顾火焰，轻捷地朝他奔来——这只能是一次全力以赴的抓捕行动。那人手中握着一件武器，与击倒福斯卡（Foscar）的那件相似。罗斯不顾一切地扑向对手，以猛烈的撞击将对方扑倒。

===Original===
In Ross's grasp the alien's body was fragile, but he moved fluidly as Murdock fought to break his grip on the hand weapon and pin him to the sand. Ross was too intent upon his own part of the struggle to heed the sounds of a shot over his head and a thin, wailing cry. He slammed his opponent's hand against a stone, and the white face, inches away from his own, twisted silently with pain.

Fumbling for a better hold, Ross was sent rolling. He came down on his left hand with a force which brought tears to his eyes and stopped him just long enough for the other to regain his feet.

The blue-suited man sprinted back to the body of his fellow where it lay by the drift. He slung his unconscious comrade over the barrier with more ease than Ross would have believed possible and vaulted the barrier after him. Ross, half crouched on the sand, felt unusually light and empty. The strange tie which had drawn and held him to the strangers had been broken.

"Murdock!"

A rubber raft rode in on the waves, two men aboard it. Ross got up, pulling at the studs of his suit with his right hand. He could believe in what he saw now—the sub had not left, after all. The two men running toward him through the dusk were of his own kind.

===Chinese===
在外星人的躯体在罗斯的擒抱中显得脆弱，但那人动作流畅，罗斯拼力要掰开他握武器的手，将他按在沙地上。罗斯过于专注自己的搏斗，没有注意到头顶一声枪响和一声尖细的哀叫。他将对手的手狠狠砸向一块石头，那张距他仅几英寸的白色面孔无声地因痛苦而扭曲。

罗斯摸索着寻找更好的把位时被甩了出去。他左手着地，力道之大令他双泪直流，也让他耽搁了片刻，足够对方重新站起来。

那个穿蓝色衣服的人飞奔回同伴身旁——同伴倒在浮木边。他轻松地将昏迷的战友扛过屏障，轻松得令罗斯难以置信，然后自己也翻了过去。罗斯半蹲在沙地上，感到出奇的轻盈和空虚。那条曾将他与陌生人连结、束缚的奇异纽带已经断裂了。

"默多克！"

一只橡皮筏随浪涌来，上面载着两个人。罗斯站起身，用右手拉扯衣服上的纽扣。现在他可以相信自己看到的了——潜艇终究没有离开。暮色中朝他跑来的那两个人是他的同类。

===Original===
"Murdock!"

It did not seem at all strange that Kelgarries reached him first. Ross, caught up in this dream, appealed to the major for aid with the studs. If the strangers from the ship did trace him by the suit, they were not going to follow the sub back to the post and serve the project as they had the Reds.

"Got—to—get—this—off—" He pulled the words out one by one, tugging frantically at the stubborn studs. "They can trace this and follow us—"

Kelgarries needed no better explanation. Ripping loose the fastenings, he pulled the clinging fabric from Ross, sending him reeling with pain as he pulled the left sleeve down the younger man's arm.

The wind and spray were ice on his body as they dragged him down to the raft, bundling him aboard. He did not at all remember their arrival on board the sub. He was lying in the vibrating heart of the undersea ship when he opened his eyes to see Kelgarries regarding him intently. Ashe, a coat of bandage about his shoulder and chest, lay on a neighboring bunk. McNeil stood watching a medical corpsman lay out supplies.

"He needs a shot," the medic was saying as Ross blinked at the major.

===Chinese===
"默多克！"

凯尔加里斯少校（Kelgarries）第一个到达他身边，罗斯丝毫不觉得奇怪。沉浸在这如梦似幻的情境中，罗斯向少校求助解开纽扣。如果飞船上的那些陌生人能通过这套衣服追踪他，他们就不会跟着潜艇回到哨所（the post），像他们对付赤色分子那样利用项目。

"得——把——这——脱——掉——"他一个字一个字地往外挤，疯狂地拽着那些顽固的纽扣。"他们能追踪这个，跟着我们——"

凯尔加里斯不需要更多解释。他一把扯开扣件，将紧贴罗斯身体的织物拉了下来，左袖从年轻人手臂上褪下时的疼痛让罗斯踉跄了一下。

风和水沫像冰一样打在他身上，他们把他拖上橡皮筏，塞了上去。他完全不记得是怎么回到潜艇上的。当他睁开眼睛时，他正躺在海底船震动的船舱深处，凯尔加里斯正专注地注视着他。阿什（Ashe）肩膀和胸部裹着厚厚的绷带，躺在旁边的铺位上。麦克尼尔（McNeil）站着看一个医护兵铺开医疗用品。

"他需要打一针，"医护兵说道，罗斯朝少校眨了眨眼。

===Original===
"You left the suit—back there?" Ross demanded.

"We did. What's this about them tracing you by it? Who was tracing you?"

"Men from the space ship. That's the only way they could have trailed me down the river." He was finding it difficult to talk, and the protesting medic kept waving a needle in his direction, but somehow in bursts of half-finished sentences Ross got out his story—Foscar's death, his own escape from the chief's funeral pyre, and the weird duel of wills back on the beach. Even as he poured it out he thought how unlikely most of it must sound. Yet Kelgarries appeared to accept every word, and there was no expression of disbelief on Ashe's face.

"So that's how you got those burns," said the major slowly when Ross had finished his story. "Deliberately searing your hand in the fire to break their hold—" He crashed his fist against the wall of the tiny cabin and then, when Ross winced at the jar, he hurriedly uncurled those fingers to press Ross's shoulder with a surprisingly warm and gentle touch. "Put him to sleep," he ordered the medic. "He deserves about a month of it, I should judge. I think he has brought us a bigger slice of the future than we had hoped for…"

===Chinese===
"你把那套衣服留下了——丢在后面了？"罗斯追问。

"留下了。你说他们能用那东西追踪你？谁在追踪你？"

"飞船上来的人。他们肯定是靠那东西一路追踪我顺河而下的。"他发现说话很困难，抗议的医护兵一直在朝他挥舞针头，但罗斯还是断断续续、勉强拼凑地讲出了自己的经历——福斯卡的死、他自己从首领火葬柴堆上的逃脱、以及海滩上那场诡异的意志对决。即使在倾诉的过程中，他也觉得这一切听起来多么难以置信。然而凯尔加里斯似乎字字句句都接受了，阿什脸上也没有半点怀疑的神色。

"所以你的烧伤就是这样来的，"少校在罗斯讲完后缓缓说道。"故意把手伸进火里灼烧，来挣脱他们的控制——"他一拳砸在小船舱的墙上，然后看到罗斯因震动而瑟缩了一下，他急忙松开拳头，以出奇温暖而轻柔的触感按了按罗斯的肩膀。"让他睡一觉，"他命令医护兵。"我估计他至少该睡上一个月。我觉得他给我们带回来的关于未来的消息，比我们期望的还要重大……"

===Original===
Ross felt the prick of the needle and then nothing more. Even when he was carried ashore at the post and later when he was transported into his proper time, he did not awaken. He only approached a strange dreamy state in which he ate and drowsed, not caring for the world beyond his own bunk.

But there came a day when he did care, sitting up to demand food with a great deal of his old self-assertion. The doctor looked him over, permitting him to get out of bed and try out his legs. They were exceedingly uncooperative at first, and Ross was glad he had tried to move only from his bunk to a waiting chair.

"Visitors welcome?"

Ross looked up eagerly and then smiled, somewhat hesitatingly, at Ashe. The older man wore his arm in a sling but otherwise seemed his usual imperturbable self.

"Ashe, tell me what happened. Are we back at the main base? What about the Reds? We weren't traced by the ship people, were we?"

Ashe laughed. "Did Doc just wind you up to let you spin, Ross? Yes, this is home, sweet home. As for the rest—well, it is a long story, and we are still picking up pieces of it here and there."

===Chinese===
罗斯感觉到针扎了一下，然后便什么都不知道了。即使在哨所靠岸后被抬上岸，后来被运回属于他的时代时，他也没有醒来。他只是进入了一种奇异的半梦半醒状态，在那里吃饭、打盹，对铺位以外的世界毫不在意。

但有一天他在意了——他坐起来要吃的，带着往日那股不驯的劲头。医生给他做了检查，允许他下床试试腿脚。起初双腿极不配合，罗斯庆幸自己只试着从铺位挪到旁边等候的椅子。

"欢迎访客吗？"

罗斯急切地抬起头，然后朝阿什微笑了一下，笑容里带着几分迟疑。年长者手臂吊在绷带里，但除此之外似乎还是往日那个沉着自若的阿什。

"阿什，告诉我发生了什么。我们回到主基地了吗？赤色分子呢？飞船人没有追踪我们吧？"

阿什笑了。"是医生刚把你上好发条让你转起来的吗，罗斯？对，这里是家，甜蜜的家。至于其他的——嗯，说来话长，我们还在各处拼凑碎片。"

===Original===
Ross pointed to the bunk in invitation. "Can you tell me what is known?" He was still somewhat at a loss, his old secret awe of Ashe tempering his outward show of eagerness. Ross still feared one of those snubs the other so well knew how to deliver to the bumptious. But Ashe did come in and sit down, none of his old formality now in evidence.

"You have been a surprise package, Murdock." His observation had some of the ring of the old Ashe, but there was no withdrawal behind the words. "Rather a busy lad, weren't you, after you were bumped off into that river?"

Ross's reply was a grimace. "You heard all about that!" He had no time for his own adventures, already receding into a past which made them both dim and unimportant. "What happened to you—and to the project—and—"

"One thing at a time, and don't rush your fences." Ashe was surveying him with an odd intentness which Ross could not understand. He continued to explain in his "instructor" voice. "We made it down the river—how, don't ask me. That was something of a 'project' in itself," he laughed. "The raft came apart piece by piece, and we waded most of the last couple of miles, I think. I'm none too clear on the details; you'll have to get those out of McNeil, who was still among those present then. Other than that, we cannot compete with your adventures. We built a signal fire and sat by it toasting our shins for a few days, until the sub came to collect us—"

"And took you off." Ross experienced a fleeting return of that hollow feeling he had known on the shore when the still-warm coals of the signal fire had told him the story of his too-late arrival.

===Chinese===
罗斯指了指铺位，示意阿什坐下。"能告诉我目前知道的情况吗？"他仍然有些茫然，对阿什旧日那种隐秘的敬畏抑制着他外露的急切。罗斯仍然害怕阿什那种擅长打消冒失鬼气焰的冷淡。但阿什确实走进来坐下了，全然没有了从前那种拘谨。

"你可真是个惊喜包啊，默多克。"他的评价带着几分旧日阿什的口吻，但话语后面没有丝毫疏离。"被扔进河里之后，你可真是忙得不轻，对吧？"

罗斯苦着脸回答。"你全听说了！"他没时间细说自己的冒险——它们已经退入过去，变得模糊而不重要了。"你后来怎么样——项目呢——还有——"

"一件一件来，别急着翻篱笆。"阿什带着一种罗斯无法理解的奇异专注打量着他。他继续用"教官"的口吻解释道。"我们顺河而下了——怎么做到的，别问我。那本身就算一个'项目'了，"他笑了。"筏子一块一块散了架，最后几英里我们大部分是趟过去的，我想。细节我也不太清楚；你得去问麦克尼尔，他当时还在场。除此之外，我们的经历没法跟你比。我们点了一堆求救信号火，坐在旁边烤了几天小腿，直到潜艇来接我们——"

"然后把你们带走了。"罗斯短暂地重温了那种空虚感——在岸上时，余温尚存的信号灰烬曾向他讲述了一个他到得太迟的故事。

===Original===
"And took us off. But Kelgarries agreed to spin out our waiting period for another twenty-four hours, in case you did manage to survive that toss you took into the river. Then we sighted your spectacular display of fireworks on the beach, and the rest was easy."

"The ship people didn't trace us back to post?"

"Not that we know of. Anyway, we've closed down the post on that time level. You might be interested in a very peculiar tale our modern agents have picked up, floating over and under the iron curtain. A blast went off in the Baltic region of this time, wiping some installation clean off the map. The Reds have kept quiet as to the nature of the explosion and the exact place where it occurred."

"The aliens followed *them* all the way up to this time!"—Ross half rose from the chair—"But why? And why did they trail me?"

"That we can only guess. But I don't believe that they were moved by any private vengeance for the looting of their derelict. There is some more imperative reason why they don't want us to find or use anything from one of their cargoes—"

"But they were in power thousands of years ago. Maybe they and their worlds are gone now. Why should things we do today matter to them?"

"Well, it does matter, and in some very important way. And we have to learn that reason."

===Chinese===
"然后把我们接走了。但凯尔加里斯同意把等待时间再延长二十四小时，万一你确实活下来了呢——你被扔进河里那次。然后我们看到了你在海滩上那场壮观的焰火表演，剩下的就简单了。"

"飞船人没有追踪我们回到哨所？"

"据我们所知没有。不管怎样，我们已经关闭了那个时间层上的哨所。你可能会对我们现代特工在铁幕（iron curtain）上下搜集到的一个非常离奇的故事感兴趣。这个时代波罗的海（Baltic）地区发生了一次爆炸，把某个设施从地图上彻底抹去了。赤色分子对爆炸的性质和确切地点一直讳莫如深。"

"外星人一路追踪*他们*追到了这个时代！"——罗斯从椅子上半站起来——"可是为什么？他们为什么要追踪我？"

"这只能猜测。但我不相信他们是出于对掠夺他们废弃飞船（derelict）的私人报复。一定有更紧迫的原因，使他们不想让我们找到或使用他们货物中的任何东西——"

"可他们是几千年前掌权的。也许他们和他们的世界现在已经消失了。我们今天做的事对他们有什么关系？"

"嗯，确实有关系，而且关系重大。我们必须弄清这个原因。"

===Original===
"How?" Ross looked down at his left hand, encased in a mitten of bandage under which he very gingerly tried to stretch a finger. Maybe he should have been eager to welcome another meeting with the ship people, but if he were truly honest, he had to admit that he did not. He glanced up, sure that Ashe had read all that hesitation and scorned him for it. But there was no sign that his discomfiture had been noticed.

"By doing some looting of our own," Ashe answered. "Those tapes we brought back are going to be a big help. More than one derelict was located. We were right in our surmise that the Reds first discovered the remains of one in Siberia, but it was in no condition to be explored. They already had the basic idea of the time traveler, so they applied it to the hunting down of other ships, with several way stops to throw people like us off the scent. So they found an intact ship, and also several others. At least three are on *this* side of the Atlantic where they couldn't get at them very well. Those we can deal with now—"

"Won't the aliens be waiting for us to try that?"

"As far as we can discover they don't know where any of these ships crashed. Either there were no survivors, or passengers and crew took off in lifeboats while they were still in space. They might never have known of the Reds' activities if you hadn't triggered that communicator on the derelict."

Ross was reduced to a small boy who badly needed an alibi for some piece of juvenile mischief. "I didn't mean to." That excuse sounded so feeble that he was surprised into a laugh, only to see Ashe grinning back at him.

===Chinese===
"怎么弄清？"罗斯低头看着自己的左手，裹在一只绷带手套里，他小心翼翼地试着伸展手指。也许他应该热切期待再次与飞船人相遇，但如果他真正诚实的话，他不得不承认他并不想。他抬眼看了看，确信阿什已经看出了他的犹豫并因此看不起他。但没有任何迹象表明他的窘态被人注意到了。

"通过我们自己的掠夺，"阿什回答。"我们带回来的那些记忆磁带（tapes）会大有帮助。不止一艘废弃飞船的位置已经确认。我们的推测是正确的——赤色分子最初在西伯利亚（Siberia）发现了一艘残骸，但那艘已经无法探索。他们已经有了时间旅行器的基本概念，于是将它应用于搜寻其他飞船，中途设了好几个中转站来迷惑像我们这样的人。所以他们找到了一艘完好的飞船，另外还有好几艘。至少三艘在大西洋（Atlantic）的*这一侧*，他们不太容易够到。那些我们现在可以对付——"

"外星人不会等着我们去尝试吗？"

"据我们所知，他们不知道这些飞船坠落在哪里。要么没有幸存者，要么乘客和船员在太空中就乘救生艇离开了。如果你没有触发废弃飞船上的那台通讯器，他们可能永远不会知道赤色分子的活动。"

罗斯变成了一个急需不在场证明的淘气小男孩——为了某种少年式的恶作剧。"我不是故意的。"这个借口听起来如此无力，他自己都忍不住笑了，却看到阿什也朝他咧嘴笑了。

===Original===
"Seeing as how your action also put a very effective spike in the opposition's wheel, you are freely forgiven. Anyway, you have also provided us with a pretty good idea of what we may be up against with the aliens, and we'll be prepared for that next time."

"Then there will be a next time?"

"We are calling in all time agents, concentrating our forces in the right period. Yes, there will be a next time. We have to learn just what they are trying so hard to protect."

"What do you think it is?"

"Space!" Ashe spoke the word softly as if he relished the promise it held.

"Space?"

"That ship you explored was a derelict from a galactic fleet, but it was a ship and it used the principle of space flight. Do you understand now? In these lost ships lies the secret which will make us free of all the stars! We must claim it."

"Can we—?"

"Can *we*?" Ashe was laughing at Ross again with his eyes, though his face remained sober. "Then *you* still want to be counted in on this game?"

===Chinese===
"鉴于你的行动同时也给了对手一个漂亮的绊马索，你被无条件赦免了。总之，你还为我们提供了关于外星人底牌的相当清晰的情报，下次我们会做好准备。"

"还会有下次吗？"

"我们正在召回所有时间特工，将力量集中在正确的时间层上。是的，还会有下次。我们必须弄清楚他们在拼命保护的到底是什么。"

"你觉得是什么？"

"太空！"阿什轻声说出这个词，仿佛在品味它所蕴含的承诺。

"太空？"

"你探索的那艘船是一个银河舰队的废弃飞船，但它就是一艘船，运用了太空飞行的原理。你现在明白了吗？在这些失落的飞船中，隐藏着让我们摆脱所有星球束缚的秘密！我们必须得到它。"

"我们能吗——"

"*我们*能吗？"阿什又用眼睛笑罗斯了，尽管脸上依然一本正经。"那么你还想加入这场游戏？"

===Original===
Ross looked down again at his bandaged hand and remembered swiftly so many things—the coast of Britain on a misty morning, the excitement of prowling the alien ship, the fight with Ennar, even the long nightmare of his flight down the river, and lastly, the exultation he had tasted when he had faced the alien and had locked wills—to hold steady. He knew that he could not, would not, give up what he had found here in the service of the project as long as it was in his power to cling to it.

"Yes." It was a very simple answer, but when his eyes met Ashe's, Ross knew that it would serve better than any solemn oath.

===Chinese===
罗斯再次低头看着自己裹着绷带的手，飞快地想起了那么多事——薄雾笼罩的清晨中不列颠（Britain）的海岸，潜入外星飞船时的兴奋，与恩纳尔（Ennar）的搏斗，甚至在河上逃亡的漫长噩梦，以及最后，面对外星人、意志交锋时他所品尝到的那种狂喜——要撑住。他知道，只要自己还有能力坚持，他就不会、也绝不会放弃在项目服务中所发现的这一切。

"是的。"这是一个极其简单的回答，但当他的目光与阿什相遇时，罗斯知道，这比任何庄严的誓言都更管用。
